> **Last refreshed:** 2026-10-19. If a section feels behind the code, trust
> the code and update this file.

## Project Overview

GameShuffle (gameshuffle.co) is a game night companion platform for streamers and their viewers, built around Mario Kart 8 Deluxe and Mario Kart World. It provides:

- **Randomizers** for casual + competitive game nights
- **Live competitive lounge scoring** with normalized per-player placements
- **Tournaments** with build restrictions, picks/bans, and live participant updates
- **Twitch streamer integration** — chat bot, channel-point rewards, OBS overlay, public lobby viewer, per-streamer modules
- **Discord adapter** — cross-platform announcements + interactions
- **Token economy** — closed-loop currency with prediction markets, awards, bounties, leaderboards, and platform-admin policy levers
- **Hub** at `/hub` for streamers to configure sessions, modules, and integrations
- **TCG Companion** — TCG-agnostic digital accessory kit (Pokémon Mode shipped first)
- **Platform admin** at `/account` and `/staff` for staff/admin operational tooling

Primary customer is the streamer. Viewers participate via chat + tactile interactions on `/live/[streamer-slug]`.

## Tech Stack

- **Framework**: Next.js 16 (App Router, TypeScript, React 19)
- **UI Library**: CascadeDS (`@empac/cascadeds`) — installed from Git (`git+https://github.com/blorentzen/cascadeds.git`)
- **Database**: Supabase (PostgreSQL + Realtime + Auth)
- **Hosting**: Vercel
- **Billing**: Stripe (Checkout + Customer Portal + webhook handler)
- **Analytics**: Plausible (cookieless) + Google Analytics (with cookie consent, GPC honored)
- **Error tracking**: Sentry (server + client + global-error)
- **Bot Protection**: Cloudflare Turnstile
- **OAuth Providers**: Discord, Twitch (via Supabase Auth)
- **Email**: MailerSend SMTP (transactional from `noreply@gameshuffle.co`, billing templates, policy-update blasts)

**Important**: All UI uses CDS exclusively. Do not use Tailwind or other utility frameworks.

### CDS-first rule (no bespoke UI)

Always build UI from CDS components. Before hand-rolling anything (tabs, selects,
checkboxes, chips, cards, stat tiles, modals, breadcrumbs, accordions,
segmented/mode switches, steppers…), check whether CDS already provides it and
use it. This keeps the UX fast and consistent and avoids reinventing a11y +
keyboard behavior.

- **Prefer the CDS component, styled via its own props** (`variant`, `size`,
  `fullWidth`, etc.) plus small CSS overrides on its class.
- **Need an enhancement CDS doesn't expose?** Wrap the CDS component and layer
  the behavior on top (e.g. `ProfileTabs` is a thin wrapper over CDS `Tabs` that
  adds `?tab=` URL sync). Modify/extend CDS — do not rebuild the widget.
- **A CDS component blows out the layout?** Restyle it to fit; it is still the
  right primitive. Don't replace it with a custom element to dodge styling work.
- **CDS genuinely has no solve?** Flag it explicitly (to the user / in review) so
  a CDS-compatible solution can be built and contributed back to CascadeDS,
  rather than shipping a one-off custom component. A from-scratch component is a
  last resort and should be called out, not slipped in.

CDS lives in `node_modules/@empac/cascadeds`; its component prop types are the
source of truth (`dist/types/app/components/empac/*.d.ts`). Check them before
assuming a prop exists.

## Build & Dev

```bash
npm install
npm run dev     # localhost:3000
npm run build   # production build
npm run lint    # ESLint
```

## Key Architecture

### Randomizers
- `RandomizerClient` — shared component for all game randomizers
- Per-game config at `src/app/randomizers/[slug]/config.ts` — controls filters, slot visibility, race counts, knockout support
- Game data as static JSON imports in `src/data/`
- **MK8DX**: 4-part combos (character, vehicle, wheels, glider), tour-only filter, drift filter, 48 races max
- **MKWorld**: 2-part combos (character, vehicle only), vehicle type filter (Kart/Bike/ATV), knockout rallies, overworld map icons for tracks, race counts [4,6,8,12,16,32]
- `PlayerCard` — conditional slot rendering via `hasWheels`/`hasGlider` props
- `TrackList` — supports `course.icon` (overworld icons), optional cup icons via `showCupIcons`, course names displayed
- `RaceSelector` — supports custom `counts` array and `label` per game
- Hooks: `useKartRandomizer`, `useTrackRandomizer` with `hydrate()` for config loading
- CDS Tabs for Kart/Race/Item sections
- Onboarding prompt on first visit
- Typed saved configs: `kart-build`, `item-set`, `game-night-setup`
- **No copy-as-text buttons on randomizers** (Britton, 2026-10-10: nobody pastes them; Copy match / teams / heroes / run were removed). Share by link only where the link rebuilds the result (Fire Red/Leaf Green's run link).
- **Card rules (every randomizer, 2026-10-06):** a card's own buttons change only that card; rolling everyone is only the intro card's Randomize button. Card buttons are `CardActions` (`src/components/randomizer/CardActions.tsx`: icon buttons on CDS `IconButton` + `Tooltip`, labels like "New rider and machine for Sam" / "Remove Sam"). Per-seat picks are sparse lists (`withSeat`/`otherSeats`/`seatLabel` in `src/lib/randomizers/seats.ts`) so a card refreshed before anyone rolled, or a seat added after a roll, fills on its own. Unrolled slots show each game's own "random" look (`RandomTile`: kirby/smash/splatoon/party via KartSlot `empty`); only Mario Kart keeps the item box. The intro card (`.kart-intro`) reads left at every width, even inside a centred page column; cards may centre.
- **Player cards grow in place (design round 3, 2026-10-20, picks https://claude.ai/artifact/FkXQ839JCDxGuhUBqnM1XZ):** every randomizer's card is `SeatCard` (exported from `PlayerCard.tsx`; `PlayerCard` is the Mario Kart one): `rolled`, `animate`, `name`, `actions`, `tile` (the main KartSlot), children (that game's details). Before the first roll it's a short row (`player-card--seat`: the game's random tile at 5.6rem, the name field, its buttons). On the first roll the tile stays in the left column and grows into the picture (`--pc-tile` = clamp(8.4rem, 40% - 5rem, 11.2rem), 0.5s) while the details unfold beside the name field (`grid-template-rows` 0fr to 1fr, staggered fade and 8px rise). Transitions sit only on `.player-card--rolled`: a card that mounts rolled doesn't animate, going back to a seat is one step, re-rolls only spin the reel; reduced motion or the Rolling animation switch off (`player-card--still`) changes in one step. KartSlot portals the tile's caption into the heading (`SlotCaptionContext`) so it rolls with the reel, and `player-card--spinning` holds back details that name the pick until it lands (`.player-card__parts` spin along; `--row` for one part). Per game: Mario Kart 8 Deluxe = character + vehicle/wheels/glider in a row + Save Build; World = character + vehicle; 64 = character; Smash = fighter + "Costume N · Series"; Mario Party = character (+ Jamboree Buddy line, CPU seats labelled); Splatoon = weapon + class, sub, special; Kirby = rider + machine + type; GoldenEye/Perfect Dark = character + team badge or handicap. The card is flex so the tile's % width resolves against it; never put `container-type` or a transform on the card or its ancestors (Save Build's window is `position: fixed` inside). Named KartSlot images take `alt=""`. HeroRoulette and Stadium keep the plain `.player-card`. The tab bar joins the intro card when the intro is the tab panel's first thing (`.randomizer-controls:has(+ * > .kart-intro:first-child …)` in randomizer.css: no gap, square corners where they meet).

### Splatoon 3 randomizer (launched 2026-10-06, marked New)
- `/randomizers/splatoon-3` (`SplatoonRandomizer`; data `src/data/splatoon/splatoon3.ts`, rolls `src/lib/splatoon/roll.ts`, types `src/lib/splatoon/types.ts`). Weapon kits (main + sub + special) for 1-8 players with class filters, replicas toggle (13 replicas share another kit; off by default) and no-repeats; one battle or a set of 3/5 (mode + stage, no stage repeated); Salmon Run stage; Alpha/Bravo teams. Saved setups: `splatoon-setup` (`describeSplatoonSetup` in `src/data/splatoon/index.ts`).
- Data parsed from Inkipedia 2026-09-29 (v11.2.0): 173 kits, 25 stages, 7 Salmon Run stages. Art (Inkipedia, 2026-10-06) is served from `public/images/splatoon-3/` as webp: `weapons/<artName>.webp` and `stages/<artName>.webp` (battle + Salmon Run, `splatStageArt`), drawn on the battle and Salmon Run cards.
- Flag `SPLATOON_PUBLIC` in `src/lib/games-visibility.ts` (on). `randomizerPublic(slug)` there filters randomizer lists for any hidden game.

### Kirby Air Riders randomizer (launched 2026-10-06, marked New)
- `/randomizers/kirby-air-riders` (`KirbyRandomizer`; data `src/data/kirby/air-riders.ts`, rolls `src/lib/kirby/roll.ts`). Rider + machine for 1-8 players (riders different, machines can repeat), machine-type filters (Legendary off by default: not allowed in every mode; Flight Warp Star left out: Free Run only), "new save" starters-only mode; Air Ride (18) or Top Ride (9) course; City Trial Stadium by kind. Saved setups: `kirby-setup` (`describeKirbySetup` in `src/data/kirby/index.ts`).
- Data researched 2026-09-29 (WiKirby, v1.3.3; no update has added content). Flag `KIRBY_PUBLIC` (on). Art (WiKirby, 2026-10-06) in `public/images/kirby-air-riders/{riders,machines,courses,top-ride}/<slug>.webp`; Noir Dedede and the Legendary machines use the Switch Online renders; Checker Knights has no course card (plain card). Course results show their card art.
### Hero roster upkeep (Overwatch, Marvel Rivals)
- **Art:** official portraits at `public/images/{overwatch,marvel-rivals}/heroes/<slug>.webp` (256px; `heroArt(game, name)` / `heroSlug` in `src/lib/heroes/art.ts`; `artReady: true` on each game). Overwatch = Blizzard's square gallery portraits; Marvel Rivals = the in-game square portraits (wiki file host), with the marvelrivals.com render cropped as a fallback. Sources in `scripts/data/hero-art.json`; `npx tsx scripts/pull-hero-art.ts [--force] [--only <game>]` pulls them (skips existing files). `HeroTile` spins on the role icon, then lands on the portrait; chat rolls use a `portrait` slot.
- Data in `src/data/heroes/{overwatch,marvel-rivals}.ts` (`checkedOn`, per-hero `released`; a future `released` hides the hero until that day). **Monthly check:** `/api/cron/hero-rosters` (1st, 15:00 UTC) runs `checkHeroRosters()` (`src/lib/heroes/rosterCheck.ts`: parses Blizzard's hero gallery and the marvelrivals.com hero list, compares by `rosterKey`), and when a hero is new, gone or the page can't be read, emails support@ and sends staff a `system` notification. Platform ▸ Health has a "Check now" card (`HeroRosterCard`, `/api/admin/hero-rosters`). Nothing updates itself: edit the data file, add the art, bump `checkedOn`.

### New randomizers with "Image coming soon" (launched 2026-10-04 as "Beta", relabelled "New" 2026-10-06)
- **Pokémon Stadium** `/randomizers/pokemon-stadium` (`StadiumRandomizer`, `src/lib/pokemon/stadium.ts`, data `src/data/pokemon/stadium.json`): 6 rentals per player per cup, optional pick of 3, Round 2 rentals; saved setups `stadium-setup`.
- **GoldenEye 007** `/randomizers/goldeneye-007` (`GoldenEyeRandomizer`, `src/lib/goldeneye/roll.ts`, data `src/data/goldeneye/multiplayer.ts`): scenario first (teams, Golden Gun, Last Alive), then a map that fits the player count, weapon set, length, characters; No Oddjob on by default.
- **Fire Red/Leaf Green run challenge** `/randomizers/pokemon-firered-leafgreen` (`FrlgRunChallenge`, `src/lib/pokemon/frlgRun.ts`, data `src/data/pokemon/frlg-run.json`): seeded run (URL `?seed=&v=&c=&tw=&nf=` rebuilds it), forced starter, catches per badge from what's reachable before that gym on that version and under the level cap, gym level cap = leader's ace, team size + twist; checklist in localStorage per run (`useSyncExternalStore`). Never change the rng call order in `buildRun` or old links change.
- **Mario Party 1, 2, 3** `/randomizers/mario-party`, `-2`, `-3`: data files `src/data/party/mario-party-{1,2,3}.ts` (generated from `specs/research/2026-10-04-randomizers/mario-party-n64.json`, N64 spellings) on the shared `PartyRandomizer`, registered in `PARTY_GAMES` (so they reach nights, tournaments and collections too). `artReady: false` → glyph hero (`PartyHero.image` optional) and board tiles; characters reuse the Superstars art via absolute `img` URLs (`characterArt()` in `src/lib/party/types.ts`, used by the randomizer, reference roster and Draft Night); `hero.isNew` adds the New eyebrow + banner. Ruleset `turnLabels` (Lite/Standard/Full Play); minigame `stickSpin` (MP1's three stick-spinners) + a skip switch. MP3 Duel Mode not rolled yet (its boards only fit that ruleset). Flag `N64_PARTY_PUBLIC`.
- **Showcase TCG cards on Pokémon randomizers:** `SHOWCASE_CARDS` (`src/data/pokemon/showcase-cards.ts`, dex → Scrydex card id) is generated by `scripts/populate-pokemon-showcase.ts` (operator-run, curated; local `tcg_cards` first at 0 credits, then the best English Special Illustration / Illustration Rare, else another English printing; `--local` free pass, `--run` spends credits, `--warm` loads the picks into another database, e.g. production). Pages read them server-side with `getShowcaseArt()` (`src/lib/pokemon/showcase.ts`, `revalidate` daily) and `TypeCard` fills the tile with the card's artwork behind the text (CSS crop of the Scrydex image: the band between name bar and attack text on full-art rarities, the illustration window on normal cards; `ART_REGION` in TypeCard.tsx); `TcgAttribution` replaces `PokemonDisclaimer` once any art shows. Empty team slots use our SVG `PokeBall` (`src/components/pokemon/PokeBall.tsx`, also the companion coin).
- **Setup pattern for the newer randomizers:** the intro card's right column shows only the choice made every time (a CDS `Select`: Stadium's cup grouped by game, FRLG's version) plus `RandomizerOptions` (`src/components/randomizer/RandomizerOptions.tsx`): an "Options" line saying what's on and a "Change options" button opening a CDS Drawer (portaled to body) with the FilterGroups and explainer text. GoldenEye uses Add / Remove Player (2-4).
- Pokémon pages use `TypeCard` (no sprites, ever). Character slots without art pass `fallback={IMAGE_COMING_SOON}` to `KartSlot`; `/apps` cards use `media={<ImageComingSoon />}` + `isNew`. Flags `STADIUM_PUBLIC` / `GOLDENEYE_PUBLIC` / `FRLG_PUBLIC` in `src/lib/games-visibility.ts`.
- **Art checklist for every randomizer:** https://claude.ai/artifact/WS4gAX8n2rZ9VNWgdd53Pb (reference links verified against each wiki, CDN paths, saved ticks).

### Pokémon Stadium rental randomizer (hidden until reviewed)
- `/randomizers/pokemon-stadium` (`StadiumRandomizer`; rules `src/lib/pokemon/stadium.ts`; data `src/data/pokemon/stadium.json`, trimmed from `specs/research/2026-10-04-randomizers/` (Serebii cross-checked with Bulbapedia via Wayback; 30 disputed movesets use the in-game-possible set and are flagged in the research files)). Pick Stadium or Stadium 2 and a cup (Pika/Petit/Poké/Prime; Little/Poké/Prime); 1 to 4 players each get 6 different rentals, no repeats across players by default, optional "Pick my 3 too", Round 2 rentals (Mew; Celebi, Surfing Pikachu) behind a toggle. Every team and pick of 3 is cup-legal because rentals sit at each cup's minimum level. Saved setups (`stadium-setup`, `describeStadiumSetup`). Hidden behind `STADIUM_PUBLIC`.
- **Pokémon imagery rule (decided 2026-10-04, revised same day):** every Pokémon randomizer uses `TypeCard` (`src/components/pokemon/TypeCard.tsx`): type color, our own Tabler icon per type, dex number, name, types, level, moves, plus `PokemonDisclaimer`. No sprites, official art, silhouettes or official type icons. Britton approved two additions: empty team slots show the companion's CSS Poké Ball (`CoinFace`) with ??? like Mario Kart's item box, and showcase TCG card artwork from Scrydex once populated (cropped to the art only, behind our text, with TcgAttribution).
- Next in this wave (plan https://claude.ai/artifact/R4rv9YGmyfPdE4njc4qG4E): Mario Party 1-3 into the party randomizer, GoldenEye 007, then the Pokémon run challenge (Fire Red/Leaf Green first).

### Random Pokémon generator (built 2026-10-20, search pages round, picks all as recommended)
- `/randomizers/pokemon` (`PokemonGenerator`, rules `src/lib/pokemon/generator.ts`, tests `npx tsx scripts/test-pokemon-generator.ts`): 1, 3 or 6 different Pokémon from all 1,025 (the Daily Dex facts, `DEX_ROWS`), narrowed by generation and type, with fully evolved only, no Legendaries or Mythicals and no repeated types in the options drawer. Each card's refresh changes only that card; Copy link (`?p=dex-dex&n=&gen=&type=&fe=&nl=&nr=`, `linkParams`/`readLink`) rebuilds the same roll. Type cards with showcase TCG art (the Pokémon imagery rule). The page: the tool, "More Pokémon on GameShuffle" (Daily Dex, the Pokémon quiz, the Fire Red/Leaf Green run), `PlansPanel` with its own copy (Pro = chat drafts your team with `!draft start pokemon`, Circuit = Pokémon TCG Swiss), then the landing (`RANDOMIZER_LANDINGS.pokemon`). First in the catalog's Pokémon group. Events `Pokemon Rolled` {count, gen, type, slot} and `Pokemon Link Copied` (Plausible goals needed).
- **Search pages round (picks 2026-10-20, all 15 as recommended):** Pokémon generator, then the bracket tools (below), then Name every Pokémon, then the Twitch chat games guide. Britton's note: nothing here may undercut a path to GS Pro or GS Circuit revenue.

### Name every Pokémon (built 2026-10-20, search pages round)
- `/name-every-pokemon` (`NameEveryPokemon`, rules `src/lib/pokemon/nameQuiz.ts`, styles `src/styles/name-quiz.css`, tests `npx tsx scripts/test-name-quiz.ts`, ~2,000 checks; headless play-through `scripts/.tmp/nq-play.cjs <dir> [width]`). All 1,025 or one generation; clock 15 minutes for all, 8 for Gen 1 and the same pace for the others (`standardMinutes`), double, or no limit. A name counts the moment it's spelled right (`quizMatcher().exact`: case, accents, punctuation ignored; "nidoran" names whichever Nidoran is missing; mr mime, type null, ho oh…); Enter also takes a close spelling that can only mean one Pokémon (`loose`, the shared `nameMatcher` with `words: false`). A name that's the start of a missing one (Mew/Mewtwo, Porygon, Klink) waits 900ms (`waitsForMore`). Notes: already named, not in this set, unknown. The board lists every dex number by generation, filling in; at the end "Show the ones I missed". Best per set and clock and the game in progress live in localStorage `gs-name-every-pokemon`. Names only, never art. Linked from the generator page, the Daily Dex's How it works, the Pokémon quiz page, /tools and the free tools list. Events `Name Quiz Started` / `Name Quiz Finished` (Plausible goals needed).
- **Share card kind `names`** (`NamesPart` in `src/lib/share/types.ts`, `namesPart` in `store.ts`, `NamesBlock` in `render.tsx`, the result page says "Try it yourself" and isn't tied to a day): the score is the player's own word (a browser quiz), checked for sense (real Pokémon in the set, each once, time inside the clock), with no tokens or leaderboard place; one card per set and clock a day (`ref`). Needs `supabase/share-cards-m2.sql` (adds `names` to the kind check and keeps `lounge`; on dev + prod); without it, Share sends the text and link without a picture.

### Twitch chat games guide (built 2026-10-20, search pages round)
- `/twitch-chat-games` (copy in `src/data/chat-games.ts`, styles `src/styles/chat-games.css`): only games GameShuffle runs, each with what chat does, the command that starts it, what viewers type and what it suits (commands are the real ones from `src/lib/twitch/commands/guide.ts`; keep them in step), a screenshot of its overlay piece where one is filmed (`shot`, files in `public/images/guides/chat-games/`, from the hidden `/social/overlays?piece=` stage), a GS Pro close (`AuthAwareCTA`, `trackFrom: chat-games-guide`) and a FAQ. Article + ItemList + FAQPage + BreadcrumbList JSON-LD. In the Stream menu and the sitemap. Its own page because `/guides` is still hidden (`GUIDES_PUBLIC`).
- **Screenshots:** `/social/overlays` (`OverlayShowcase`, `src/styles/overlay-showcase.css`) draws the real overlay components with typed sample data; the battle, chaos and picks-and-bans banners copy OverlayClient's inline markup (keep in step). `scripts/.tmp/overlay-crops.cjs <dir> <piece…>` shoots each at 2x, cropped to its visible parts between 4:3 and 16:9; the guide's files and their sizes (`shotSize`) come from that. Bounties have no overlay piece, so no picture.
- **Challenge wording (same day):** the overlay banner and the live page's Events tab printed a challenge as its raw kind and values ("Placement: 3"). `LiveChallenge.text` now carries the event's own line (the channel's wording first, from `gs_event_overrides`) through `challengeTemplates` + `fillChallengeLine` in `src/lib/economy/events/challenges.ts`, shared with the control room (which now respects channel wording too); the raw label is only the fallback.
- **Middleware fix (same day):** `PROTECTED_ROUTES` in `src/middleware.ts` matched raw prefixes, so `/twitch-chat-games` was sent to sign in; it now matches whole path segments (`underProtected`), like `isAppRoute`.

### Free bracket tools (built 2026-10-20, search pages round)
- Three URLs, one tool (`BracketTool page="elim|roundrobin|swiss"`, `src/components/brackets/`; page shell `BracketToolPage` + `bracketToolMetadata`; copy in `src/data/bracket-tools.ts`; styles `src/styles/brackets.css`): `/bracket-generator` (single or double elimination; targets "bracket maker" 14,800 / "tournament bracket generator" 12,100 / "bracket generator" 8,100), `/round-robin-generator`, `/swiss-pairing-generator`. Up to 64 players (`MAX_PLAYERS`), no account, kept in this browser (localStorage `gs-bracket-<page>`), Copy link (`?b=` base64url JSON: names, title, options, results as one character per match, `encodeLink`/`decodeLink` in `src/lib/brackets/tool.ts`; opening a link clears it from the address bar and the browser keeps it), Print (only the board, landscape, scaled to fit with `--bt-print-zoom`). Tests `npx tsx scripts/test-bracket-tools.ts` (~2,000 checks: every size, links, pick changes, schedules, Swiss runs).
- **Engines, unchanged:** elimination on `src/lib/tournaments/bracket.ts` via `src/lib/brackets/elim.ts` (players are list indexes; double elimination pads to a power of two with bye seats that always lose, so any count from 4 works; results are picks by match id and the bracket is rebuilt from scratch on every change, so changing an early winner drops later picks that no longer fit, `applyPicks`). Swiss on `src/lib/tournaments/swiss.ts` via `src/lib/brackets/swissTool.ts` (organizer-set results, results only in the round in play, Pair again before any result, drops between rounds, top cut from standings with picks by player; the link carries every table since pairings are random). Round robin is new and pure: `src/lib/brackets/roundRobin.ts` (circle method, one or two legs, odd counts sit out once a leg; win 3 / tie 1, head-to-head when exactly two are level, then wins, then seed).
- **The bracket drawing** is `BracketTree` (a tree with CSS connector lines: each round's matches share the column height equally; losers bracket rounds with the same count join straight across; a Champion box after a single elimination final). The tournament pages keep their laddered `BracketView`. Pick rows are buttons with aria-pressed; round robin and Swiss results use `MatchPick` (CDS Buttons). Bracket drawings have no CascadeDS counterpart.
- **Run it live** (the paid path stays the live tournament): saves a handoff (`gs-bracket-handoff`, 24 hours, `readHandoff`) and opens `/tournament/create?from=bracket` (signed out: `/login?redirect=…`). The create form fills the format and name (a round robin becomes Swiss with `roundRobinAsSwiss(n)` rounds, no top cut), shows a "From the bracket tool" Alert, and after creating calls `POST /api/tournament/[id]/import-guests` (organizers and co-organizers, draft or open only, names already there skipped, seats stop at the event's own max and, once Circuit billing is on, the plan's field size via `resolveTournamentCircuit`; returns added / left / cap so the toast can point at GS Circuit), then opens the manage page's Players tab. Dev check `scripts/.tmp/bracket-handoff-e2e.ts` (7, novastreams + modmarco jars from `make-cookie.ts`).
- Found from: the /tools hub (three tiles), Compete ▸ Run one, the free tools list, the sitemap. Events `Bracket Made` {format, players}, `Bracket Link Copied`, `Bracket Printed`, `Bracket Run Live` (Plausible goals needed). Share images from `render-page-og.cjs`.

### Competitive / Live Scoring
- Normalized data model: `lounge_sessions`, `lounge_players`, `lounge_races`, `lounge_placements`
- Each player writes only their own placement row (no race conditions)
- Supabase Realtime subscriptions on all tables
- Session phases: waiting → character_select → lobby → in_progress → complete
- Team modes: FFA, 2v2, 3v3, 4v4, 6v6
- Character variant data in `src/data/mk8dx-variants.ts`

### MMR card (built 2026-10-20, three picks rounds, all as recommended)
- Picks: scope https://claude.ai/artifact/NrE1Ldcx2erXZdp31jQ99a, looks https://claude.ai/artifact/4U19hr8EqkAtQfVXsMw1XJ, directions + softer backgrounds https://claude.ai/artifact/YNyHRgyENSeVy9CnpeDoey. A Mario Kart lounge player's MMR on stream, live, built from GameShuffle's own parts (Britton: "uniquely GS, not the sample", i.e. not lounge-card). Free with an account; looks past the basics are GS Pro.
- **Data:** MKCentral's public lounge API (no CORS, so always server-side) in `src/lib/mmr/mkcentral.ts`: `loungePlayer({ id | name }, game)` (`/api/player/details`, the whole season's `mmrChanges`, cached 90s per instance; throws `LoungeUnavailable`) and `divisionSteps(game)` (division starts from `/api/player/stats` `ranks`, cached 6h; `FALLBACK_RANKS` in rules.ts are the 2026-10-20 values). Games `mkworld12p`, `mkworld24p`, `mk8dx` (MK8DX divisions are numbered: Platinum 1/2). MMR moves once per verified event (reason `Table`); `Placement` is the start; penalties and bonuses move the number quietly.
- **Rules** `src/lib/mmr/rules.ts` (pure, tests `npx tsx scripts/test-mmr-rules.ts`, 39 checks): `standing` (division, next, to next, progress), `tonightRun(changes, since)` (number change incl. penalties, events' record), `winStreak`, `formSquares` (last ten: gain / loss / gain to a new season peak), `setsPeak`, `milestoneTier` (world top 1,000 / 250 / 100 / 10), `viewOf(source, uptoId?)` (the card as it stood after a change), demo night (`demoSource`, `DEMO_EVENTS`, `withEvent`).
- **Looks** `src/lib/mmr/look.ts`: layouts **Deck card** (default, a card on the homepage's deck; a new division deals the next card), **Overlay panel** (the trivia/poll/bingo family, "Type !mmr in chat"), **Squares strip** (the dailies' squares), **Sticker** (also the profile badge). Free: every layout, emblem frame (tile = the site's icon tile, crest, coin, name only, off), surface (dark / light / see-through), division colors / GameShuffle blue / a profile accent, solid / glow / glyphs backgrounds, roll or count, one saved look, "Built by GameShuffle" (official wordmark, or a tab, or the GS mark). GS Pro (`pro` in `LOOK_CHOICES`): the brand themes + your two colors, stripes, dots, your picture or GIF (`/api/mmr/picture`, R2, 4 MB, only our uploads saved), Bebas Neue / Chakra Petch / Fredoka / Space Grotesk, two-color border, foil, your picture as the emblem, hiding the GameShuffle line, up to 10 looks that can follow the Twitch game (`pickLook`). Gradients are a glow on the card's own surface, never a full-color fill (Britton). A free save keeps the free version of GS Pro choices tried in the preview (`freeLook`, `freeSaved` names them); a lapsed Pro card renders `freeLook` but keeps its choices. The emblem always wears its division's colors.
- **Card** `src/components/mmr/MmrCard.tsx` + `src/styles/mmr-card.css` (every class `mmr-`, sizes in container units so it scales like an OBS source; page previews keep a min width and scroll sideways in `.mmr-frame`, never shrink text). Emblems `MmrEmblem.tsx` (our own marks: chevrons, star, gems, crown, crown in a laurel, ? in placement; MK8DX adds I/II). Moments: the change pops while the card steps back, then the number rolls (randomizer-style reels) or counts; new division = banner, glyph sparks, the layout's move (deck deals, panel/strip flip the emblem, sticker flips) and the glyph field swaps to the division's glyphs; three wins = flame chip; new peak = gold chip; first time inside a world milestone = gold laurel banner. `MmrLive.tsx`: `MmrLive` polls `/api/mmr/live/<token>` every 30s while visible and plays each new event in order (`momentsSince`), `MmrDemo` (the made-up night on a loop), `useMmrPreview` (studio's "Try a moment").
- **Store** `src/lib/mmr/store.ts` on `mmr_cards` (`supabase/mmr-cards-m1.sql`, dev + prod): `saveCard`, `resetToken` (new OBS link), `liveFor(card)` (look by Twitch game for Pro, lounge read, pins the player id, tonight = since going live on Twitch else midnight Pacific, `noteMilestone` stores the best tier per game+season and marks the first sighting `initial` so it never plays), `profileMmr`, `mmrTokenFor`. APIs `/api/mmr/card` (GET / PATCH / POST reset, signed in), `/api/mmr/lookup` (studio's Find me, rate limited), `/api/mmr/live/[token]` (public by link, edge cache 20s), `/api/mmr/picture`.
- **Surfaces:** `/mmr-card` (signed out: pitch, `MmrShowcase`, FAQ; signed in: `MmrStudio`, autosaved), `/mmr/[token]` (OBS, chrome-free, transparent via `ObsRoute`), the profile's **MMR card (lounge)** section (`mmr` in `PROFILE_SECTIONS`, the owner's look as a sticker sized to its widget column, without the Built by line since it's our own page; `show_on_profile`), `!mmr` / `!mmr <name>` (`src/lib/twitch/commands/mmr.ts`, everyone, 15s cooldown, loaded lazily from registrations because the store is server-only), `/gs-mmr [name] [game]` (`src/lib/discord/commands/mmr.ts`, deferred + follow-up; **run `npx tsx scripts/register-discord-commands.ts`**), and the GameShuffle overlay piece `mmr` (`MmrOverlay`, Layout editor ▸ Apps, off by default in format.ts; `/latest` carries `mmrToken`). Compete menu + footer, sitemap; robots disallows `/mmr/`. Events `MMR Card Saved` / `MMR Card Link Copied` (Plausible goals needed). Dev check `scripts/.tmp/mmr-e2e.ts <proJar> <freeJar>` (18).
- **Competitive dashboard (built 2026-10-20, picks https://claude.ai/artifact/D858wcmXPh87bXj6np4Nga, all as recommended; `supabase/lounge-m1.sql` on dev + prod).** Season analysis `src/lib/lounge/season.ts` (pure, tests `npx tsx scripts/test-lounge-season.ts`, 59 checks): `formatOf` (teams vs players: 12 of 12 = FFA, 6 = 2v2...), `nights` (6am to 6am Pacific, `nightOf`), `byFormat`, `byTier` (lounge order SQ, X, S, A, AB, B...), `partners` (from `partnerIds`/`partnerScores`), `placements` (FFA histogram), `paceTo` (events and nights to the next division at this season's pace), `bestWinStreak`, `seasonSeries`. Chat lines `src/lib/lounge/lines.ts` (`last10Line`, `tonightLine`, `compareLine`, `loungePagePath`), the night's recap `src/lib/lounge/recap.ts` (`nightRecap`). The lounge client keeps each event's teams, players and partners and the player's no-SQ average, biggest gain and earlier names, and adds `loungeBasic` (by id or **Discord id**) and `countryCountAbove` (a country rank from one leaderboard total). **Confirming** (`src/lib/lounge/store.ts` `confirmLounge`, `POST /api/lounge/confirm`, button `LoungeConfirm`): MKCentral keeps each player's Discord id, so the account's `users.discord_id` finds its player in each game; no codes. Links in `lounge_links`; the newest confirmation of a player wins. **Pages:** `/lounge` (search via `/lounge/find`, Your lounge, GameShuffle boards with `?game=` and `?board=everyone|friends|crew|country` (friends = you and who you follow; crew = anyone on a crew with you), climbers since Monday) and `/lounge/[name]` (`BrowseHero` with the division emblem, country rank, a "On GameShuffle" chip for confirmed players, next division with pace, six StatCards, `SeasonChart` (our own SVG with division bands: CDS charts take no reference lines; min 880px so labels stay 12px, scrolls on phones), nights, formats, partners, `PlacementBars` (our own: CDS BarChart draws no axes, it wraps them in a Fragment), tiers). Noindex unless confirmed; confirmed players are in the sitemap. Posts go to the `lounge` route category when the owner has Pro, else the default channel. Settings card `DiscordLoungeCard` (`/api/discord/bot/lounge?guild=`) on Discord bot ▸ Channels & posts (board) and Roles (roles). **Night recap** (GS Pro): the MMR card's OBS link with `?view=recap` (`MmrCard recap`, the panel skin in the player's look; the studio has its link) and a "Lounge tonight" field on the Discord end-of-stream recap (`loungeRecapField`, `liveFor(card, { since })`). Event `Lounge Confirmed` (Plausible goal needed). Not yet posted to a real Discord server (rank-ups, roles, board). Dev test links: novastreams = Kevo, quiet_quinn = Mikasa, modmarco = Saber.
- **Round 2 additions (built 2026-10-20, picks https://claude.ai/artifact/HifzmkKiM1kvZRVAf7ypzu, all as recommended; `supabase/lounge-m2.sql` on dev + prod).** The model `src/lib/lounge/odds.ts` (`eventDeltas`, `payouts`, `breakEven`, `likelyPlaces`, `aheadOdds`, `againstRoom`; per-format `PAY`: FFA 40 a pair at scale 7,000, 2v2 80/4,000, 3v3 120/4,000, 4v4 160/3,500, 6v6 200/3,500; checked by `scripts/.tmp/lounge-odds-check.ts <list.json>`: every format within ~2 MMR). **Table copy** `src/lib/lounge/tables.ts` + hourly `/api/cron/lounge-tables` (`?chunks=` for a backfill; a week per request, two days of overlap, prunes seasons older than the previous). **Early changes:** `unverifiedTables(game)` (cached a minute) + `pendingFor` (`src/lib/lounge/pending.ts`) → `MmrLiveData.pending`: a dashed chip on the card (deck/panel), a line on the lounge page, and `!mmr` adds "table in: 1st, about +244 once verified". **Match read:** `src/lib/lounge/room.ts` (`parseRoom`: numbering, mentions, MMR in brackets and headings dropped; teams one per line), `roomServer.ts` (`lookUpRoom` via `loungeBasic({ name })`, `saveRoom`/`tonightsRoom` in `lounge_rooms`, six hours), `POST /api/lounge/room`, `RoomCheck` on /lounge (Check a room: average, seed, likely finish, break-even, a payout table, the lineup), the overlay piece `lounge_room` "Tonight's room" (`LoungeRoomOverlay`, GS Pro, off by default, `/latest` carries `loungeRoom` cached 10s), `!odds` (GS Pro; **`!room` was taken by the lobby room code**), `/gs-room names [me] [format] [game]` (free; teams separated by slashes). **Head-to-head:** `src/lib/lounge/h2h.ts` (`headToHead`: two histories matched on table id, opponents vs teammates, `h2hLine`), `/lounge/compare/[a]/[b]` (noindex; side by side + meetings; nav floats via a regex in `isHeroPath`), the player page's Compare box (`/lounge/find?vs=`), `!h2h <name> [name]`, `/gs-mmr` option `versus`. **How you play** on the player page: Against the room (free, `againstRoomFor` from the copied tables: share of events above the likely place, average places), and for GS Pro viewers Late in a night (`lateInNight`), Time of night (`TimeOfNight`, the viewer's own time zone) and Rivals (`rivalsFor` → `lounge_rivals`); others see one GS Pro line (`/gs-pro?from=lounge`). Events `Lounge Room Read` (Plausible goal needed). **Run `npx tsx scripts/register-discord-commands.ts`** (adds /gs-room and /gs-mmr's versus option). Fitted on 932 MK World tables: each player's change ≈ 40 × Σ(result − 1/(1+10^((Rj−Ri)/7000))) within ~2 MMR (teams: K = 40 × team size, within 3 to 5); win odds a separate fit, scale 3,500 (higher MMR finishes ahead 66% of pairs). No live room endpoint; `/api/table/unverified` lists submitted tables before staff verify them.
- **Lounge night (built 2026-10-20, picks https://claude.ai/artifact/NGWrbcYYb93jfXf1t8xGbD, all as recommended; `supabase/lounge-night-m1.sql` on dev + prod).** **Tonight's goal** (GS Pro): `mmr_cards.goal_mmr/goal_game/goal_set_at/goal_keep`; rules `src/lib/mmr/goal.ts` (a goal counts for the 6am-to-6am Pacific night it was set on, or the Twitch stream it was set during, unless "Keep my goal"); `saveCard` refuses non-Pro (403 `pro_required`; clearing never needs Pro); `liveFor` returns `goal` only for Pro and the matching game; `MmrCard goal` draws a filling chip ("228 to 15,900", gold once reached) on every layout and the recap; `MmrPlay.goal` plays a gold "Goal reached" banner; `!mmr` adds "goal 15,500, 184 to go" (the Discord `/gs-mmr` doesn't show it yet). **Share the night** (free): share kind `lounge` (`LoungePart`, `loungeHeadline`/`loungeSub`/`loungeBest` in share/types.ts), only the account's own `lounge_links` player, the night from `nightFor` (`src/lib/lounge/night.ts`, reusing `nightRecap`), emblem drawn by `src/lib/mmr/emblemSvg.ts`; `/r/<code>` `LoungeResult` ("Kevo went +180 tonight", the card, the lounge page, make your own MMR card); button `LoungeShareNight` (`src/components/mmr/LoungeShareNight.tsx`; in the studio, and on `/lounge/[name]` only for the confirmed owner; each button makes its card on mount and `/api/share` allows 30 a minute per IP, so never a button per row). **Weekly report** (GS Pro): `/api/cron/lounge-weekly` (Mondays 16:00 and 17:00 UTC, sends from 9am Pacific; `?dry=1&user=` previews, `?force`, `?week=`), `src/lib/lounge/weekly.ts` (pure: the week is seven 6am-to-6am Pacific nights) + `weeklyReport.ts` (claims each send in `lounge_weekly_reports`; an MKCentral outage leaves the account for the next run), rivals via `lounge_rivals_between()`, notification `lounge_weekly` (Settings ▸ Notifications group "Mario Kart lounge"), email `src/lib/email/lounge.ts`, switch `mmr_cards.weekly_report` in the studio (turns off email too). - **Player page round 3 (built 2026-10-20, lounge picks https://claude.ai/artifact/NGWrbcYYb93jfXf1t8xGbD, all as recommended).** `/lounge/<name>` opens on `LoungeHero` (the band in the division's colors from `heroColors()` in season.ts, tested for AA; the emblem as the header's art at the right edge, faded behind the text on phones; eyebrow one label, "Season 3"; MMR and world rank only in the numbers strip), then the game pills (only games the player is registered in, `loungeGames`) beside `LoungeSeasonSelect` (`?season=N` shows that whole season with its own bands, top % and country rank; finished seasons are noindex with the current season as canonical), then `LoungeTabs` (CDS Tabs, `?tab=overview|events|teammates|play|career`, read on the server, switched with replaceState): Overview (`NumbersStrip`: MMR, World · top %, season peak, Events · win %, average score, Last ten with the MMR card's squares; next division; Share the night for the confirmed owner only, `LoungeShareNight` with `nightFor`, which is why `LoungeProfile.confirmed` carries a server-only `userId`; season chart; last ten), Events (nights, formats, best and worst events, tiers, places), Teammates (partners with their average, the head-to-head box), How they play (Against the room free; Rivals, Late in a night, Time of night and `DayOfWeek` for GS Pro viewers), Career (`CareerTab`, fetched from `/lounge/<name>/career?game=`, rate limited: a season table with a Career row and `CareerChart` with each season's own division lines; "Didn't play" for a skipped season). `LoungeProBand` (eyebrow "GS Pro", "Know how you play, and show it", the trial walls' offer, hidden for Pro) is on /lounge and at the foot of player pages; /gs-pro#lounge shows the same rows (`LOUNGE_PRO_ROWS`). MKCentral client: `loungePlayer(…, { season, final })`, `loungeStats(game, { season, final })`; a finished season is kept for good in memory (200 players) and Next's data cache (`force-cache`, so the player page must not be `force-dynamic`). `LOUNGE_FIRST_SEASON`: MK World 1 (shared by 12 and 24 players), MK8DX 4. Placement rows stay in `changes` with id 0 (the chart starts at the placement number; never an event, so the first win above it can show as a new-peak square), a table with a later `TableDelete` row is marked `TableDeleted`, and a non-JSON reply from MKCentral counts as unavailable. MKCentral never sends `largestLoss`; the worst event is worked out from the events. Tests `npx tsx scripts/test-lounge-season.ts` (160).
- **Lounge lookup (built 2026-10-21, picks https://claude.ai/artifact/R77gkEYWs5F1fdFYpGdvqP, all as recommended; Britton: every input clearly labeled).** `/lounge` opens on a search in the header (`LoungeLookup`): label "Find a player", helper "A lounge name, part of one, or a friend code", "Search in" chips (All games / 12P / 24P / MK8DX). From two letters `/api/lounge/suggest` (rate limited, edge cache 60s) searches MKCentral's `/player/leaderboard?search=` (matches any part of a name) in all three games, joins rows by name (`groupByName`, `mergeSuggestions` in `src/lib/lounge/lookup.ts`) and returns up to 6, best MMR first; an exact name leads only if that player has events this season. A friend code (`parseFriendCode`) uses `/player?fc=`. The list is our own ARIA combobox on a CDS Input (CDS Combobox can't render rich rows: CDS gap). Enter with nothing highlighted goes through `/lounge/find` (also resolves friend codes and opens MK8DX-only players on MK8DX). Under the search: "Looked up lately" (localStorage `gs-lounge-recent`, `LoungeRecent`, written by `RememberLookup` on player pages) and `LoungeCompare` (modal: First player / Second player / Game → `/lounge/find?vs=`). Page order: the season so far in one line (`seasonSummary`: players, events, median MMR, tables waiting via `unverifiedTables`); Boards (`LoungeBoards`, CDS Tabs, `?board=world|climbing|countries|gs`, gs = the GameShuffle boards with `?scope=`, old `?board=friends` links still work; climbing = `sortBy=LastWeekRankChange`, negative = climbed; countries = six or more players ranked by top six average); Divisions (`#divisions`, `divisionTable`, `LandingTry` "Try an MMR": exact count above from `/api/lounge/land` via `minMmr`+`minEventsPlayed=1`, else `estimateAbove` from division counts); "For lounge players" (Check a room in a CDS flush Accordion, forced to `height: auto` while open because CDS measures it once; Your lounge). Server reads `src/lib/lounge/lookupServer.ts` (memory cache, stale read on outage). Player page: `playerSentence` under the name, Compare and Share in `LoungeHero actions` (`LoungeShareButton`: share sheet, else copy), "Near X in the world" on Overview (`nearInWorld`, skip = rank − 3). A name nobody has shows "Did you mean" (`closestPlayers`: first 3-4 letters, the whole name and the last 3, all three games, by edit distance; players with no events this season count one edit further). Tests `npx tsx scripts/test-lounge-lookup.ts` (81). Events `Lounge Search Used`, `Lounge Board Viewed`, `Lounge Player Shared`, `Lounge Compare Used` (Plausible goals needed). `.lounge-season` is the season picker's class; don't reuse it.
- **What's on the card (built 2026-10-21, picks https://claude.ai/artifact/YNPyD2rhBNbR9dTP3zGCXb, all as recommended, no migration).** `MmrLook.info` replaces `show`: a switch per piece (`on`), `big` (mmr or world), `recent` (3 or 5), GS Pro's `order` and `line` (40 characters, word filter in `saveCard`; refused lines save empty with `lineRefused`), and a switch per moment. The single list of pieces is `src/lib/mmr/pieces.ts`: `PIECES` in groups Who/Numbers/Tonight/Season (`mmr` is read only by Rolling), `PRESETS` (Just the number, Number and division, Standard = new looks, Everything; presets set free pieces only), `pieceText` (each piece as label, value, chip and `short` for the strip's narrow slot, 20 characters at most), `LAYOUT_SPOTS` (own spots and room for details: deck 3 chips, panel 6 tiles, strip 1, sticker 1) and `detailsFor` + `fitSlots` (extras take turns in the last slot every 8s with dots). The strip measures whether its progress fits beside the division (`useStripRoom` in MmrCard); if not, it hides and joins the details, never cut to a stub. With no next division the progress tile says "Top division" instead of repeating the MMR. Icons: `src/components/mmr/pieceIcons.ts`. Old looks (`show`) map by their saved layout so they keep exactly what they drew (`legacyInfo`). New pieces: country rank (`liveFor` reads `countryCountAbove` only when shown), Top % (`totalPlayers` from `loungeStats`), win streak (from 2 wins; the flame at 3 when that moment is on), recent events, events and win %, average score; season numbers come from the changes (`seasonNumbers`, identical to MKCentral's). GS Pro: your own order (`SortableList`) and a line of your own; `freeLook` uses list order and turns the line off but keeps its text. The profile badge keeps its usual pieces. Studio: three tabs (Look, What's on the card, Moments; `MmrInfoControls.tsx`). Shared card parts (number roll, moment layers and `playMoment`, theme variables, summary) live in `src/components/mmr/cardParts.tsx`.
- **Rolling (built 2026-10-21, picks https://claude.ai/artifact/R77gkEYWs5F1fdFYpGdvqP, all as recommended).** A fifth layout that shows one piece at a time (`src/lib/mmr/rolling.ts`, `src/components/mmr/MmrRolling.tsx`). `look.rolling = { shape: bar|box|tall, pin, motion: up|across|flip|fade|deal|ticker, secs: 4|6|8|12, perSlide, progress: dots|line|none }`, cleaned by `cleanRolling`; defaults bar, pinned, slide up, 6s, dots. Slides (`rollSlides`) are the pieces that are on and have a value, in the look's order (own order and own line included). Name, flag and division are the pinned head or an "id" first slide; the game sits under the MMR; the last change is a chip beside it. One slide timer per card, re-armed per slide (`slideSecs`), paused while hidden or during a change. A change jumps to the MMR slide (a temporary one if it's off; over the ticker it fades in), plays the pop with the moment switches, rolls the held-back number in, holds `ROLL_HOLD_MS`, then `resumeAfter` returns to the slide it left. Reduced motion: cuts or fades, and the ticker becomes slides. Every shape and motion is free; a time per slide is GS Pro (`freeRolling`). Studio: Layout ▸ Rolling shows Shape, Name and emblem, Motion, Each slide, Progress, and the GS Pro times folded away. OBS widths: bar 700, box 380, tall 320, heights measured from the preview. The showcase's rolling bar plays an event every 20s. Tests in `scripts/test-mmr-rules.ts` (166).

### Tournaments
- Normalized data model: `tournaments`, `tournament_participants`
- Tracks identified by unique IDs (`c{cupIdx}-t{courseIdx}`) to handle duplicate names
- Track selection modes: guided, ffa, randomized, limited
- Drag-and-drop track ordering via `@dnd-kit` (`SortableTrackList` component)
- Build restrictions: weight class, drift type, character ban/allow lists
- Custom item selection when items set to "custom"
- `requireVerified` setting — organizer toggle for email-verified-only tournaments
- Organizer preview: public page shows viewer perspective (no private data)
- Participant join pulls profile data (display name, friend code, Discord) automatically
- Real-time participant updates via Supabase Realtime
- **Random-character format** (randomized rounds for games with a roster): `settings.randomizer.dimensions.roster = { noRepeat }` rolls a pick per confirmed player each round (`directive.playerPicks`) from `src/lib/tournaments/rosters.ts` (Smash fighters minus Miis, Mario Party characters, Splatoon kits with sub + special, Kirby riders with a machine). No-repeat walks each player's earlier picks (`picksByPlayer`); Mario Party and Kirby rounds keep picks distinct (`unique`), Smash/Splatoon allow duplicates. Ban/allow character lists apply. Roster labels live in `src/data/randomizer-games.ts` (`roster`); hidden games are filtered out of `RANDOMIZER_GAME_OPTIONS`.
- **Lifecycle and archiving (decided 2026-10-07, `supabase/events-archive-m1.sql`, rules in `src/lib/events/lifecycle.ts`):** statuses are `draft | open | in_progress | complete | cancelled` (no check constraint). Cancelled or archived (`archived_at`, organizer action via `/api/tournament/[id]/archive`, `/api/game-nights/[id]/archive`, undo any time) = off every list and search (browse, profiles, hub, composer upcoming, more-from, reminders), page still loads by link with a note, noindex. Drafts are private (RLS: organizer + co-organizers) and a draft nobody joined can be deleted (`/api/tournament/[id]/draft`). Stale = an open tournament dated more than 2 days ago that never started: off browse, doesn't hold a free organizer's one active slot (DB trigger + `canCreateTournament`), and the organizer is nudged once (`nudgeStaleTournaments`, event-reminders cron, `stale_nudged_at`). Stuck (2026-10-19) = still `in_progress` more than 3 days (`STUCK_LIVE_DAYS`) after its date: off browse and search too (`notStaleOpenFilter` covers both rules), its own "Still marked as running" group in My Stuff, one nudge sharing `stale_nudged_at`; it still holds the active slot (the DB trigger only knows stale opens). Dev had 13 of these from September. Browse: All = live/upcoming then "Recent results" (a year, newest first), no Cancelled tab, championship events only on their season page. My Stuff groups cancelled + archived into a collapsed Archived accordion and lists co-organized tournaments and championship seasons. Championships: End season = `status 'archived'` ("Season complete").
- **Formats:** `ffa_points`, `round_robin` (generic race scoring), `single_elim`/`double_elim` (bracket blob in `tournaments.bracket`), and **`heat_mains`** — the sprint-car consi ladder persisted in `tournaments.heat_mains` jsonb. Pure engines in `src/lib/tournaments/{bracket,scoring,heatMains}.ts`; shared run/results UI in `src/components/tournament/{BracketView,HeatMainsView}.tsx` (the same components power the DB-free `/tournament/sandbox`)
- **Swiss with deck lists (built 2026-10-12, mockups https://claude.ai/artifact/UdRxhmQDmTUsNzFjiWiMUy, `supabase/swiss-m1.sql` on dev + prod):** format `swiss`, any 1v1 game. Pure engine `src/lib/tournaments/swiss.ts` (tests `npx tsx scripts/test-swiss.ts`, ~2,800 checks) follows the Play! Pokémon handbook (revision Sept 1, 2026): `STRUCTURE` = TCG Single Day (4-8 players 3 rounds no cut … 9-12 4 + Top 4, 13-20 5 + Top 4, 21-32 5 + Top 8 … the cut stops at Top 8), fixed when round 1 is paired; round 1 random, then random inside each match-point bracket pairing down, no rematches (`pairList` backtracking, rematches only if nothing else works), the bye to the lowest bracket at random and never twice; win 3 / tie 1; standings by points, opponents' win %, opponents' opponents' win % (win % = wins without byes / rounds, floor 25%, cap 75% after a drop), head-to-head when exactly two tie, then a fixed hash order; late joiners get losses for missed rounds (`lateFrom`); top cut = `generateSingleElim` from the standings replayed with every result (`cutBracket`). State on `tournaments.swiss` (version-guarded writes in `swissStore.ts` `mutate`); each player's own report is a `tournament_swiss_reports` row (`r3` / `c:<matchId>`), a match counts once both agree or the organizer sets it (`tableStatus`), and a finished round is frozen into the blob when the next one is paired; records can change until the round after next is paired. Route `/api/tournament/[id]/swiss` (GET the view; POST organizer start/pair/repair/cut/finish/result/timer/drop_player/undrop, player report/drop; a drop concedes an unfinished match), `/api/tournaments/swiss-ready`. New rounds and new top cut matches alert each player with their table and opponent (`notifyRound`, `tournament_youre_up`, once per player per match). UI in `src/components/tournament/swiss/`: `SwissSettingsFields` (create form + manage settings), `SwissRunPanel` (manage dashboard, near the top: clock with +5 / pause / restart, tables with one-sided reports shown, a result window to settle, drop, re-pair before anyone reports, one main button), `SwissPublicPanel` (the player's match card with report buttons, the round, standings with the cut line, the top cut, earlier rounds), `SwissBoard` (TV display), `useSwiss` (4s refresh while running). Swiss hides team mode and the seeding draw. **Deck lists** (Pokémon TCG only, `settings.deckLists` {when off/join/start, format standard/expanded/any, after private/top_cut/all}, `src/lib/tournaments/deckLists.ts` + `deckListStore.ts`, route `/api/tournament/[id]/deck-lists`): a copy in `tournament_deck_lists` (service role; the player, organizers, and the public only after the event as chosen), locked once the event starts; `quick-join` takes `{ deckId }` and refuses `deck_required`, guests are pointed to an account; `checkDeck(lines, info, format)` (Expanded checks counts and warns about the card pool, "any" skips marks); `/api/tcg/decks?format=` checks saved decks against a tournament's format. UI in `src/components/tournament/decks/`: `DeckListPicker` (join card + swap; reuses `PasteDeckModal`), `MyDeckListCard`, `OrganizerDeckLists` (stats, missing with Remind, open + Mark checked), `PublicDeckLists` (Copy for TCG Live, Save to my decks). Dev end to end: `scripts/.tmp/swiss-e2e.ts` (60 checks, Nova runs it, Marco/Steph/Tara play from their accounts with a cookie jar, seven guests; `SWISS_STOP=2` leaves it mid-round for screenshots).
- **Manage page in tabs (2026-10-13, mockups + picks https://claude.ai/artifact/8FDrgYjfNMsJAeuwSrpeL8):** a status bar (status, when · where, the next step; Archive / Cancel / Delete draft under a CDS Dropdown "More"), then CDS Tabs used as a bar (`?tab=run|players|rules|page`, read with `useSyncExternalStore`, written with `history.replaceState`; `.manage-tabs .empac-tabs__content` hidden). **Run** exists when there's something to run (Swiss always, brackets/heats/flights once open, everything once in progress) and opens first while running: the Swiss console, live race control, brackets, heats, flights, scoring, final results, and the Mario Party / Smash panels once started. **Players** opens first before the start: counts (CDS Cards) that filter one table, the shared `AttendeeTable` with `rowDetail` / `rowActions` / `toolbarStart` / `statusFilter` / `reloadKey` / `hideStats` (Accept / Reject / Drop / Remove, team and crew pickers, claim links, the check-in switch; the old Participants list and Pending window are gone), check-in closing, Add people (find a player / invite by email / add a guest), lobby codes, crews (folded until used), deck lists, and tickets (`TicketingManager hideWhenPaused`: nothing while paid entry is paused unless the event has orders). **Format & rules**: when, where and sign-ups (date, place, who can enter, verified email, Accept people automatically/by hand), Format and seeding (`SeedingPanel` folds a random draw's names behind "Show the order"), the Mario Party / Smash panels before the start, Races and tracks (race settings, tracks, `TournamentRandomizerCard bare`), Build restrictions folded to a one-line summary, Rules for players (rules + build notes, still two fields). **Page & team**: co-organizers, page branding and display board in one card with one Circuit note. Max players still can't be changed after create (it feeds the waitlist engine). Styles: `.manage-*` in competitive.css.
- **Create form (2026-10-13):** What are you running (CDS pick cards with the site's icon tile, `.run-pick`) → The event (name, game search, six popular games + "Show all") → Format ("Draft it with AI" opens `AiTournamentHelper bare` in a Modal; a game GameShuffle doesn't know keeps its Rules here, since they are the format) → When and who (single only) → More options, a folded Accordion (description, organizer and community, rules) that opens itself when the AI fills it. Styles: `.create-*` in competitive.css.
- **Game picker on create (2026-10-10):** one search over every game (our games first, then `GAME_CATALOG`; CDS Combobox with "Use" for any typed name, the pick's box art beside it), then **Most popular games**: fixed-width CDS `Card`s (box art + name, `.game-pick__grid`, 6 across at the form's width, 3 on phones) for the games with GameShuffle tools. A catalog game keeps its catalog slug and name; only Mario Kart gets race settings, other games without tools store `{ game_label }`.
- **Official GameShuffle tournaments (built 2026-10-10, `supabase/official-events-m1.sql`):** an admin's create form has **Host as GameShuffle** (`tournaments.hosted_by_gs`; a trigger lets only admins set it). The page shows GameShuffle as the host (`OrganizerCard official`, monogram, no follow), an Official badge, JSON-LD organizer GameShuffle; browse shows organizer "GameShuffle" and an Official tag (the view's `organizer_name`); the share card says "Official GameShuffle tournament". **The name is held** (`src/lib/reservedName.ts` + the same pattern in the database, `gs_name_is_reserved`): a tournament title or community name with "GameShuffle" is refused unless official (the manage page now toasts refused saves instead of showing them as saved); a display name or community name with it keeps the old name for anyone but staff/admins (silently in the database, because the sign-in sync writes other fields in the same update; the profile editor says why first). Circuit: official events already get every feature (the organizer is an admin). Separate from Platform ▸ Events ▸ "Sponsor it" (`tournament_entitlements` `gs_sponsored`, free Circuit for someone else's event).
- **Create flow** (`/tournament/create`): a Single vs Championship toggle. Single → a `tournaments` row (any format). Championship → a `championships` row → `/tournament/championship/[id]/manage`
- **Championship Series** (a season of Heat → Mains events): `src/lib/championships.ts` + tables `championships`, `championship_members` (accounts-only roster), `championship_invitations` (email invites); each event is a `tournaments` row with `championship_id`/`event_number` and format `heat_mains`. Manage at `/tournament/championship/[id]/manage` (roster invites — platform search + email; events; season standings), public season page at `/tournament/championship/[id]`. Invite/join via `/api/championship/*` + `/championship/join/[token]` (email → signup w/ `?redirect` → auto-join). Season points: tiered A-Main-premium curve + light heat bonus (`src/lib/tournaments/championship.ts`, `computeSeason`). Schema: `supabase/championship-m1.sql`

### Auth & Security
- Supabase Auth: email/password, magic link, Discord OAuth, Twitch OAuth, Facebook OAuth
- **Sign in with Facebook (built 2026-10-18, picks https://claude.ai/artifact/MgHT7WBbCAPiDwcwrRF4b3; X left out because its API is pay-per-use):** hidden behind the switch `auth_facebook` (Platform ▸ Switches, group Sign-in), read by `/api/auth/providers`; login and sign-up build their buttons from `useSignInProviders()` (`src/lib/auth/providers.ts`, Discord and Twitch always, Facebook once the switch is on) and the consent line from `providerList()`. Connections (`src/lib/connections.ts`) shows Facebook while the switch is on or it's already linked. A new Facebook account's display name is the **first name only** (`syncProfileFromOAuth`: display names are public and Facebook names are real full names); its picture is never an avatar choice. The backup password page names the provider the account signed up with. Meta app = "GameShuffle Login" (live); Supabase Facebook provider on in both projects. Privacy 2.2 and 8.3 (deleting Facebook data) cover it; Meta's data deletion URL is `/privacy#data-retention-deletion`.
- **Auth errors are plain language + reported** (`src/lib/auth/errors.ts` `describeAuthError`: Supabase code/message → message with a next step + `report` flag). Never show `error.message` from Supabase directly. `/auth/callback` keeps the failure reason (error params or a failed code exchange), logs it, reports it to Sentry (`reportAuthError`, tags `auth_code`/`auth_provider`/`auth_surface`; only our/provider failures, not typos or cancels) and redirects to `/login?auth_error=<code>` (or back to an explicit `/account…` destination for the Connect button). `AuthErrorNotice` shows it on login, signup and Connections; `AuthHashErrorCatcher` (root layout) sends errors Supabase dropped after the `#` on other pages to /login. Every Twitch/Discord button starts through `startOAuth` (`src/lib/auth/oauth.ts`), which remembers the provider in sessionStorage (return URLs are untouched: they must stay on Supabase's redirect allowlist or sign-in silently falls back to the Site URL).
- **Sign-ins that skip `/auth/callback` (found 2026-10-16):** when a return URL isn't on Supabase's redirect allowlist, Supabase sends the person to the Site URL with `?code=` and the browser client signs them in on its own, so the callback never runs (no Twitch/Discord ids, avatar or gamertags saved, no backup-password prompt, `?redirect=` lost). GoTrue's auth log `referer` field is where it sent them. Since August most Twitch/Discord accounts had no `twitch_id`/`discord_id` this way. Now `syncProfileFromOAuth` (`src/lib/auth/syncOAuthProfile.ts`) also runs from `/api/account/ensure-username` (AuthProvider, once per browser session), and the menu name (`useDisplayIdentity`) and the TCG Companion use the profile's `display_name`/`username`, never the email address. The allowlist itself lives in Supabase ▸ Authentication ▸ URL Configuration; `https://www.gameshuffle.co/**` and `https://gameshuffle.co/**` were added 2026-10-16 (check the auth log's `referer` on `/authorize` after any change there).
- **Blind spot:** if Supabase fails at its own `/callback` and renders an error page instead of redirecting (the 2026-09-29 bad-secret incident), neither our site nor Sentry sees it. Detection there needs Supabase auth logs.
- Cloudflare Turnstile CAPTCHA on signup, login (passed to Supabase as `captchaToken`), forgot password, guest joins, contact/beta/report/ideas forms and the support checkout
- **Rate limits** (`src/lib/ratelimit.ts`) use Upstash Redis or Vercel KV when `UPSTASH_REDIS_REST_URL`/`_TOKEN` (or `KV_REST_API_URL`/`_TOKEN`) are set, else memory per server instance, which a bot spread across instances can outrun. Upstash for Redis (Vercel Marketplace, free, us-east-1) was connected on 2026-10-18 for Production and Preview (`KV_REST_API_URL` / `KV_REST_API_TOKEN`); it takes effect from the first deployment after that.
- Brute force protection: client-side lockout after 5 failed attempts (60s cooldown)
- Email verification required for tournament create/join + tier-gated features
- Verified badge system (`VerifiedBadge` component, `email_verified` column synced via DB trigger)
- Password requirements: min 8 chars, uppercase, lowercase, number, special character. One copy for every form: `src/lib/auth/passwordRules.ts` (`passwordProblem`, `PASSWORD_HINT`); `describeAuthError` reads a weak password's `reasons` (breach vs rules)
- **Password flows (audited + fixed 2026-10-10):** "has a password" = `app_metadata.has_password`, kept by a trigger on auth.users (`supabase/auth-has-password-m1.sql`; `hasPasswordFrom` / `passwordFlag`, falling back to `providers` containing `email` until it runs). The providers list alone was wrong: Supabase only adds `email` after an email sign-in, so a Twitch/Discord account that saved a password was asked forever. Email links should use the token-hash templates (`{{ .SiteURL }}/auth/confirm?token_hash={{ .TokenHash }}&type=recovery&next={{ .RedirectTo }}`, type per template, `next` last): /auth/confirm shows one button (so link scanners can't use the link up) and `/auth/confirm/verify` runs `verifyOtp` on any device; the PKCE `/auth/callback` route only works in the browser that asked. Allowlist shared in `src/lib/auth/redirects.ts` (2026-10-13: widened to every page that sends people to sign in, e.g. /tcg-companion, /pokemon-tcg/, /trivia, /daily, /weekly, /chat-brain, /communities, /c/, /game-nights, /ideas, /mod/, /discord; also refuses backslashes and control characters; a page that starts a new sign-in flow with `?redirect=` needs its prefix here, or Twitch/Discord/magic-link sign-in lands on /account). /login runs `?redirect=` through `safeRedirect` (the password path used to `router.push` it raw) and URL-encodes it into the callback link; a failed reset link goes to /forgot-password with the reason. Reset and the backup-password prompt ask for the 2FA code first when the account has it (`useSecondStep`, `MfaChallenge allowRecovery={false}`; Supabase refuses `updateUser({password})` from an aal1 session). Settings handles `reauthentication_needed` (emails a code, retries with `nonce`). Middleware: only a **verified** factor counts, and a half-signed-in session is sent to `/login?mfa=1` instead of bouncing between /login and /account. **Recovery codes (fixed 2026-10-11, Britton's call):** a valid code (`recovery.consume` on `/api/account/mfa`) turns two-factor off for the account (`turnOffMfaAfterRecovery`: every factor removed through the admin API, the other codes voided, the setup nudge back), and since removing a verified factor signs the account out everywhere, the route signs this browser straight back in (`admin.generateLink` magiclink + `verifyOtp` on the server client). The person gets an email, a text to a verified number and an in-app `system` notice asking them to set it up again; `MfaChallenge` shows "Two-factor sign-in is off" with Continue. Recovery codes now work on the reset-password and set-password pages too.
- Leaked password rejection (Supabase server-side)
- **Backup passwords (changed 2026-10-10):** every account should have one, but it's a strong recommendation, not a wall. A Twitch/Discord-only account is sent to `/signup/set-password` ("Add a backup password"), which offers **Not now** (`POST /api/account/password-later` sets the 30-day `gs-pw-later` cookie, `src/lib/auth/passwordNudge.ts`; the middleware skips the redirect while it's set). Staff and admins get no Not now (the route refuses them, `/api/account/needs-password` returns `canSkip`). Settings ▸ Sign-in & security shows "Add a backup password (recommended)" until one is saved.
- Account deletion: self-service via `/api/account/delete` — full cascade (Stripe sub cancel + Twitch disconnect: revoke tokens + delete EventSub subs + remove channel point rewards)
- Manual identity linking enabled (Discord/Twitch link/unlink)
- **Handle uniqueness** — public handles (`users.username`, used at `/u/[username]` + as the `/live` slug) are lowercase, unique case-insensitively (unique index on `lower(username)`), and format/reserved-word gated at the DB (`supabase/username-hardening.sql`). Shared rules in `src/lib/username.ts` (`validateUsername`, `RESERVED_USERNAMES`); availability pre-check at `/api/account/username`. Slug reads lowercase the incoming param. Discord/Twitch usernames are display metadata (not unique)
- Middleware at `src/middleware.ts` protects `/account/*` and `/twitch/*`, gates `/login`/`/signup` for already-signed-in users, AND writes `x-pathname` header for theming
- `AuthProvider` context wraps the app; `UserMenu` in navbar with avatar support
- Staff impersonation: `src/components/staff/{ImpersonationBanner, ImpersonationProviderMount, ImpersonationControlMount}` — staff-only "act as user" with banner + floating control. Real Supabase user + impersonated user both available via `useImpersonation()`

### Database / RLS
- **Service-role conventions:** server-only admin APIs use `createServiceClient()` from `src/lib/supabase/admin.ts`. Never ship the service role key to the client
- **`user_directory` view** joins `public.users` with `auth.users.email`. Hardened with `WITH (security_invoker = on)` + REVOKE from anon/authenticated + GRANT to service_role. Requires `service_role` to have SELECT on `auth.users` (granted via `supabase/grant-service-role-auth-users.sql`)
- **RLS policy hygiene** — three lints to stay clean on:
  1. `auth.uid()` / `auth.jwt()` always wrapped in `(SELECT …)` — Postgres else treats VOLATILE and re-evaluates per row. Migration: `supabase/rls-auth-uid-perf-fix.sql` (dynamic, idempotent)
  2. No multiple permissive policies per `(table, role, command)` — each gets evaluated per row. Migration: `supabase/policies-dedupe-permissive.sql` (split FOR ALL into per-cmd OR merge FOR SELECTs)
  3. Every public-schema function/procedure has `SET search_path = ''` (Supabase security lint). Migration: `supabase/functions-search-path-lock.sql`
- **Bootstrap an admin** via `supabase/bootstrap-admin-britton.sql` (idempotent, audits to `gs_role_audit_log` if the table exists)

### Account (Multi-Section, Sidebar Navigation)
- **The IA is four sections, each its own route** (Account, My Stuff, Streaming,
  Platform), driven by `src/lib/account/nav.ts` (single source of truth) +
  `AccountSidebar.tsx`. Tabs switch via `?tab=`; a tab hitting the wrong section's
  route auto-redirects (`sectionForTab`/`hrefForTab`, other query params kept).
  Nav items can be `proOnly` / `freeOnly`; `src/app/account/layout.tsx` works out
  Pro (`effectiveTier`) and the sidebar hides the rest.
- All admin tabs (prefix `Platform*`) gate on `effectiveTier({ tier, role }) === 'pro'` or `role IN ('staff','admin')`

**Account** (`/account`, everyone; consolidated 2026-10-09, plan https://claude.ai/artifact/ETbprH4zVurfNNSv6ZuL28): four views with tabs, built on the admin kit. Screens in `src/components/account/{profile,theming,settings}/`; `src/app/account/page.tsx` is just `ProfileStoreProvider` + the trial banner + `SectionTabs`.
- **Profile:** About you (avatar, display name, username, banner, bio, pronouns, location) · Games = **My gaming collection** (`GamingCollection`, 2026-10-10: one shelf of video games (`user_game_profiles` rows) and board games; star a game to show it on your profile (`users.favorite_games`, max 12, shown in the order starred; no reordering, Britton 2026-10-10); add from the catalog or any typed name; a details window per game with `MyGameEditor` for games with settings; favorites are migrated into owned rows on load) plus board game preferences (genre chips with an "Other" add, comfort level, lengths); old My Stuff `my-games`/`board-games` ids alias here · Gamertags & socials · Friends (`TopFriendsEditor`).
- **Theming:** Theme = one **Colors** section (2026-10-10: free accounts pick one color with CDS ColorPicker; GS Pro picks a brand theme (`ThemeTab bare`), and a brand theme other than the default overrides the color in `getOwnerThemeVars`) + `ProfileSkinEditor` (no card border/corner controls any more: `skinCssVars` always sets medium corners and a 1px border) + `ProfileCssEditor`, Pro · Profile page (`ProfileStatusEditor`, pinned post, favorite TCG card, links, layout) · Walk-up song (`AnthemSettings`).
- **Settings:** General (email, Display = `ThemeToggle`, timezone, live page recap) · Sign-in & security (`ConnectionsCard`, password, `TwoFactorCard`) · Notifications · Privacy (public profile, blocked, data request, delete account).
- **Plans & billing** (`PlansTab`). **Support GameShuffle** (`SupportScreen`, picks 2026-10-19).
- **ProfileStore** (`src/components/account/profile/ProfileStore.tsx`) loads the `users` row once for the section and autosaves it, sending only the changed columns (handle checks as before). Components that own other data save themselves.
- **One status line, one game:** the old `profile_tagline` / `profile_featured_game` show on /u (and seed the editor) only while `profile_status` / `profile_now_playing` are NULL; the status API stores `""` when cleared so the old value never returns. The old columns stay in the database.

**My Stuff** (`/account/stuff`): Events (Tournaments · Game nights) · Purchases (Tickets · Shop orders · Payouts; Payouts only when `paid_tickets_enabled` is on or the user has a `gs_connect_accounts` row) · Saved configs (`SetupsTab`; track-list, player-preset and ruleset are hidden since nothing on the site makes them; every setup has a View window with `ConfigVisual` (`src/components/configs/`, shared with the profile), Open in the randomizer and Copy share link; `?config=` also loads item sets) · Decks (`DecksTab`, 2026-10-10: your own Chance cards, missions and house rules for game nights and live nights, moved from Streaming ▸ Game defaults; what each kind does, then `DeckEditor scope="me" ai` with **Make cards with AI**; free accounts see the trial prompt) · My Collection (Cards = My Cards · Deck lists = `TcgDecksTab`, the Pokémon TCG deck lists below). `StuffTabs` passes a screen map to `SectionTabs`; leaving a tab out of the map hides it. **Design pass 2026-10-09:** every screen is an `AdminScreen`. Events are compact rows (`src/components/account/stuff/StuffList.tsx`: `StuffRow` on CDS List, thumbnail = the event's cover, else the game's box art, else its generated art; title links the public page, a Manage button only on yours) grouped by stage: Tournaments = Running now, Championship seasons, Coming up, Didn't start (`isStaleOpen`), Finished (5 at a time), Archived accordion; Game nights = Coming up, Past, Archived. Discovery pages keep the big `EventCard`. Times use `formatEventShort` in the profile timezone (the page passes `users.timezone`), else the browser's. Tickets, Shop orders and Payouts ▸ By event are `AdminTable`s; a ticket moves to Past 12 hours after its event starts. Each ticket has a **Receipt** (CDS Modal from `MyTicket.receipt`: price, the fees the buyer paid, total, order number; Print shows only the receipt via `@media print`); shop order pages have **Print receipt** (`PrintButton`, print styles in shop.css). Saved configs deletes ask first (`useConfirm`) and `SetupCard` uses `IconAction`.

**Sections share `SectionTabs`** (`src/components/account/SectionTabs.tsx`): views/tabs from `nav.ts`, CDS Tabs for multi-tab views, `?tab=` aliases resolved, and a tab owned by another section redirected there. Platform admin uses it too.

**Autosave everywhere (decided 2026-10-09):** settings save themselves through `useAutosave` (`src/lib/hooks/useAutosave.ts`): waits ~1s after the last change, skips unchanged and initial values, one request at a time, flushes on unmount, toast "Saved". Header status via `SaveStatus` (admin kit). Explicit buttons only where a check comes first: password change, delete account, custom CSS, overlay layout, edit windows. Converted so far: profile fields, brand theme, walk-up song, My Games, Discord channel routing, Tool looks (dice, coin, timer, Oracle, Bingo, Tier list), chat feed, stream schedule, walk-up policy.

**Streaming** (`/account/streaming`; Stream Setup + Community & Chat merged 2026-10-09, mockups https://claude.ai/artifact/K23oSsSY9FJvFBKfVTfX6S). Screen map in `src/components/account/streaming/StreamingTabs.tsx`; the page resolves Pro and the profile timezone server-side. Six views:
- **Setup:** Twitch (`TwitchHubTab`: EventSub health, overlay tokens, channel points; reauth banner dismissible, heals sub drift once/12h) · Discord (`DiscordBotRoutingCard`, install + default channel; the install returns to `?tab=discord`) · YouTube & Kick · Schedule (`StreamScheduleEditor bare`, uses the profile timezone from Settings ▸ General, warns when the browser's differs) · Mods (`ModsTab bare`).
- **Discord bot:** `DiscordBotTab part="posts|roles|moderation|qotd"` = Channels & posts (routing saves on change, go-live alerts, announcements) · Roles · AutoMod & logs · Question of the Day. No server yet = an EmptyState pointing to Setup ▸ Discord.
- **Overlay:** Layout (`OverlayLayoutTab bare`; keeps its Save button) · Tool looks (`StreamToolsTab`: one row per tool, Edit opens a CDS Drawer, autosaves; Oracle/Bingo/Tier list use the shared config cards with `bare`) · Wheels (`WheelsTab bare`) · Chat feed (`ChatOverlaySettings`, autosaves; 2026-10-10: messages on screen 1-25, how long each stays (until pushed off, 15s to 2 min, also hidden on reload), text size S/M/L, newest at the top or bottom; columns from `supabase/chat-feed-options-m1.sql`, and reads/writes fall back without them). **Layout editor** (2026-10-10): the canvas fills its column; a toolbar picks one piece from a grouped Select (Tools/Apps), with All pieces / Only this one (the others dim and can't be grabbed), 16:9 / 9:16 and Preview; the selected piece gets size 50-200% (RangeSlider), a 3×3 Snap to grid and Show; the OBS links sit in a collapsed "Add to OBS or Streamlabs" accordion.
- **Chat:** Commands · Modules (`CommunityTab`, `ModulesManager embedded`) · Game defaults (`GameModulesTab`, Mario Kart session defaults only; decks moved to My Stuff ▸ Decks). **Commands** (`CommandsScreen`, `src/components/account/commands/`, 2026-10-10; replaced the Commands + Built-in tabs, `?tab=built-in` aliases here) is one searchable list of every command on the channel from `commandCatalog()` (`src/lib/commands/catalog.ts`, `/api/commands/catalog`): the 82 built-ins (registry help + `COMMAND_GUIDE` in `src/lib/twitch/commands/guide.ts`: group, needs, example; `scripts/test-command-routing.ts` fails if a built-in has no entry), the ready-made replies (`gs_default_commands` + overrides; viewer wording in `REPLY_SUMMARIES`), your own commands and the events people type (`!hug @name`). Each row: how to type it, what it does, who can use it, a switch, and details (example, reply text with Change/Use ours/Answers, Edit/Delete for yours). "Works on my stream / Everything" hides what can't happen on this streamer's streams (`streamSetup`: games from the last 60 days or upcoming, a wheel, a live tournament, party nights). Rows that never answer (a custom command or reply another command takes first) say so (`shadowedBy`). Built-in switches write `gs_command_toggles` (`supabase/command-toggles-m1.sql`, `src/lib/twitch/commands/toggles.ts`; dispatch checks it, missing table = all on). Variables and event wording open in Drawers. **Viewers' page** `/c/<slug>/commands` (public, noindex, owner theme): only what's on, works and isn't shadowed, mod commands in a closed section, Print (one-sheet print styles in `src/styles/commands-sheet.css`) and Copy for Discord. `!gs help` and the `!commands` reply (`{commands_link}` template variable) point there.
- **Live games:** Polls (`PollsTab`: Running now, Start a poll, Recent polls table) · Bingo · Chat Draft · Chat Brain.
- **Viewers:** Engagement · Walk-up songs (`ChannelAnthemSettings bare`, autosaves).
- **Free accounts** see only **Get started** (what streaming with GS Pro does, the trial buttons, three steps with their Twitch link status) and **Discord bot** (Channels & posts with the locked Pro list; one server works on Free). Every other tab falls back to Get started.
- Older tab components are wrapped in `AdminScreen` with `admin-screen--wrap`, which hides their own `h2.account-tab__heading` title (`--section` keeps a section heading).

**Platform admin (staff/admin only), reorganized 2026-10-09** (plan https://claude.ai/artifact/GXdp7FE3ui95uGZUf2Robx): ten views in five sidebar groups, each view a set of tabs (CDS Tabs, URL-synced). Every tab keeps its `?tab=platform-*` id, so old links still open the right screen; `nav.ts` items carry `group` and `tabs`, and `itemTabIds` / `viewForTab` / `sectionForTab` look inside views. `PlatformTabs.tsx` maps tab id → screen (`SCREENS`); add a new screen there and to `nav.ts`.
