- **Run the site:** Overview (Health · Growth · Revenue · AI usage; **Revenue** (2026-10-10, `PlatformRevenueTab`, `/api/admin/revenue`, `src/lib/billing/revenue.ts`): monthly revenue from paying subscriptions in the `subscriptions` mirror at their plan's price (annual / 12, via `planFromStripePrice`, else tier + period length), trials and what they'd add, cancellations coming up, by plan, accounts → Twitch → tried GS Pro → paying, latest changes; before tax and Stripe fees) · **Switches** (`PlatformSwitchesTab`: every `gs_platform_flags` switch the code reads, from the registry `src/lib/platform/switches.ts`, shown even before it has a row; plain names, On/Off meaning, last changed by; admins flip after a confirmation, staff see; `/api/admin/platform-flags` PUT is admin-only and registry keys only. **Add new flags to the registry.**)
- **Shop:** Shop (Orders · Products · Featured cards = the TCGplayer cards, formerly "Shop")
- **Content:** Originals (Overview · Chat Brain · Weekly Challenge · Trivia) · Library (Guides · Card decks · Game catalog)
- **Community:** Chat bot (Commands · Variables · Chaos events · Engagement) · Events (Tournaments · Ticket sales) · People (Moderation · Staff & roles)
- **Money:** Pricing · Token economy (Overview · Streamers · History · Levers · Rules). Streamers/History extras come from `/api/admin/economy/dashboard` (`src/lib/economy/policy/dashboard.ts`); `/staff/economy` now redirects to Token economy. Token flows (earned, awarded, burned, bet) come from `gs_token_type_totals` (`supabase/economy-type-totals-m1.sql`): PostgREST aggregates like `amount.sum()` are **off** on our projects (PGRST123), so never use them; until that function exists `snapshot.ts` adds the rows up itself.
- **Admin kit (phase 2 design pass, `src/components/account/admin/AdminKit.tsx`, styles at the end of `account.css`):** every admin screen is one `account-card admin-screen` built from `AdminHeader` (title as the page's h1, one plain sentence, actions on the right), `AdminStats` (CDS StatCards, 2 across on phones and 4 or 3 on desktop via a container query; numbers line up), `AdminSection` (h2, optional icon tile), `AdminPanel` (a bordered block instead of a card inside a card), `AdminTable` (full-width CDS Table in a keyboard-focusable scroller, first column pinned on phones), `AdminRowMenu` (CDS Dropdown "more" menu for Archive/Retire/Remove, still confirmed) and `AdminUpdated`. Modal/Drawer editors, EmptyState with a next step, long lists paged (25), plain words (no table names, file paths or spec numbers). Every view uses it (Overview, Shop, Originals 2026-10-09; Chat bot, Events, People, Library, Pricing and Token economy 2026-10-10). `.admin-table` is `position: relative` so a hidden `.sr-only` column header can't widen the page on phones. Pricing edits prices in dollars and fees in percent + dollars (stored as cents / basis points); Stripe ids only show inside the price window. Health's People row counts accounts by `users.last_seen_at`; its DAU/WAU/MAU (token activity) are labelled Chat viewers. Shop ▸ Orders reads `ordersSummary()` (`?summary=1`). CDS `Select` treats `""` as no choice and hides its label (shows "Select an option…"): import `Select` from `@/components/ui/Select` (a thin wrapper that carries `""` options on a stand-in, so "Any" / "All games" show) instead of from CDS whenever an option's value is `""`. Headless screenshots: CDS charts draw blank in `captureBeyondViewport` shots; use a tall viewport.

- OAuth profile sync: auth callback syncs display name, username, avatar from Discord/Twitch into `users` table
- Avatar updates broadcast via `profile-updated` window event for navbar refresh
- Operational role (`users.role`) is **separate** from subscription tier (`users.subscription_tier`); always call `effectiveTier({ tier, role })` before gating. Role audit lives in `gs_role_audit_log`.

### Supabase
- Client: `src/lib/supabase/client.ts` (browser)
- Server: `src/lib/supabase/server.ts` (server components, App Router cookie shim)
- Admin: `src/lib/supabase/admin.ts` (`createServiceClient()` — server-only, bypasses RLS)
- Schema migrations: actively applied migrations live in `supabase/*.sql`; historical migrations archived in `supabase/archive/`. Both are gitignored from runtime — apply manually in the Supabase SQL editor
- **`npm run migrations:check`** walks every `supabase/*.sql` and confirms each table, view and column it creates exists in the live database (the `.env.local` project by default; set `SUPABASE_URL` + `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` to target another). Exits 1 on anything missing. Run it before a release: the hand-kept `PENDING-MIGRATIONS.md` drifts, and two migrations once went unapplied and untracked for weeks
- RLS policies on all tables — see "Database / RLS" above for the three lint hygiene rules
- **Key tables:**
  - **Core:** `users`, `saved_configs`
  - **Tournaments:** `tournaments`, `tournament_participants`, `tournament_results`
  - **Lounge (legacy competitive):** `lounge_sessions`, `lounge_players`, `lounge_races`, `lounge_placements`
  - **Game data:** `game_competitive_configs`, `game_tracks`, `game_characters`
  - **Sessions (Spec 02 generic):** `gs_sessions`, `session_participants`, `session_events`, `session_picks_bans_drafts`, `session_picks_bans_rounds`, `session_picks_bans_ballots`, `session_modules`, `session_module_config`, `session_schedules`
  - **Twitch integration:** `twitch_connections`, `twitch_sessions` (legacy), `twitch_webhook_events_processed`, `twitch_session_participants`, `twitch_session_shuffles`, `twitch_randomizer_configs`
  - **Discord integration:** `discord_integrations`, `discord_randomizer_sessions`, `discord_prequeue_*`
  - **Mods:** `mod_invitations`, `mod_permissions` (per the mod accounts spec)
  - **Companion (TCG):** `companion_sessions`, `companion_save_states`
  - **Walk-Up Anthems:** `gs_anthem_tracks` (provider catalog), `gs_user_anthems` (personal anthem), `gs_channel_anthem_policy` (streamer channel policy), `gs_anthem_plays` (play log + cooldown)
  - **Polls (GS Pro):** `gs_polls` (question + options jsonb + status), `gs_poll_votes` (one row per identity; partial unique indexes on `(poll,gs_identity_id)` + `(poll,anon_session_id)`) — see the Polling section below
  - **QOTD:** `gs_qotd_history` (unified no-repeat rotation, one claim per community per local day), `gs_qotd_discord_posts` (Discord per-day dedup)
  - **Token economy:** `token_events` (the ledger), `gs_identity`, `gs_account`, `gs_communities`, `gs_streams`, `gs_economy_config`, `gs_streamer_allowance`, `gs_markets`, `gs_market_outcomes`, `gs_bets`, `gs_market_predictions`, `gs_market_templates`, `gs_game_variable_map`, `gs_picks_bans_*`
  - **Email + DSAR:** `email_subscriptions`, `dsar_requests`
  - **Trust & Safety:** `reports`, `user_blocks`, `moderation_appeals`, `moderation_audit_log`, plus `users.moderation_status`/`moderation_until`
  - **Social:** `follows`, `notifications`, `invitations`, `conversations` (kind/scope) + `conversation_members`, `messages`, plus `users.last_seen_at` / `top_friends` / identity fields (`bio`, `pronouns`, `location`, `socials`, `favorite_games`, `profile_banner_url`, `profile_banner_source_url`, `profile_theme`)
  - **Admin / audit:** `gs_role_audit_log`

### CSS

**The root font-size is `10px`.** CDS font tokens are therefore pixel-named:
`--font-size-14` is `1.4rem`. Write a bare `0.74rem` and you get **7.4px**, not
the ~12px it reads as. Always size type with the tokens. Two live consequences
were fixed on 2026-09-26: bingo squares rendering at 7.4px, and the venue
display boards laid out in a 16px dialect (including `max-width: 80rem` = 800px
on a 1920 projector). **Exception:** `rem` inside a `@media` query always
resolves against 16px regardless of the root, so breakpoints are correct as
written and must not be scaled.

- `src/app/globals.css` — global overrides (theme tokens, navbar, auth, account, modals, footer, cookie banner, beta banner, feedback CTA, CDS-component force-light overrides for marketing pages, plus two CDS bug overrides pending an upstream fix: `.empac-marketing-footer__bottom-links` needs `flex-wrap` and `.empac-carousel__dot` needs a 24px hit area)
- `src/styles/randomizer.css` — randomizer-specific styles
- `src/styles/competitive.css` — competitive hub + lounge + tournament styles + verified badge
- `src/styles/companion.css` — TCG companion styles
- `src/styles/stream.css` — stream overlay styles
- `src/styles/overlay.css` — Twitch broadcaster combo overlay styles

### Image Paths
- Game data JSON uses `/files/images/...` paths
- `getImagePath()` in `src/lib/images.ts` transforms to `/images/...`
- `IMAGE_BASE_PATH` constant for future CDN migration
- Platform icons (Discord, Twitch, PSN, NSO, Xbox, Steam, Epic) at `public/images/icons/`

### Analytics
- Plausible: cookieless, always loaded. **Every custom event name lives in `src/lib/analytics/events.ts`** (`EVENTS`): send with `track(EVENTS.x, props)` from client code (or the older `useAnalytics` hook), or tag a plain link with `className={tagged(EVENTS.x, { from })}` (the loaded script is the tagged-events build, so no JS). New names follow "Thing Verbed" with short props, never personal data; older names (Randomize Karts, Party Setup Rolled, Tool Used…) stay as they are so their history doesn't split. **Plausible only charts an event once it's added as a goal in the dashboard**, so a new name means a new goal.
- Covered (Oct 2026 audit): randomizer rolls + extras (Details, slot chosen, run ticks, GoldenEye refresh, copy), the Originals (Daily, Weekly, Chat Brain by source), GS Pro funnel (upgrade clicked by place, checkout started/completed/canceled, waitlist, portal), live nights (started by format + source, joined, ended, game finished, recap, TV), game nights (created, RSVP, series), beta/contact, account link/unlink, every free + companion tool (`Tool Used` {tool}), /live viewer actions (poll, bingo, draft), TCG (card collected, deck viewed), randomizer card clicks, and the AI features.
- **Video short addresses** (2026-10-18): `VIDEO_LINKS` in `next.config.ts` (gameshuffle.co/dex, /shuffle, /quiz (Fire Round), /top5, /wheel, /jackbox, /mkw, /ow, /tiers, /8ball, /tod, /tcg, /jamboree, /superstars, /smash, /nuzlocke) 307 to the page with `src=video-<code>` (lead capture: Plausible "Lead" {source}, carried to the signup event) and `utm_source=video&utm_campaign=<code>` (Vercel + Plausible sources). The social video stages show them (`DailySocialStage`, `WinnersStage`, the clip pills); captions link `https://gameshuffle.co/<code>`. A new video topic = a `VIDEO_LINKS` entry.
- Google Analytics: loaded conditionally via `CookieConsent` component (only on user accept)

### AI features (Claude)
- All go through `src/lib/ai/claude.ts` (`draftStructured`, structured output, `claude-opus-5-5`). Rules: AI drafts and a person approves; AI never decides a random result (randomizers still roll); everything passes the word filter; we say when text was written by AI.
- **Access + allowance** (`src/lib/ai/access.ts` `aiAccess(feature)`, `src/lib/ai/usage.ts`, client-safe `src/lib/ai/features.ts` `AI_FEATURES` = which features are free to try; table `ai_usage` from `supabase/ai-usage-m1.sql` + `-m2.sql` (token columns + levers), storing only who/feature/when/tokens): GS Pro gets `ai_pro_per_30d` (60) generations per rolling 30 days across every feature, free accounts `ai_free_per_day` (3) per Pacific day (reset at midnight Pacific) on the free features (setup, plan, tournament), packs + recaps stay Pro, staff/admin unlimited. Both numbers are pricing levers (`aiLimits()`, fallbacks if the row is missing), edited on **Platform ▸ AI usage**. Each route wraps its generation in `withAiTokens` (`src/lib/ai/tokens.ts`, AsyncLocalStorage; `draftStructured` adds each call's usage) and `recordAiUse` stores the tokens. If the table is missing the allowance isn't enforced (logged, not blocking). Error copy in `src/components/ai/errors.ts`.
- **Client gate:** `GET /api/ai/access?feature=` → `useAiAccess(feature)` (shared per page, `spent(remaining)` after a call) → `aiBlock` (signin / pro / daily / allowance) → `AiGatePrompt` (CDS Modal: signed out = make a free account first, or GS Pro for Pro tools; event `AI Gate Shown`). `allowanceText` is the "2 of 3 free tries left today" line. Signed-out visitors see `AiSetupBar` as a button, not an input. Every AI component (`AiSetupBar`, `AiPackModal`, `AiRecapButton`, `AiTournamentHelper`, `NightPlanner`) uses it; a new AI tool should too.
- **Platform ▸ AI usage** (`PlatformAiTab`, `/api/admin/ai`, `src/lib/ai/adminUsage.ts`): 24h/7d/30d generations + accounts, tokens, a stacked daily chart by tool, by tool, by plan, top accounts (with At limit), and the two limits (POST sets both levers, audited).
- **Deck cards** (GS Pro, AI feature `deck`, 2026-10-10): `POST /api/ai/deck` { family, kind: chance|mission|rule, theme?, avoid? }, `src/lib/ai/decks.ts` (cards must stick to choices players make in the game, never change coins/Stars/stocks by hand; every card passes the word filter and `validateCard`), `AiDeckModal` (kind + theme → tick → "Add N as drafts" through the normal create path; drafts still need Publish with the family-safe question). Opened from the deck editor's **Make cards with AI** on My Stuff ▸ Decks.
- **Content packs** (GS Pro): `POST /api/ai/pack`, `src/lib/ai/packs.ts` (`PACK_SPECS`: wheel, bingo, tierlist, mostlikely, oddoneout, with counts + length limits), `AiPackModal` (theme → checklist → "use"). On the Wheels tab, Tool looks bingo prompts, the tier list maker, Most Likely To and Odd One Out (a one-off pack; `dealPrompt`/`dealRound` accept a pack object).
- **Recaps** (GS Pro): `POST /api/ai/recap` (`night` by code for the host once ended, from `recapText`; `stream` by sessionId for the owner, from `streamFacts`), `AiRecapButton` → Discord post + short post, editable, copy adds the link. On a live night's recap and the hub session header once ended.
- **Plain-language setup** (free 3/day): `POST /api/ai/setup`, `src/lib/ai/setup.ts` (`SETUP_GAMES`: one zod schema + brief per randomizer; nullable = leave as is), `AiSetupBar` in the intro card. Live on GoldenEye and Pokémon Stadium (requests like "a rain team" fill that player's slots as Your choice, species validated). Add a randomizer = a schema entry + an `applySetup` in its component.
- **Night Planner** (free 3/day): `/game-nights/tools/night-planner` (`NightPlanner`, `POST /api/ai/plan`, `src/lib/ai/planner.ts`): roster size, time, owned console games, vibe → a lineup of `NIGHT_GAMES` (validated) + a Jackbox pick; reorder/drop, start as a live night.
- **Tournament helper** (free 3/day while Circuit is in preview): `AiTournamentHelper` on `/tournament/create`, `POST /api/ai/tournament`, `src/lib/ai/tournament.ts`: format suggestion + description, rules and announcement in the organizer's voice with [placeholders].
- Later: live "caster" lines on the overlay (needs tone guardrails).

### Facebook Instant Game: Daily, Dex and Chat Brain (built 2026-10-18)
- Picks https://claude.ai/artifact/P1nLRnQraMgvSavH3c4Jgk (all recommended): today's Chat Brain question to answer + today's board to play + a picture card to share into Messenger chats or the feed; streaks in Facebook's player storage; no ads; listed as "GameShuffle". Instant Games aren't playable inside Messenger (since 2019): Messenger carries the shares.
- **The dailies in the game** (picks https://claude.ai/artifact/VuVfmLAmw6DXapsC3s3eRZ): three CDS Tabs, **Daily** (the Daily Shuffle, opens first), **Dex** (the Daily Dex) and **Chat Brain**; a shared card opens on its own game (`data.game` on `shareAsync`, read with `getEntryPointData`; `?game=dex` locally). Same day's puzzle as the site, played in the game from the site's own rules (`src/lib/originals/daily.ts` / `dailyDex.ts`, bundled): Start button, the CDS Combobox, the guess grid (sideways scroll, name column pinned), the clue ladder, the Dex's "N still fit" list with Show all, and the last chance as first letter + length for both games. **Names only, never art** (a letter tile per guess), Britton's call; all three went into the first Meta submission together (his call), so a Meta objection to character names holds up Chat Brain too. Guesses, clock and per-game streaks live in Facebook storage (`save.daily`, `save.streaks`). Share card `drawDailyCard` (squares, streak, time, never the answer; Shuffle violet, Dex warm). **Leaderboard by nickname** (his call): the game makes a guest secret (`save.guest`, the site's guest id format) and `/api/instant/daily` (GET standing, POST `start` / `join` / `result`) runs the site's guest board code (`markStart`, `joinBoards`, `saveGuestResult` re-judges, `boardView`); no Turnstile inside Facebook, so it leans on the 3-new-names-per-network rule, the word filter and rate limits. A finished game is sent automatically once the player is on the board; the first join sends it then (so day one's server time includes typing the nickname). Shared CORS helpers in `src/lib/instant/cors.ts`. Test scripts (scratchpad-only) played both on dev: win, join, auto-send on a loss, still-fit narrowing, reload restoring the save.
- **Game:** `instant-game/` (React + CDS, bundled by `scripts/build-instant-game.ts` with esbuild → `instant-game/gameshuffle-instant.zip`, gitignored; `--dev` builds `instant-game/dist` against localhost:3000, serve it with `python3 -m http.server 8787 -d instant-game/dist`). `src/host.ts` = the Instant Games SDK 8.0 on *.fbsbx.com (initializeAsync, startGameAsync, player.get/setDataAsync, shareAsync, getEntryPointData) or a localStorage stand-in elsewhere; it keeps a random `anon` id, the streaks, the guest secret and the last 7 days. `src/main.tsx` = the tabs, `src/daily.tsx` = both dailies, `src/brain.tsx` = Chat Brain, `src/card.ts` draws the 1200x630 share cards. Britton uploads the zip in the Meta App Dashboard ▸ Instant Games ▸ Web Hosting (Zero Permissions on so it can call us).
- **Getting it into Facebook (2026-10-18, guide https://claude.ai/artifact/CAc8Au8C9k7xpxXvut4Wsh):** `npm run instant:kit` builds the zip and opens `instant-game/facebook-kit/` (gitignored) in Finder: numbered folders in the App Dashboard's order (zip + `comment.txt` with the day and commit, Settings ▸ Basic app icon, game artwork, screenshots, gameplay video), `LISTING.md` (every field's text: settings, description, keywords, reviewer notes, Approved Partner Program answers) and a `Setup guide.webloc`. `npm run instant:upload` sends the zip through Meta's two-step upload API (`/{app-id}/uploads` then `rupload.facebook.com/{gg_graph_api|fb_game_bundle}/upload:…`, v24.0) with `FB_INSTANT_APP_ID` + `FB_INSTANT_UPLOAD_TOKEN` (Web Hosting ▸ Get Asset Upload Access Token, may expire) from `.env.local`; staging and production stay in the dashboard. Listing source in `instant-game/listing/`: `art/` from `npm run instant:art` (`scripts/render-instant-game-art.tsx`: the GS badge rebuilt full-bleed from the logo SVG's G and arrowed S, 800 icon + 1024 app icon, covers 1200x630 / 1080x1080, banner 1200x400, plus `art/older-sizes/` for Meta's older Details page: 16px icon, 800x150 cover, 1200x627 banner, 750x1334 splash; no third-party names or art), `screenshots/` (1080x1920, filmed from the dev build) and `gameplay.mp4` + `gameplay-landscape.mp4` / `gameplay-square.mp4` (the portrait video centred on brand blue with ffmpeg, for the older page's 16:9 or 1:1 ask; all gitignored, 28s, filmed with the scratch recorder rec3.cjs against a dev build served on :8787). `fbapp-config.json` must match the bundle config reference Meta's uploader validates against (docs/games/build/instant-games/reference/bundle-config): `platform_version` "RICH_GAMEPLAY", `orientation` "PORTRAIT", and the required `navigation_menu_version` "NAV_BAR" (an upload without it is refused). An upload with the newer guide's values ("8.0", lowercase "portrait", no menu setting) was refused with "Invalid value found" on 2026-10-18. Meta requires the **Approved Partner Program** (1 to 4 weeks, 180-day wait after a rejection) before any game goes live, then the quality review (3 to 10 business days). Layout rule learned filming it: grid children need `min-width: 0` and the guess table's scroller `position: relative`, or the table's `.sr-only` text widens the page and phones zoom out.
- **Server:** `/api/instant/brain` (`src/lib/chatbrain/instant.ts`): GET `?anon=` = today's question (open family-safe public prompts, picked by the Pacific day from a fixed id order) + today's board (claimed once a day in `brain_daily`, a published family-safe board via `brainPool()`, no repeat within 30 days) without its answers, or launch progress when there's no board; POST `answer` (filed as a signed-out answer under the hashed anon id, source `facebook`, country from the connection), `guess` (`matchGuess` on the server), `reveal`. No Facebook IDs, names or photos ever reach us (Meta's Zero Permissions rule). CORS: the route sets its own headers for `*.fbsbx.com` (and localhost outside production); `next.config.ts` keeps it out of the site-wide gameshuffle.co-only API rule (`/api/:path((?!instant/).*)`).

### Discord Bot
- HTTP-based Interactions API (no WebSocket gateway — serverless compatible)
- Interactions endpoint: `/api/discord/interactions` (Node.js runtime, signature verified via `discord-interactions`)
- Commands: `/gs-randomize` (kart randomizer with user tagging, per-player re-rolls — supports MK8DX + MKWorld), `/gs-result` (post lounge results)
- Game registry pattern: `GAMES` map in randomize.ts — each game defines data, title, URL, slot visibility
- Reuses pure randomizer logic from `src/lib/randomizer.ts` — no React deps
- Session state in `discord_randomizer_sessions` table (combos, re-roll counts, tagged users)
- Per-player re-roll: only the tagged user or invoker can re-roll a slot, limit configurable (0-5)
- "Open in GameShuffle" deep link: encodes combos as base64url in `?d=` param, hydrated by RandomizerClient
- Command registration: `npx tsx scripts/register-discord-commands.ts`
- **`discord-worker/` — a second deployed service, on Railway, not Vercel.** The
  Vercel app is HTTP-interactions only, so it cannot receive Gateway events.
  This always-on Node service holds the WebSocket for emoji reaction roles,
  join autorole and server logging. Root dir `discord-worker`, start `npm start`,
  deploys from GitHub on push to `main`, env `DISCORD_BOT_TOKEN` / `SUPABASE_URL`
  / `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` (service role, so the browser-role column
  revokes do not affect it). It requires the **Server Members** and **Message
  Content** privileged intents; without them Discord refuses the connection and
  the service crash-loops. Failures now print one `[worker] FATAL (...)` line.
- Env vars: `DISCORD_APPLICATION_ID`, `DISCORD_PUBLIC_KEY`, `DISCORD_BOT_TOKEN`
- Lib structure: `src/lib/discord/` — verify.ts, handler.ts, respond.ts, user.ts, commands/randomize.ts, commands/result.ts
- Account linking: `resolveDiscordUser()` in `src/lib/discord/user.ts` checks Discord→GS link + tier
- Feature gating: `/gs-result` requires Creator+ tier, `/gs-randomize` is free for all
- Cron: daily cleanup of sessions older than 24h via Supabase pg_cron
- Session save uses `next/server after()` to run after response (avoids Discord 3s timeout)
- **Several servers per account (built 2026-10-07, `supabase/discord-multi-server-m1.sql`):** `discord_guilds` holds one row per server (server id = key, so one account per server) with its settings (default channel, ping role, event posts/pings, QOTD hour, Pro role, logging). Free 1 server, GS Pro 5 (`DISCORD_SERVER_LIMITS`). The main server (first connected, `is_main`, changeable) keeps working without Pro; the others pause (computed, `guildIsActive`). `removed_at` = the bot left the server (worker GuildDelete; GuildCreate clears it). Module `src/lib/discord/guilds.ts` (`listGuilds`, `activeGuilds`, `pickGuild`, `guildContext`, `addGuild` with the limit, `updateGuild`, `setMainGuild`, `removeGuild`, `teardownDiscordServers` on account deletion). Channel routes and AutoMod are keyed by server; scheduled posts name a server (null = all); QOTD claims are per server per day (one question per account, each server at its own hour, Pro only); a live session keeps one message per server (`gs_sessions.discord_live_messages`). Account-wide posts (go-live, rounds, recaps, QOTD, Chat Brain, Weekly, game nights, room codes) go to every active server with a channel for them (`resolveRoutings` in `src/lib/adapters/discord/index.ts`; category routes apply only while the owner has Pro). Dashboard: every bot API takes `?guild=` (`botServer()` in `src/lib/discord/botServer.ts`); the Discord Bot tab and the Integrations card have a server picker, "Add another server" and "N of 5". `users.discord_guild_id/_name` still mirror the main server for one release; the other `users.discord_*` server columns are legacy (drop them in a later migration, with the column-privacy grants re-run).
- **Go-live alerts (GS Pro, 2026-10-10, `supabase/discord-live-alerts-m1.sql`):** per server, up to 25 creators (someone you follow on GameShuffle with Twitch linked, or any Twitch channel by name or link), a channel (`discord_guilds.live_alert_channel_id`) and an optional role ping (`live_alert_role_id`); each creator can have its own message with {name} {game} {title} {link}. Module `src/lib/discord/liveAlerts.ts`, API `/api/discord/bot/live-alerts?guild=`, UI `DiscordLiveAlerts` under Channel routing. Twitch lets the app token subscribe to stream.online for anyone: connected streamers already have it, everyone else gets one `twitch_watched_channels` row (`watchStreamOnline` in eventsub.ts; a 409 = exists; removed with the last alert for them; status follows verification/revocation via `recordSubscriptionStatus`). The stream.online webhook calls `postLiveAlerts` for every broadcaster before the connected-streamer check: one post per stream per server (`last_stream_id` claim), only while the owner has Pro, embed with title, game and the stream thumbnail. On dev the callback is localhost, so watches record `failed` (Twitch needs https); test the post path with a signed fake stream.online to the local webhook.

### Discord Activity (GameShuffle inside Discord, live 2026-10-07; production app verified)
- Like Wordle in Discord: launched from the App Launcher (the `/launch` entry point lives there, not in the `/` menu), `/gs-daily` or a Play button, it runs the site's own Daily, Weekly and Chat Brain components in one page (`src/components/activity/ActivityApp.tsx`, route `src/app/discord/activity/page.tsx`). Research + rules: `specs/gs-discord-activity-research.md` (local).
- **Host:** Discord's URL mapping `/` → `activity.gameshuffle.co` for the production app, `activity-dev.gameshuffle.co` for the dev app (Vercel domain on the `dev` branch, like dev.gameshuffle.co; Namecheap CNAMEs to Vercel). Each deployment signs in with its own environment's `DISCORD_*` (preview = the dev app). `src/middleware.ts` rewrites that host's `/` to `/discord/activity` and redirects any other page path to www; the root layout renders `isActivityPath` (`src/lib/activity/hosts.ts`) bare (dark, Toast + Confirm only: no chrome, AuthProvider, analytics or staff tools). `next.config.ts` scopes headers by host: the Activity host gets `frame-ancestors` Discord + discordsays and no X-Frame-Options; every other host keeps SAMEORIGIN. In development `*.trycloudflare.com` counts as the Activity host and `allowedDevOrigins` lets the tunnel in.
- **Never navigate inside the Activity:** one SDK handshake per frame, so a reload or redirect breaks it. Tabs switch games; links go through `OriginalsLink` → `openExternalLink`.
- **Only our origin loads inside Discord:** Discord's proxy blocks every other host (the Mario Party art on cdn.empac.co broke on 2026-10-07). Image URLs go through `useArtSrc()` (OriginalsHost), which points `https://cdn.empac.co/gameshuffle/images/…` at `/images/cdn/…`, a rewrite in `next.config.ts` (under `/images/` so the middleware passes it). Any new image in an Original must use it; other external hosts need the same treatment or a Discord URL mapping.
- **Sign-in:** SDK `authorize` (identify, prompt none) → `POST /api/activity/token` (`src/lib/activity/discord.ts` exchanges the code with the client secret and reads `/users/@me`) → our signed session (`src/lib/activity/session.ts`, HMAC with `ACTIVITY_SESSION_SECRET` or a key derived from the service role key, 12h) sent as `Bearer` to `/api/activity/*`. `DISCORD_ACTIVITY_APP=dev` uses the dev Discord app (DEV_DISCORD_*).
- **Same components, swapped data:** `OriginalsHost` (`src/components/originals/OriginalsHost.tsx`) gives DailyShuffle, WeeklyChallenge, ChatBrainAsk and the audience hook an `api()` and an `activity` handle. On the site it's plain fetch; the Activity maps `/api/daily|weekly|chat-brain` to `/api/activity/daily|weekly|brain` with the session. New Original UI should call `useOriginalsHost().api` instead of `fetch` and link with `OriginalsLink`.
- **Data:** Daily results for Discord players live in `daily_identity_results` (identity per day, with the guesses so far so a game resumes; `supabase/discord-activity-m1.sql`), no account needed. `src/lib/daily/results.ts` merges them with `daily_results` for anyone linked (`users.discord_id`): account row wins for a day, first finished result stands, everything re-judged. `/api/daily` and `/api/weekly` now use `dailyView`/`saveAccountResult` and `weeklyView`/`saveWeeklyPlay`. Weekly plays still need a linked account (weekly_entries is per account); Chat Brain answers go to the Discord identity with source `activity`. Sharing uses SDK `shareLink` (custom_id `daily`).
- **Signing up from the Activity:** unlinked players get a one-time welcome card (CDS Modal: what a free account adds, "Sign up free with Discord" / "Play without an account"; seen-flag in the iframe's localStorage) and a "Join free" button in the band. Both open `gameshuffle.co/discord/join?src=discord-activity` in the browser (one rail: reasons, then "Continue with Discord", or "Connect Discord" when already signed in; consent line as on /signup). OAuth returns through `/auth/callback?redirect=/discord/joined` (both paths allowlisted); new Discord-only accounts set a password first (site rule). Meanwhile the Activity polls `POST /api/activity/refresh` every 5s and on refocus for up to 10 minutes and swaps in a session naming the new account (toast; Weekly and the profile streak work without relaunching). Inside the Activity, `OriginalsLink` turns any /login or /signup link into this flow (`activity.signUp`). `/signup/set-password` now only follows same-site `return_to` paths.
- **Play buttons and `/gs-daily`:** `src/lib/discord/activityLaunch.ts`: `/gs-daily`, the Weekly card's Play and Chat Brain's "Answer more" (`brainplay`) answer LAUNCH_ACTIVITY (type 12) after noting the game in `discord_activity_intents` (read once by the token route). On unless `DISCORD_ACTIVITY_LIVE=0` (then buttons keep their in-chat forms and `/gs-daily` links to /daily). The dev app is unverified, so there it opens only for the team and App Testers in servers under 25 members.
- **Results in the channel (Wordle-style):** the token route sends the SDK's `instanceId` to Discord's activity-instances API (`verifiedChannel`, bot token) and puts the confirmed server channel in the session (`cid`/`gid`; never from the page, DMs have none). Activity saves record it on `daily_identity_results.channel_id` and, after the response, `updateDailyCard` (`src/lib/activity/channelCard.ts`) keeps one bot message per channel per Pacific day (`discord_daily_cards`, `supabase/discord-activity-m2.sql`). **Drawn cards (2026-10-10, mockup https://claude.ai/artifact/EhssJ55Z9PGHemUqTMknxb):** the message is a Components V2 container (flag 1<<15: media gallery with the picture as an attachment, a text line, the buttons; a text-only container if drawing fails, and a pre-V2 card that can't be edited is replaced). `src/lib/discord/cards.tsx` draws it with next/og at 2x: navy + the Originals glyph field, each player's face (ring: green solved, amber playing, grey out), squares only, a score word ("Psychic"…"Phew", "Better luck tomorrow"; "Tomorrow" draws with a gap in this font), a flame for a 3-day streak, up to 12 faces; every text line has a fixed width and ends in "…" (the renderer only adds it to a fixed-width line, and flex centring breaks it). Faces: `src/lib/discord/faces.ts` (`gs_identities.avatar_url` from `supabase/discord-cards-m1.sql`, saved at Activity sign-in; a linked account shows its site avatar; initials otherwise; only Discord/Twitch CDNs and our storage are fetched). The morning post is a podium (`renderPodium`, top 3 + the channel's streak). /discord's `DiscordMock` shows pictures from `scripts/render-discord-card-examples.ts` (rerun after changing the look). Each card has a green "Play the Daily" button (`activity:daily` → `launchActivity`, LAUNCH_ACTIVITY). First post is claimed (`message_id` null → "pending") so it can't double-post; a deleted card is re-posted; a channel the bot can't post in (403/404) is `skipped` for the day. `/api/cron/discord-daily-summary` (9am Pacific: 16:05 and 17:05 UTC, guarded) posts yesterday's ranking and the channel's solve streak with "Play today's Daily" and "Weekly" buttons, claimed via `summary_posted_at`. Needs the bot in that server.
- **Marketing page:** `/discord` (see Route Structure). `DiscordMock` (`src/components/discord/DiscordMock.tsx`) draws the day's card and the morning summary as Discord would show them (illustration, fixed Discord colours); keep its wording in step with `channelCard.ts`. Share image `src/app/discord/opengraph-image.jpg` + `twitter-image.jpg` (also covers /discord/join); the page restates openGraph whole (a page's openGraph replaces the root one) and sets a Twitter card. The cover band (`CoverMarquee`, custom: CDS Carousel has no marquee) shows Live now (CHAT_GAMES) and On our list (catalog candidates, ideas not promises) with a "Suggest it" link to `/ideas/submit?category=randomizer` (the form preselects `?category=`). Linked from the Community menu + footer, the Stream pillar page, `DiscordPlug` under the Daily/Weekly/home Play today, the /for-streamers toolkit, the GS Pro Discord card and the Discord Bot tab. Events `Discord Add Clicked` / `Discord Play Clicked` / `Discord Link Clicked` {from}.
- **Who answered, on the bot's posts:** `src/lib/discord/promptPosts.ts` + `supabase/discord-prompt-posts-m1.sql`. Chat Brain and Weekly posts the bot makes (both crons, plus manager `/gs-brain` and `/gs-weekly`, recorded after the reply via the interaction webhook) are kept in `discord_prompt_posts`; answers from that server (the post's buttons, the Weekly form, the tier lock, the Activity with a confirmed `gid`) go in `discord_prompt_answerers` and every post of that topic there is edited with a "✅ 12 answered here: Sam, Riley… and 9 more" field (names only). Topic = `prompt:<id>:<edition>` (the Weekly's survey uses its prompt's topic, so one answer shows on both) or `week:<date>` for a Tier War; person = `u:<account>` when linked, else `i:<identity>`. Missing tables are skipped with one warning.
- **Commands:** `scripts/register-discord-commands.ts` reads the app's commands first and keeps its type-4 entry point (a bulk update without it fails with 50240); `--activity` adds `/launch` (handler 2) if missing.
- **Brand:** the site's light look with the navy aurora band from the site's heroes across the top (white logo, player pill), and every navy surface (band, sign-in/error/outside cards, corner window) carries the site's drifting Tabler glyph field (`IconField` with the `originals` art category: puzzle, calendar-week, brain, trophy, sparkles). The corner window adds the open game's glyph on a blue tile. Launcher art (`scripts/render-discord-activity-art.cjs` → `public/images/discord/`) uses the same five glyphs, veiled behind the type; the root layout renders the Activity `data-theme="light"`. Signing-in / error / outside-Discord cards sit on the band with the colour logo.
- **Trivia tab (built 2026-10-11, no migration):** `ActivityTrivia` (`src/components/activity/ActivityTrivia.tsx`, `/api/activity/trivia`, `src/lib/activity/trivia.ts`). When the server's owner (`discord_guilds` by the session's confirmed `gid`) is running a trivia night on stream, players answer it here as the same player Discord's buttons use (`viewerForDiscord`), scored with chat and /live (`streamView`, `answerFromWeb`); polls every 2s while live, 10s otherwise, only while the tab is open. Otherwise a **practice round**: five approved pack questions (no pictures, one per category first, `practiceSet`), each checked on the server (`checkPractice`; the answer is only sent back after an answer), nothing scored or saved; "Play another round" skips the last 60 seen. Dev run `scripts/.tmp/activity-trivia-live.ts` (Nova's test server).
- **Play together (built 2026-10-12, `supabase/activity-rooms-m1.sql` on dev + prod):** the **Together** tab (`ActivityRoom`, `/api/activity/room`, engine `src/lib/activity/room.ts`, shared rules + view types `src/lib/activity/roomRules.ts`, tests `npx tsx -r ./scripts/server-only-shim.cjs scripts/test-activity-room.ts`). One room per Activity instance: `/api/activity/token` now checks the instance with Discord (`verifiedInstance`: the player must be in it; any call, DMs too) and puts it in the session as `ins` (the results card still only uses a server channel). Players are Discord identities, no account needed; the first in runs the room (crown), a host who stops checking in for 30s hands it to whoever looks in next, and the host can **Hand over**. Nothing runs on a timer: screens poll every 1.5s while the tab is open (that's the check-in; `ACTIVE_MS` 25s), each poll moves the room along (`step`), and every change is a version-guarded update so racing polls land once; scores live in the room state and change in the same write as the reveal. **Quick-fire rounds** reuse break mode's games unchanged (make → accept → judge) for the call: sets in `QUICKFIRE_SETS` (A bit of everything, Know your characters, Trivia, Emoji and words, Roster Rush), a focus for character facts (Mario Kart / Mario Party / Smash), 5/10/15 rounds; intro 3s → open (the game's seconds, closing early once everyone here answered) → reveal 6s with +points → done (podium, Play again / Back to the lobby); no repeats in a room (`seen`). Round numbers only go up in a room (a game remembers its `base`), because answers are keyed by round: resetting them let a new game see the last game's answers and close at once. The round display is `BreakRoundPlay` (`src/components/break/`), now shared with /live's break card. **Roll for the call**: a `CHAT_GAMES` pick for everyone here (unique games keep picks different; newcomers get one), your own reroll, the host's Reroll everyone, and the match setup from `CHAT_SETUPS`. CDN art is pointed at `/images/cdn` server-side. The other tabs peek (`?peek=1`, no check-in) every 6s: the tab reads "Together · N" and a game starting toasts "Join". Tested 2026-10-12 on dev: `scripts/.tmp/room-e2e.ts` (56 checks, three API players), screenshots `scripts/.tmp/room-shots.cjs`, tour https://claude.ai/artifact/AKmg52GrzSkt88dc47wJ8U. Together is the second tab, and below 30rem the tabs drop their icons so all five fit. Dev preview: `/discord/activity?preview=guest&name=Sam&room=test&tab=room` (a guest name is its own player, `room` makes `ins` = `dev-<room>`), several windows for several players. **Party games** (built 2026-10-12, no migration): Most Likely To, Odd One Out and Tier Wars on the live nights' own rules and phone panels (`MostLikelyPanel`, `OddOneOutPanel`, `TierWarsPanel` with `inCall`: call wording, no duplicate standings). `src/lib/activity/roomParty.ts`: everyone in the room gets a seat (`seats`, newcomers play from the next round), host-driven like live nights (deal, everyone answers, reveal), votes/ballots in `activity_room_answers` under the round's room round number and frozen into the round at the reveal, every round (Odd One Out's word and odd seat included) in the room's `secret`, views carry only this player's card and vote. Three people needed; Finish → the shared Done screen. E2E `scripts/.tmp/room-party-e2e.ts` (27 checks). **Bingo** (same engine, slug `number-bingo`, `BingoPanel inCall`): a card per player (only your own in your view), the room calls numbers on a timer (`callSeconds` 6/10/15 or 0 = by hand, host's "Numbers called" picker; `partyTick` in each poll's step), claims checked against the numbers called (`patternHit`), marks kept per room round (`current.scope`), two players needed. Party actions retry on a version clash (the timer's call landing at the same moment as a claim). **Chat Brain boards** (`src/lib/activity/roomBrain.ts`, game `brain`): the call plays published boards like the stream's speed round: first to name a hidden answer takes its points, every find restarts the 20s clock, a timeout is a strike, three strikes or a cleared board ends it, 1/3/5 boards. Public family-safe boards only (newest edition), so the lobby card hides itself until Chat Brain launches (production had none on 2026-10-12); in development community test boards count. Answers and spellings stay in `secret`. E2E `scripts/.tmp/room-brain-e2e.ts` (13). **Daily "who's playing"** (`DailyCallStrip` above the Daily, `/api/activity/daily/call`, `src/lib/activity/callDaily.ts`): everyone in this call (Discord's activity-instances API, cached 10s; dev rooms use the room's players) with today's progress as squares only, an account's website result when they didn't play in the Activity, polled every 15s while the Daily tab is open; hidden when you're alone. E2E `scripts/.tmp/callstrip-e2e.ts` (7).
- **Layout:** compact bar, CDS underline Tabs pinned under Discord's top inset (`--discord-safe-area-inset-*`), nothing in a tab may widen the page, and a logo-plus-game view below 300px tall (Discord's corner window). Inside the Activity the Daily and Weekly show an "Answer a Chat Brain question" button (`activity.showTab`) instead of the Chat Brain card. **Dev preview without Discord:** `localhost:3000/discord/activity?preview=linked|guest&tab=daily|weekly|brain` (signs in through `/api/activity/dev-session`, development only).
- **Testing locally:** `brew install cloudflared`, `DISCORD_ACTIVITY_APP=dev` in `.env.local`, `npm run dev`, `cloudflared tunnel --url http://localhost:3000`, paste the tunnel host into the dev app's URL Mappings (`/`), launch from the App Launcher in a test server. Next: live channel card + daily server summary (Wordle-style), Discord-only Weekly entries, a Daily post with a Play button.

### Twitch Streamer Integration
- Settings live in Streaming ▸ Setup ▸ Twitch (`TwitchHubTab`): connect, EventSub health, the channel points reward and "Show the lobby on your live page" (`ChannelPointsSettings.tsx`, channel-wide, moved out of the session page 2026-10-10). Overlay links + "Make a new link" (regenerate) are on Streaming ▸ Overlay ▸ Layout. `/twitch*` pages only redirect
- Separate streamer-integration OAuth flow at `/api/twitch/auth/start` (not the sign-in flow) — captures the full streamer scope bundle + refresh token, stores AES-256-GCM encrypted via `src/lib/twitch/crypto.ts`
- Webhook endpoint at `/api/twitch/webhook` — HMAC-SHA256 verification against `TWITCH_EVENTSUB_SECRET`, message-id dedupe in `twitch_webhook_events_processed`, routes on subscription type
- EventSub subscriptions created at OAuth time: `channel.update`, `stream.online`, `stream.offline`, `channel.chat.message`. Channel point redemption sub created lazily when the streamer enables channel points
- Sessions — `twitch_sessions` rows opened on `stream.online` or manually on "Start test session"; `randomizer_slug` follows the streamer's current Twitch category via stream.online + channel.update. Category lookup via `src/lib/twitch/categories.ts` tries ID first, falls back to name match and self-heals the seed row
- Bot runs as a shared Twitch account; `TWITCH_BOT_USER_ID` is the bot's numeric Twitch ID. Chat sends use the app access token (the bot grants `user:bot` + `user:read:chat`, broadcaster grants `channel:bot` — that combo lets app-token calls send on the bot's behalf). No separate bot OAuth flow or token refresh needed
- **Command routing fixes (2026-10-10, `scripts/test-command-routing.ts`):** custom commands live in a per-channel table (`resolveCustomCommand`), not the shared registry (two channels with `!socials` used to cross-talk and the second failed to load); `resolveTypedCommand` tries bare two-word commands (`!party join`, `!chance vote`, `!mission done`, `!wheel add x`) before the verb alone; `!a`, `!cb` and `!whosaid` are one-word triggers with aliases (they were two-word paths, so `!cb ask` never routed); the overlay tool commands (`!spin`, `!gs timer`, `!draw`…) let mods through (actor was "host"); a built-in the caller can't use falls through to the reply chain (a viewer's `!commands` gets the list link); the test also checks every command's own usage line routes to it.
- Commands dispatched from `src/lib/twitch/commands/dispatch.ts`:
  - `!gs-shuffle` (broadcaster + participants; cooldown-gated for viewers, broadcaster bypasses)
  - `!gs-join` / `!gs-leave` / `!gs-mycombo` / `!gs-lobby` (viewer lifecycle; 60s rejoin cooldown)
  - `!gs-kick @user [min]` / `!gs-clear` (mods + broadcaster)
  - `!gs-battle` (mods + broadcaster, `src/lib/twitch/commands/battle.ts`): a viewer battle. Rolls everyone in the lobby at once (`taken` keeps picks different in Smash and Mario Party; `ChatGame.battleSetting` adds a Smash stage from the competitive list), writes each roll to the participant row, posts the lineup (split by `lineupMessages`, max 3 messages, then the lobby link) and records one streamer shuffle event whose `ChatRoll.title` turns the overlay card into a lineup (one tile per player, even rows, up to 12)
  - `!gs-help` / bare `!gs` (info)
- Streamer is auto-seated in every session via `ensureBroadcasterInSession()` — can't leave, can't be kicked, can't be cleared
- **Chat rolls cover every game** (2026-10-06): `src/lib/twitch/chatGames.ts` (`CHAT_GAMES`, `getChatGame`) gives each game's roll from its own randomizer (MK8DX/World, MK64, Smash fighter + costume, Mario Party character kept distinct across the lobby, Overwatch/Marvel Rivals hero with an optional role arg `!gs-shuffle tank`, Splatoon kit, Kirby rider + machine, GoldenEye/PD character, Pokémon Stadium = the streamer's team only, cup arg). Lobby caps = the game's online room. Hidden games stay out of chat in production. A roll is stored as `{v: 2, game, slots[], text}` (`src/lib/twitch/chatRoll.ts`, client-safe; `rollSlots`/`rollText` also read old bare kart combos, and MK rolls keep the kart fields). Every surface draws parts with `RollSlotArt` (overlay card, the `/live` Lobby tab, recap, Hub feed). Twitch categories for the other games are in code (`chatGameCategories.ts`, Helix ids); the `twitch_game_categories` table still wins when it has a row. `src/lib/twitch/games.ts` is now only Mario Kart data for picks/bans. Channel-points reward renamed "Reroll the Streamer's Pick" (old title still adopted). **Match rolls:** `!gs setup [option]` (mods + host, `src/lib/twitch/commands/setup.ts`) rolls what everyone plays on from each game's randomizer (`CHAT_SETUPS` in `src/lib/twitch/chatSetups.ts`: MK tracks/rally/battle course, Smash stage + rules, Mario Party board + turns + bonus, hero-shooter map by mode, Splatoon battle/set/Salmon Run, Kirby course/stadium, the GoldenEye/PD match, a Stadium cup), posts it and records a streamer shuffle event whose roll has `kind: "setup"` + `title` (overlay card with a list icon; `rollKind()`; Hub feed + recap say "rolled the setup"). Chat text is `emoji title: part · part`, split at the first ": " (titles never contain one). During the stream.offline grace period the session uses only `active_game` (cleared at offline), so rolls pause.
- Channel points ("Reroll the Streamer's Combo" reward, one per streamer): created + EventSub subscribed via `/api/twitch/channel-points` on enable; redemptions trigger a **broadcaster** shuffle (viewer credited in chat), auto-refund on no-session / unsupported-category. Uses `src/lib/twitch/userToken.ts` for transparent refresh of the broadcaster's user token
- Broadcaster overlay at `/overlay/[token]` — `(stream)` group, OBS browser-source-ready, polls `/api/twitch/overlay/[token]/latest` every 2s, animates combo card for 8s on new broadcaster shuffle
- **The lobby lives on the live page** (2026-10-10): `!gs-lobby` (overflow) and `!gs-battle` link `/live/<slug>?tab=room` via `getLiveUrlForUser`. The old `/lobby/<overlay token>` page put the overlay token in public chat; it now only redirects, and its `/api/twitch/lobby/[token]` route and `twitch-lobby.css` are gone
- Env vars: `TWITCH_CLIENT_ID`, `TWITCH_CLIENT_SECRET`, `TWITCH_EVENTSUB_SECRET`, `TWITCH_ENCRYPTION_KEY` (64-char hex), `TWITCH_BOT_USER_ID`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`
- Lib structure: `src/lib/twitch/` — admin.ts, client.ts, crypto.ts, scopes.ts, eventsub.ts, categories.ts, channelPoints.ts, games.ts, userToken.ts, commands/{parse,dispatch,shuffle,participants,moderation,messages}.ts
- ChromeFree overlay routes: `src/components/layout/ConditionalChrome.tsx` suppresses navbar/footer/cookie banner on `/overlay/*`, `/stream*`, `/stream-card*` — root layout wraps children in it

### Twitch extension (built 2026-10-20, not yet submitted)
- Picks https://claude.ai/artifact/GrYGnPaUHT2yktw3b4XPhu (all recommended): a play-along card as a **video component** plus the **phone view**, a **panel** under the stream, a **config** page; the card runs polls, Stream Bingo, trivia, break games, Chat Brain, Chat Draft and picks and bans (no predictions); viewers are anonymous until their first play, when the card calls Twitch's `requestIdShare`; free to install (the games follow the streamer's plan); invite-only first; control room (Live Config) and Bits later. Setup, every console field, art and the reviewer walkthrough: `twitch-extension/listing/LISTING.md`.
- **Client** `twitch-extension/` (React + CDS, bundled by `scripts/build-twitch-extension.ts` with esbuild like the instant game): `video_component.html` / `mobile.html` (card.js, `src/card.tsx`), `panel.html` (`src/panel.tsx`), `config.html` (`src/config.tsx`), each loading Twitch's helper script first and `data-theme="light"` on `<html>` (without it CDS's raw dark-mode media rules paint inputs and secondary buttons dark on a dark OS). `src/host.ts` = Twitch.ext (only trusted on `*.ext-twitch.tv`, since the helper script defines it anywhere) or a local stand-in signing in through `/api/twitch-ext/dev-token`; the viewer id and role are read from the token itself. `src/api.ts`: one store (state every 2s, the viewer's part every 4s once shared and something runs, nothing while hidden; `failures` drives a "not answering" line) and `liveApi`, which answers the live cards' own site paths from the store and turns their POSTs into plays, so the card renders **the same components /live uses** (LiveBreakCard, LiveTriviaCard, LiveChatBrainCard, LivePollCard, LiveBingoCard, LiveDraftCard through `LiveHostContext`; site paths starting /images, /api or /files are made absolute) plus `PicksCard` (tracks only, saved as you tap, Lock in). `npm run ext:dev` builds `twitch-extension/dist` against localhost:3000 (serve with `python3 -m http.server 8788 -d twitch-extension/dist`, open `/video_component.html?channel=196723`, Nova's dev channel; `&user=<Twitch id>` = already shared, `&role=broadcaster`); `npm run ext:build` makes the zip against www (`--api=https://dev.gameshuffle.co` for a Hosted Test on dev). card.js is ~1MB minified, mostly CascadeDS, which ships as one bundle esbuild can't tree-shake and pulls in @react-google-maps/api (151KB) through its Map component: on the CascadeDS fix list.
- **Server** `/api/twitch-ext/*` (`src/lib/twitch/extension/`: `jwt.ts` HS256 with `TWITCH_EXTENSION_SECRET` (base64), a fixed local secret in development; `context.ts` channel → owner, community, slug, Pro, live (cached 60s); `viewer.ts` the shared Twitch id → the chat `twitch` identity (+ `accountUserId` when that Twitch account is linked); `state.ts` `publicState` (edge-cached 2s), `myState`, `panelState` (30s); `act.ts` plays, returning the same data the site's routes do (tally, card, won game, draft); `http.ts` CORS for `https://<TWITCH_EXTENSION_CLIENT_ID>.ext-twitch.tv` (any ext-twitch.tv host until it's set), localhost outside production). Routes `state` and `panel` (public, `?channel=`), `me` and `act` (Bearer token; `act` refuses anonymous viewers with 403 `share_needed`, 20 plays per 10s). The live cards take `api` and `signIn` from `LiveHost` (`src/components/live/LiveHost.tsx`; on /live `LiveSiteHost` gives fetch + the Twitch sign-in modal). Shared pieces split out for it: `src/lib/chatbrain/live.ts` (`brainLiveView`/`brainLiveAnswer`), `src/lib/picks-bans/ballot.ts` (`saveBallot`, only the fields given), Stream Bingo holders `string | {userId} | {identityId}` (`supabase/stream-bingo-m3.sql`, dev + prod).
- Privacy 2.5 "Playing on Twitch" and Terms 3.5 (extension tokens are Arcade Tokens, never bought with Bits) cover it. Listing art `npx tsx scripts/render-twitch-extension-art.tsx`; screenshots `scripts/.tmp/ext-listing-shots.cjs` with games seeded by `scripts/.tmp/ext-seed.ts` (scratch).

### Subscriptions & Feature Gating
- `src/lib/subscription.ts`: two tiers, `free` and `pro` (legacy member/creator normalize to pro via `normalizeTier`). `role` (staff/admin/beta) is a separate axis: always resolve with `effectiveTier({ tier, role, circuitTier?, circuitStatus? })` (staff, beta and an active Circuit 256 count as Pro). Server checks use `isProUser(userId)` (`src/lib/subscription-server.ts`); the database mirror is `gs_is_pro(uid)` (`supabase/pro-gates-m1.sql`, keep in step). `hasCapability(user, name)` exists, but most capability names are never checked: gate with the Pro check at the place that matters.
- **What's Pro (decided 2026-10-07, `project_free_vs_pro` memory):** Twitch streamer integration (no free taste: the wall offers the trial), Discord server tools, brand themes + profile/community skins + custom CSS (`src/lib/profile/proLooks.ts`: saves need Pro, owner surfaces render them only while the owner has Pro, saved choices are kept), season history beyond this month (`SeasonPicker`, judged on the page owner), unlimited saved setups/tournaments, My Cards past `MY_CARDS_FREE_LIMIT` (250 different cards), AI packs/recaps. Accent, status, links and layout stay free.
- **The trial moment:** every free-account wall uses `ProTrialPrompt` (`src/components/billing`): what's locked, "14 days free, then $9 a month" while the account still has its one trial (`users.has_used_trial`), and `ProUpgradeCtaButtons` (which look eligibility up via `useTrialEligibility` when a page doesn't pass it). `ProOnly` shows children to Pro and a fallback (usually the prompt) to everyone else. Copy only: the save routes enforce the same rule.
- **Who gets offered GS Pro (fixed 2026-10-19, audit https://claude.ai/artifact/FuJ1X3bxR6bozhJxEKPYry):** `/api/stripe/checkout` refuses `already_pro` (409) when `getProAccess` lists a paid source (a Pro subscription or trial, or GS Circuit 256), so nobody is billed twice; staff and beta can still subscribe. `ProUpgradeCtaButtons` shows "You have GS Pro · Manage your plan" to Pro accounts and stays hidden (space kept) until the country is known. The line above the buttons is `ProTerms` (`src/components/billing/ProTerms.tsx`): the trial and price from the price list while the trial is unused, "Upgrade any time" after, nothing outside the US. `usePaidAvailability` makes one lookup per page and treats a failed one as available (checkout still refuses outside the US). `subscriptions` holds Pro and Circuit rows (`product` 'pro' | 'circuit'): read Pro with `.eq("product", "pro")` (Plans & billing and the Account trial banner read Circuit as Pro until this); Plans & billing says "Included with GS Circuit 256" for that plan. /gs-pro's hero and closing band change with the viewer (member, outside the US, trial used). Still to do in the Support build: the marketing rows that say "14 days free" (PlansBand, PlansPanel, tool bands, /live footer, navbar Go Pro) for visitors outside the US and used trials.
- **Circuit launch (built 2026-10-10): the Circuit billing switch is the launch.** Turning on `organizer_billing_enabled` (Platform ▸ Switches) stamps the launch moment (`organizer_billing_enabled_at`), opens Circuit checkout and makes the Terms §3.4 (plans and prices: Circuit 64 $12/$120, Circuit 256 $29/$290 with Pro, Pro add-on $5/$50), /gs-circuit, `CircuitPricing` and the create form read live (`circuitLaunchState()` in `circuit-resolve.ts` for server pages, `/api/tournaments/billing-status` for client ones: `launchedAt`, and per organizer `plan` staff/grace/circuit_64/circuit_256/free + `graceUntil`; the flag PUT revalidates /terms and /gs-circuit). `ORGANIZER_BILLING_LAUNCH` stays as an optional future day to announce ("free until"). **Earlier organizers:** Terms §3.4 promises anyone using Circuit an email 30+ days before any charge, so tournaments created before the launch keep Circuit for good, and anyone who created one keeps every Circuit feature free until the end of the Pacific day `CIRCUIT_LAUNCH_GRACE_DAYS` (30) after the later of the launch and the launch email (`circuitGraceEnd`, `circuitGraceFor`, resolver source `grace`). The email: the Launch email panel under the switch (`CircuitNoticePanel`, `/api/admin/circuit-notice`, `src/lib/tournaments/circuitNotice.ts`): admins only, only once the switch is on, stamps `organizer_circuit_notice_at` on the first send and records who has it in `organizer_circuit_notice_sent` (so pressing again only reaches anyone missed; staff skipped). Paid event entry stays off (the marketplace-facilitator question). **After launch, for organizers:** the manage page asks `/api/tournaments/billing-status?tournament=<id>` (organizers, co-organizers, staff) for that tournament's answer (resolver source preview / grandfathered / grace / override / subscription / free, `playerCap`, `paidFeatures`): the field note says what the plan allows, the Team access / Page branding / Display board tags say included, kept from before launch, free until a date or "GS Circuit plan" (with a See the plans line), and the organizer's own Add player respects the Circuit cap. **Nobody is turned away by a plan (Britton, 2026-10-10): past the Circuit cap people join the waitlist.** `getEventMeta` gives tournaments `circuitCap` (the organizer's finite Circuit limit, else null) and the waitlist engine works to `seatLimit(meta)` = the smaller of the event's own limit and that (`planLimited(meta)` = the plan is the one that's full), so `joinDecision`, `fillOpenSeats`, claims and `myWaitlist` all follow it; the organizer's Offer now / Seat now go past their own limit but return `circuit_cap` at the plan's. Joining the line because of the plan sends the organizer a `tournament_plan_waitlist` notification (once a Pacific day per tournament, with how many are waiting; `noteCircuitWaitlist`, wording by cap via `biggerFieldLine`). The attendee table shows "N people are waiting. Your plan fits 12 players" with **Upgrade to Circuit 64/256** (owner only; Circuit checkout takes `returnTo` = the manage page and the page toasts on return), and a new or upgraded Circuit subscription runs `fillCircuitWaitlists` from the Stripe sync, so the people waiting are offered spots in line order. The public page's full state uses the same limit (`circuitCap` prop from the server page) and `WaitlistCard` says the organizer can make room. Known gap: the database guard only knows the event's own limit, so a hand-made API insert could pass the plan's (the join routes don't). The Plans tab ladder tells an organizer in the grace period how long they have.
- **Circuit** caps and paid features (`src/lib/tournaments/circuit*.ts`) are no-ops while `organizer_billing_enabled` is off: `atCircuitCap` on quick-join/guest-join, `tournamentHasFeature(…, "seeding")` on the seeding draw.
- DB fields on users: `subscription_tier`, `subscription_status`, `stripe_customer_id`, `stripe_subscription_id`, `has_used_trial`, `circuit_tier`, `circuit_status`, `role`, `is_public` (`trial_ends_at` is unused; trial ends live in `subscriptions.trial_end`). All tier checks happen server-side; never trust the client.

### Stripe + Billing
- **Who can buy what (Oct 2026, `specs/monetization-launch-plan.md`):** `src/lib/billing/availability.ts`. Paid plans are **US-only**: `/api/stripe/checkout` refuses non-US visitors by edge IP country (`us_only`, unknown country allowed) and the webhook backstop (`src/lib/billing/backstop.ts`, `checkout.session.completed`) cancels, refunds and emails anyone whose **billing address** is non-US. Non-US visitors get `PaidPlansWaitlist` (MailerLite group "Paid Plans Waitlist (International)", built-in Country field; non-prod only logs) in place of upgrade buttons; `/api/billing/availability` tells the UI. Both checkouts set `automatic_tax`, required billing address and `customer_update`; Circuit adds `tax_id_collection` and **refuses entirely while `organizer_billing_enabled` is off** (Circuit is free during preview). **Paid event entry is off** behind platform flag `paid_tickets_enabled` (missing row = off): paid tiers, paid ticket checkout and Connect onboarding throw `paid_entry_paused`; `TicketingManager`/`PayoutsTab` show `PaidEntryNotice` unless an event already has orders. Turn either on only from Platform Admin, after the tax and organizer-agreement questions are answered.
- **Fraud warnings and disputes (2026-10-18, `src/lib/stripe/fraud.ts`):** `radar.early_fraud_warning.created` refunds the charge in full with reason `fraudulent` (idempotency key per warning; Stripe then puts the card and email on the Radar block list) and cancels any subscription the charge paid for (found through `invoicePayments.list` by payment intent: charges no longer carry their invoice), then emails support@ and sends a Sentry warning once. `charge.dispute.created` emails the amount, reason and evidence deadline in Pacific (and cancels the subscription when the reason is fraud); `charge.dispute.closed` emails won/lost. Platform account only. **The live and test webhook endpoints must list these three event types**, or Stripe never sends them. Dev check: `scripts/.tmp/fraud-e2e.ts` (test mode; it removes the block list entries its refunds create, because a fraudulent refund on 4242 blocks that test card for everyone).
- Checkout entry: `/api/stripe/checkout` (creates Stripe Checkout session)
- Customer Portal: `/api/stripe/portal` (subscription management, ToS Section 6 commits to this path)
- Webhook handler: `/api/stripe/webhook` — signature-verified, handles `checkout.session.completed`, `customer.subscription.*`, `invoice.payment_*`, `trial_will_end`
- **Support GameShuffle** (`/support`, `src/lib/support/store.ts`, `supabase/support-m1.sql`): one-time or monthly support through Stripe Checkout (`gs_kind: "support"`, routed before the Pro sync and guarded in `upsertSubscriptionFromStripe`), cards only, US only (IP block + non-US billing refunded), monthly needs an account; Supporter badge for a year or while monthly is active; a goal bar and supporters wall (Platform ▸ Support). Terms §3.6. **Hardening (2026-10-18):** the form needs a passed Turnstile check before `/api/support/checkout` opens a session (`verifyTurnstileToken`, `{ error: "captcha" }`; `TurnstileWidget resetKey` gives a fresh challenge after each try, since a token works once), because an open $3 checkout is what card testers look for; every support price (one-time and monthly) hangs off one Stripe product `gs_support` ("Support GameShuffle", made on first use by `supportProduct()` in `src/lib/support/store.ts`, inline product only if Stripe can't be reached), not a new product per checkout.
- **Support beside GS Pro (built 2026-10-19, audit https://claude.ai/artifact/FuJ1X3bxR6bozhJxEKPYry, mockups + 17 picks https://claude.ai/artifact/VDh1QfETVjNTjjzeiKqLNg, all as recommended; `supabase/support-m2.sql`).** Support is as important as GS Pro and open to everyone, GS Pro members included; supporters get thank-yous, never features, items or tokens (that keeps it a gift for sales tax; non-US support waits on the accountant, so outside the US it's a waitlist). Say "support" or "chip in", never "donate". Pieces: `useSupportStatus` (one `/api/support/status` lookup per page: available, supporter, since, name, monthly, progress, supportersThisMonth); `SupportMoment` (after something finishes: the dailies, Fire Round, the Weekly, a Chat Brain answer on /chat-brain, a trivia night (phone), a party night, a finished tournament, free tools after a button is pressed (`ToolBody`); flat under a thin line, at most once a week per browser (localStorage `gs-support-moment`), supporters get "Thanks for supporting GameShuffle, Sam." instead; never in the Discord Activity) and `SupportInstead` (under every `ProTrialPrompt` and the AI limit window); `BackingPair` (GS Pro and Support, same size, on /gs-pro and /support; one waitlist outside the US, `PaidPlansWaitlist product="both"`; the waitlist also takes `support`); the homepage `PlansBand` has three rows (GS Pro, GS Circuit, Support) for everyone and reads `paidPlansHere()` + the viewer's trial and supporter status; Plans & billing has a Support card; **Account ▸ Support GameShuffle** (`?tab=support`, `SupportScreen`, `/api/account/support` GET + PATCH wall): since when, badge until, monthly with Change or stop, every gift, the wall name and note (autosaved), Share your card, and the give form; the user menu has a heart item and "Help" (was "Help & Support"), the phone menu has GS Pro and Support rows. **Recognition:** a rose ring and heart on the supporter's profile picture, "Supporter since Oct 2026" (the owner's badge shares their card, `SupporterBadge`), a heart by their name on daily leaderboards (`supportersAmong`, `BoardRow.supporter`), the wall. **Supporter card:** share kind `supporter` (`SupporterPart`: since + monthly, never an amount; accounts that support now, or a gift's paid Checkout session from the thank-you page, which gets no guest name), drawn on their colors like the result cards; `/r/<code>` says "Sam keeps game night free" with Play free (their invite code) and Support GameShuffle. Shared from the thank-you page (`SupporterShare`), Account ▸ Support and the profile badge; the thank-you email links it once. **Signed-out gifts** keep `support_gifts.email_hash` (sha256 of the lowercased email, never the address) and `claimSupportGifts` gives them to the account with that confirmed email (status route, Account ▸ Support, `/api/account/ensure-username`). The Arcade's 500-token "Supporter" badge is retired (support-m2 refunds the tokens, removes it and sends a note). `TrialNote` swaps "14 days free" on marketing rows (plans panel, /live footer, the Pokémon page) for visitors outside the US or whose trial is used. Terms 3.6 and Privacy 2 updated (effective Oct 19). Dev checks: `scripts/.tmp/support-build-e2e.ts` (24).
- **Support levels and "support first" (built 2026-10-19, picks in the support first plan, all as recommended; `supabase/support-m3.sql` on dev + prod).** Support now leads GS Pro: the navbar's "Go Pro" became a heart **Support** button for visitors and free accounts (`NavSupport`; members and supporters get a thank-you heart to Account ▸ Support), Support comes first in `PlansBand`, `BackingPair` and the phone menu, and the GS Pro walls' second line (`SupportInstead`) points at the Backer level. **Monthly support is three levels** (`SUPPORT_LEVELS` in `src/lib/support/rules.ts`): Supporter $3 (thanks only, a gift), **Backer $9** (GS Pro included) and **Champion $25** (GS Pro, top of the wall, the credits); one-time support stays any amount from $3, thanks only. The give form (`SupportForm`) opens on Monthly with three pressable CDS Cards, `?level=` starts it on one. A level with GS Pro is a sale: its own Stripe product (`gs_support_backer` / `gs_support_champion`, GS Pro's tax code copied from the Pro price's product), Stripe Tax and the Terms consent at checkout, and it's refused (`already_pro`, 409) for an account that already has GS Pro from a plan, trial or Circuit 256. **How Pro is granted:** while a Backer or Champion subscription is live, `syncSupportPro` sets the same `users.subscription_tier='pro'` GS Pro uses (so every existing gate works) and marks that subscription `granted_pro`; when no level is live it takes back only a grant support made (no own GS Pro plan), with GS Pro's Twitch-disconnect cleanup. The GS Pro sync (`upsertSubscriptionFromStripe`) asks `supportProGrant` before it would downgrade anyone, `getProAccess` reports the source as `support_level` ("Included with your support" in Plans & billing, which shows a Your support button instead of upgrade buttons). A new level replaces the old one (`endOtherMonthly` cancels it with proration). Monthly support from before the levels has no level and reads as Supporter. **Also:** the wall orders Champions, then Backers (`supportersWall`), `/credits` ("Made possible by", `supportCredits`, in the footer, sitemap and welcome page), a Founding supporter badge for the first 100 supporters (`foundingSupporters`, worked out from the paid gifts, shown on profiles and in Account ▸ Support), short **updates** on /support written in Platform ▸ Support (`support_updates`, latest five), level counts there, the level on profiles ("Backer since …"), the thank-you page and email, and Terms §3.6 rewritten for the levels. Dev check `scripts/.tmp/support-levels-e2e.ts` (16 checks, Stripe test mode on quiet_quinn; dev's deployed webhook runs older code that files support as a GS Pro plan, so the script clears those rows).
- **Webhook payloads follow the account's default API version (`2026-04-22.dahlia`), not the SDK pin.** Since 2025-03-31 an invoice's subscription is at `invoice.parent.subscription_details.subscription` and a subscription's period end is on its items; read them through `src/lib/stripe/payload.ts` (`subscriptionIdOf`, `periodEndOf`, `intervalForPrice`). Reading the old top-level fields silently skipped the payment-failed and cancelled emails until 2026-10-08.
- Subscription state mirroring: `src/lib/stripe/subscriptions.ts` keeps `public.users` in sync with Stripe
- Billing email templates: `src/lib/email/billing.ts` (trial reminders, payment receipts, cancellation confirmations)
- Account email templates: `src/lib/email/account.ts` (deletion confirmations, etc.)

### Hub / Sessions
- **Stream hub redesign (2026-10-10, audit + mockups https://claude.ai/artifact/4npmxxzygb6MWZemkxtQ54):** the hub is for running streams; setup lives in Streaming settings. `/hub` = setup alert (no Twitch / bot not authorized / EventSub < 4), a navy Live card per active stream (box art, game · lobby count · picks open, "Live page" + "Open control room"), Coming up rows, This month stats (streams, viewers who played, rolls), Past streams as a CDS Table with `?q=` search, `?test=1` and `?limit=` Show more. "New stream" opens `NewSessionModal` (one draft per streamer is a DB unique index; the refusal returns the draft and the modal offers "Open …").
- **One stream page, tabs by phase** (`src/app/hub/sessions/[slug]/page.tsx`, `phaseOf` + `resolveTab` maps old tab ids): plan (draft/scheduled/ready) = Plan (`PlanChecklist` + `SessionConfigureTab`) · Games; recap (ended/cancelled) = Recap (numbers, What happened, tokens handed out) · Activity; **live (active/ending) = Control room · Lobby · Picks & bans · Live games · Predictions · Games · Activity · Settings** (2026-10-10, second pass). **Just in time:** everything on a live stream follows the game on stream. `hubGameActions(slug)` (`src/lib/hub/gameActions.ts`, server-only) says what the game can do (from `CHAT_GAMES` `noun`/`choices` and `CHAT_SETUPS` `choices`): the streamer's own roll ("Roll my fighter", hero roles, Stadium cups), the match setup with its variants, viewer battles (not streamer-only games), race rolls + Picks & bans (MK8DX/MKW only) and prediction markets (MK only; other games get a **Bounties** tab). A queue gets none. `HubLiveProvider` (`src/components/hub/live/`) polls `GET /api/hub/running?session=` every 5s (game on stream, lobby count, the last roll in this game, and what's running: picks round, poll, bingo, draft, Chat Brain, market, bounties) and calls `router.refresh()` when the Twitch category changes, so controls, cards and tabs switch with the game. Control room: main column = `GameRolls` (this game's rolls; MK race buttons use `/api/twitch/race/reroll`, everything else `POST /api/hub/roll` {kind: pick|setup|battle, arg}, which runs the chat handlers as the broadcaster), `RunningNow` (a card per running thing, same footer on every card: its next step left, Open right; "+ Poll / Bingo / …" starts only what this game can run) and `HubToolsRun` ("Overlay tools": CDS pill tabs pick one tool and only its row shows, remembered per browser in localStorage `gs-hub-tool`); side column = `LastRoll`, the lobby (scrolls; full list on the Lobby tab) and Activity (components in `src/components/hub/live/`). **Lever rows (decided 2026-10-10, "CTAs all over the place"):** every roll and tool is a `Lever` (`src/components/hub/live/Lever.tsx`) with the same three columns (name, one CDS Select or nothing, actions) so options and buttons line up down the page. Button rules: small only; a row's actions are `secondary`, the first gets `hub-lever__main` (shared min-width); utilities (Clear, Entries, Mark squares) are `ghost`; navigation (Open, + Start) is `ghost`; exactly one `primary` on the page, the game's main roll. Results go in the row's `status`, bigger panels (entrants, bingo board, tier list) in its children. The tool controls (`WheelControl`, `DiceControl`, …) render a `Lever` each and use CDS Select/Input/Checkbox/Chip (they had native selects and emoji labels). Lobby tab: everyone with kick, "Lobby on your live page" (moved out of Settings while live), chat commands. Picks & bans tab: `DashboardLiveControls part="picks"` (round controls plus a read-only "Leading so far"). Live games tab: `LiveGamesPanel` (the Streaming ▸ Live games screens as pills, `?view=polls|bingo|draft|chat-brain`, a Live badge on what's running). `SessionDetailTabs` mounts a tab on first open and keeps it, with arrow-key navigation. The session page keeps its explicit Save (streamer feedback); `SaveBar` shows only when something changed. Removed earlier: Redemptions, Viewers and Stream Tools tabs, per-session channel points and modules surfaces.
- Core table: `gs_sessions` — multi-platform session entity, replaces legacy `twitch_sessions` for new work
- Generic platform participants: `session_participants` (platform + platform_user_id + display name + combo)
- Session events: `session_events` (audit log for state transitions + adapter actions)
- Session phases: `draft → scheduled → open → active → ending → ended`
- **Spec 02 lifecycle:** scheduled→open transitions fired by a pg_cron-driven scheduler (`scheduled-opens-sweep`), policy-aware event publisher fans out to Twitch chat + Discord on lifecycle transitions
- Configure surface: `src/components/hub/tabs/{SessionConfigureTab, SessionModulesTab}` + per-section forms
- Real-time updates via Supabase Realtime on `gs_sessions` + `session_participants` + `session_events`
- Recap: `loadRecapForStreamer()` in `src/lib/sessions/recap.ts` — public read for past sessions

### Platform Adapters (PlatformAdapter pattern)
- Adapter interface defined in `src/lib/adapters/` — each platform implements
- `TwitchAdapter` in `src/lib/adapters/twitch/` — primary platform
- `DiscordAdapter` in `src/lib/adapters/discord/{adapter, embeds, roomCode}` — second platform live
- Adapter-agnostic event publisher: `src/lib/events/policy.ts` — emits domain events, adapters subscribe and translate to platform messages
- Sessions bind to multiple platforms via `gs_sessions.platforms` JSONB; dispatcher fans out
- New platforms (YouTube, Kick) implement `PlatformAdapter` — never branch on platform type directly in Hub UI or session code

### Token Economy + Command Suite
Closed-loop currency system. Tokens never bought with money, never redeemed for real value. See `specs/gs-token-economy/` for full spec set.

- **Balance is derived, never stored** — always `sum(token_events.amount)`. Atomic spend path under lock prevents negative balances
- **Identity:** `src/lib/economy/identity.ts` — `gs_identity` keyed on `(platform, platform_id)`. Optional `gs_account_id` links to a GS user. Account upgrade is a LINK (preserves balance/history), never a recreate
- **Token events ledger:** `token_events` table is the source of truth. All mints (`grant_*`, `earn_*`, `award_mint`), burns (`chaos_burn`), and transfers (`bet`, `transfer_out`, `give`) flow through it
- **Minting policy:** all economy numbers come from `gs_economy_config` (+ `gs_streamer_allowance` per-period ceilings). No magic numbers. Dashboard-tunable via Platform Economy tab
- **Awards / bounties:** `src/lib/economy/{awards, bounties}.ts` — `!gs award @user <amount>` discretionary, `!gs bounty <amount> <condition>` outcome-pegged
- **Prediction markets:** `src/lib/economy/markets/` — `broadcasts`, `lifecycle`, `resolveFanout`, `spectator`, `templates`. Live view via `LiveMarketsTab` with Realtime subscription
- **Module registry:** `src/lib/modules/` — `registry`, `store`, `templates`, `picks`, `bans`, `streamerDefaults`, `templateResolver`. First-party only for now
- **Compliance gate:** `src/lib/economy/compliance/{gate, region}` — region + spectator-mode gate checked BEFORE the streamer module toggle. Cannot be overridden by streamers
- **Command suite:** `src/lib/twitch/commands/` — dispatcher + registry + custom commands + default-handler fallback + help renderer. All commands register through Spec 03 `CommandDef` (actor / surface / economy / help). Help is a view over the registry
- **Event system:** `src/lib/economy/events/{consent, engine, partners}` — M3 (`!chaos`/`!random`) basics; M4 event deck depth + challenges + secret missions still in flight. An event's `game_scope` is a game slug: `!chaos`/`!random` draw it only while that game is on stream (`canonicalizeGameKey` in `eventCommands.ts` used to match only the two Mario Karts, so events set to any other game were never drawn; fixed 2026-10-10). **Settling challenges (built 2026-10-11):** the control room's Running now shows a **Challenges** card (from `HubRunning.challenges`: open ones, plus market-settled open ones nobody was paid for) that opens `ChallengesModal`: Done / Not done per challenge; a challenge pegged to a viewer pays them or takes the penalty, an open one asks **Who did it?** (the lobby's existing wallets, then recently active chatters; read-only lookups so no wallet is opened) and pays each person picked (`event_delta`, max 10). `src/lib/economy/events/challenges.ts` (`hubChallenges`, `winnerCandidates`, `settleChallenge`: claims the row first, so no double pay) and `/api/hub/challenges` (owner or Full mod).
- **Leaderboards:** `src/lib/economy/leaderboards.ts` — three-layer (viewer perf / streamer engagement / global). Streamers excluded from Viewer Leaderboard (operators, not participants)

### Picks/Bans (Track + Item Randomization Phase A/B)
