- Library: `src/lib/picks-bans/` — `queries`, `aggregate`, `rateLimit`, `modePresentation`, `types`, custom icons
- UI: `src/components/picks-bans/PicksBansPicker.tsx`
- Schema: `session_picks_bans_drafts`, `session_picks_bans_rounds`, `session_picks_bans_ballots`
- Public read on OPEN rounds in active sessions; streamer reads everything on their own session
- Anonymous viewer ballots via `anon_session_id` (browser sessionStorage UUID); authed via `viewer_twitch_user_id`
- Rate limiting enforced server-side, not via RLS
- Per-stream chat commands: `!picks`, `!bans`, plus dispatched directly through `src/lib/twitch/commands/picksBans.ts`

### Live View (`/live/[streamer-slug]`)
- Public read-only view of a streamer's currently-active session
- `src/components/live/LiveStreamView.tsx` — composes participants + current track/items + picks/bans state + markets. Tabs: Play (Your roll when the signed-in viewer is seated, Picks & bans with the room's tally under the ballot while a round is open, Events only while one is active), Predict, **Lobby** (id `room`, so `?tab=room` and old links open it; labelled Standings and without the lobby grid when the session's `feature_flags.public_lobby` ?? `twitch_connections.public_lobby_enabled` is false), History. `?tab=` picks the first tab. The page root is `<main style={brandStyle}>`; brand inks are derived against #eeeeee / #1c1c22 (`INK_SURFACE_*` in `brand.ts`) so they pass on the grey page and tinted alerts.
- Real-time updates via Supabase Realtime
- **Tidied 2026-10-19:** the Picks & bans tabs are plain labels on one line (your counts sit in the round bar as "Your picks N · bans N"), one guest sign-in line under the ballot (no info box on top), the stream panel (`CurrentSettings`) is flat under the player on phones and leaves out Races/Items until rolled, "How the room is voting" sits flat under a rule, and the footer has a GS Pro line for viewers who stream (`Upgrade Clicked` {from: live-footer}).
- **Viewer economy surfaces** — a persistent **token-balance badge** in the header (`ViewerBalanceBadge` + `useViewerBalance` → read-only `/api/economy/balance`; pre-bet "available" context in `LiveMarketsTab`), and an **Events tab** (`LiveEventsTab`) — the viewer face of the Spec 04 event system: active modifiers + open **public** challenges (`listLiveSessionEvents` → `/api/live/[slug]/events`; secret missions excluded). The OBS overlay renders the same modifiers/challenges in a top-left banner (overlay `latest` payload + `OverlayClient`).
- Twitch viewer OAuth flow creates minimal GS user records (auth-for-tactile only) — not a viewer-experience surface
- Slug resolution: `users.username` first (canonical GS slug), falls back to `users.twitch_username`
- A signed-in streamer viewing their own slug sees the same viewer UI — streamer controls live on `/hub`
- **Offline state** (no active GS session) — embeds the **Twitch player** (`TwitchEmbed`): the live stream when live, otherwise **autoplays the last broadcast VOD** (`getReplayVodId` → Helix `getStreamsByUserIds` + `getLatestArchiveVideoId`; passes `videoId` to embed `?video=` over `?channel=`). Plus the community leaderboard, the **last-stream recap** (`loadRecapForStreamer` — prefers the most recent non-test ended session, falls back to the latest ended incl. test so it's never empty), and a link to the streamer's `/u` profile

### Mods (Mod Accounts)
- `/mod/invite` — landing for invite tokens
- `/mod/[streamer]` — the mod's view of the streamer's stream: banner, status header, Lobby (kick/timeout via `ModParticipantsPanel`) + Activity, then the streamer's decks. Full-access mods get the stream's controls as tabs while the streamer is live (below)
- **Self-serve join (2026-10-10, verified Twitch mods get in right away):** `/mod/join/[streamer]` (`src/lib/mods/join.ts`, `POST /api/mod/join`). Signed out = "Sign in with Twitch" (returns to the page; `/mod/join/` is on the callback allowlist), signed in = "Join as {name}'s mod": `joinAsMod` reads the streamer's Twitch mod list (Helix with the streamer's token) and, when the account's linked Twitch id is on it, upserts the `streamer_mods` row as active (source `twitch_auto_import`). A mod the streamer removed by hand (`revoked_by_user_id`) can't rejoin; the streamer and active mods are sent to `/mod/<slug>`. Plain errors in `JOIN_ERRORS`. Ways in: `!gs mod` in chat (mods + host, posts the link), the Mods tab's "Copy the mod link", and "Post in chat" on a pending mod (`/api/account/mods/[id]/chat-invite`, the bot mentions them). Joined mods get the standard level (lobby kick, activity, decks)
- **Full access (built 2026-10-10, `supabase/mod-access-m1.sql`):** `streamer_mods.access` = `standard` | `full`, set per active mod on the Mods tab (CDS Select; hidden until the column exists, `accessReady` from `/api/account/mods`, PATCH `{ mod_id, access }`). A Full mod's `/mod/<streamer>` page, while the streamer is live, shows the stream page's own components as tabs: Control room (`GameRolls` with `streamerName`, `RunningNow predictions={false}`, `HubToolsRun setupLink={false}`, last roll, lobby, activity), Picks & bans (race games), Live games (`LiveGamesPanel`), Decks; the streamer previewing their own mod page sees the same. **How the controls act for the streamer:** `streamOwnerFor(userId)` in `src/lib/mods/acting.ts` returns the streamer when the request comes from `/mod/<slug>` (server actions: the middleware's `x-pathname`, which it overwrites on every page request; `/api` fetches: the same-origin Referer) and the database says the caller is an active Full mod there; otherwise the caller's own account. Used by `bingoOwner` (bingo, drafts, Chat Brain stream), polls `ownerContext`, `/api/break`, `/api/hub/roll`, `/api/hub/running`, `/api/twitch/race/reroll`, `GET /api/account/wheels`, and `resolveAuthorizedUser(MODS_TOO)` in the stream page's actions (picks and bans + overlay tools only; start/end/edit/restart, queue and race config stay owner-only). Plan checks use the streamer's plan; overlay tools credit the mod (`modLabel`, "Sam (mod)"). Not for mods: predictions, bounties, ending or editing the stream. Closed picks rounds: `supabase/mod-picks-tallies-m1.sql` (2026-10-12) lets an active Full mod read their streamer's ballots, so a closed round's tallies show on the mod page while the streamer is live (checked on dev with `scripts/.tmp/mod-tally-check.ts`: Standard can't, Full can). Help: `/help/streaming/mods`
- Mod permissions configured via `ModsTab` on `/account`
- See `specs/gs-pro-updates/gs-mod-accounts-spec.md` for the model
- Mod actions in chat dispatched via `src/lib/twitch/commands/moderation.ts` (`!gs-kick`, `!gs-clear`)

### Public Profile (`/u/[username]`) — identity surface
- Public read of a `is_public` profile. Server component; viewer-specific bits (follow state, block enforcement) read cookies, so it's dynamic.
- **Identity fields** on `users`: `bio`, `pronouns`, `location`, `socials` (JSONB, content platforms — distinct from `gamertags`), `favorite_games` (`text[]`, picked via CDS `Combobox` from `src/data/favorite-games.ts` with real cover art), `profile_banner_url` + `profile_banner_source_url` (R2), `profile_theme` (personal brand theme).
- **Enrichment** (`src/lib/profile/enrichment.ts`, service-client so a viewer's RLS doesn't blank the owner's data): token wallet (sum of `getBalance` over the account's `gs_identities`), communities (distinct `token_events.community_id`), config count, tournaments (organized + joined), `isStreamer`/`isLive` (`twitch_connections`), `isOnline` (`last_seen_at`).
- **Badges**: Staff (role) / GS Pro (`effectiveTier`) / live-aware streamer badge → `/live/[username]` ("Watch live" → red "Check out live page" when `twitch_connections.is_live`).
- Gamertags + socials render with **service icons** (shared `src/components/PlatformIcon.tsx`). Configs open a **detail modal** (`ProfileConfigs` renders `config_data` visuals).

### Personalization (per-user profile theming + UGC)
- **Personal brand theme** — `users.profile_theme`; `getBrandThemeForOwner` prefers it, falls back to `gs_communities.brand_theme`. The **Theme** tab is ungated (any account; in the Account sidebar group).
- **Profile banner** — uploaded to Cloudflare R2 (`gameshuffle-ugc`, served via `gs-ugc.empac.co`). `src/lib/storage/r2.ts` (`@aws-sdk/client-s3`; env `R2_ACCOUNT_ID`/`R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID`/`R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`/`R2_BUCKET`/`R2_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`). `BannerUploader` → `BannerEditModal` (crop/zoom/position via `react-easy-crop`) → `/api/account/banner` (stores cropped + original). **Reposition** re-crops the original via a same-origin proxy (`/api/account/banner/raw`) to avoid canvas taint. CSP `img-src` must include `gs-ugc.empac.co`.

### Trust & Safety (reporting · blocking · moderation · appeals)
- `src/lib/moderation/` — `reports`, `store` (staff review queue + actions), `blocks` (`isBlocked` checks both directions; severs follows on block), `appeals`, `audit`, `status` (`isPubliclyVisible`).
- **Reporting** — `ReportProfileButton` on `/u` → `/api/reports` (authed skip captcha; anon clear Turnstile, hashed-IP dedupe). **Blocking** — `BlockProfileButton` + `/api/account/blocks`; manager in account → Security; mutual hide. **Appeals** — `ModerationNotice` on `/account` for suspended/banned users → `/api/account/appeal`.
- **Staff** — Platform Moderation tab (`PlatformModerationTab`) + `/api/admin/moderation`: dismiss / clear_display_name / clear_bio / clear_banner / warn / suspend / ban / unban + grant/deny appeals. Ban/unban admin-only; staff can't moderate staff/admin. `moderation_audit_log`.
- A suspended/banned profile is withheld from the `/u` body, OG metadata (`robots: noindex`), and the sitemap.

### Social Layer (follows · presence · notifications · invitations · messaging)
- All built on CDS social components (`FollowButton`, `UserCard`, `Notifications`, `Chat`, `MentionInput`) and block-aware throughout.
- **Follows + presence** — `src/lib/social/follows.ts` (`getFollowState`/counts, `follow` creates a notification on a *new* follow), `src/lib/social/topFriends.ts` (top friends + connections lists), `last_seen_at` heartbeat (`PresenceHeartbeat` in `AuthProvider`, `/api/account/heartbeat`; online = seen < 5 min). On `/u`: `FollowStats` (clickable counts → followers/following modal), `ProfileFollow`, Top Friends grid (`FriendTile`).
- **Notifications** — `src/lib/social/notifications.ts` + `notifications` table (RLS read/mark-read own, service-role inserts, in the realtime publication). Surfaced in the **Comms Center** (`/comms` Alerts tab) via `useNotifications` — realtime, unread badge, Accept/Decline actions for invites; the navbar bell icon deep-links there.
- **Invitations** — `src/lib/social/invitations.ts` + `invitations` table → notification with Accept/Decline. `InviteButton`/`InviteFollowersModal` on the tournament manage page + hub session page.
- **Messaging** — `src/lib/social/messaging.ts` + **membership-based** `conversations` (`kind`: `'dm'` canonicalized on `user_lo/user_hi`, or scoped `'crew'`/`'tcg'`/… deduped on `(kind, scope_id)`) + `conversation_members` (membership + per-member `last_read_at`) + `messages` (realtime). `getOrCreateScopedConversation` is the foundation for **crew/app group chats**. Surfaced in the **Comms Center** (`/comms` Messages tab, CDS `Chat` embedded) via the `useMessaging` hook (renders DM vs group); `/messages` redirects to `/comms?tab=messages`. `MessageButton` on `/u`; a new DM creates a deduped ping notification.
  - **Comms Center** (`/comms`) — unifies notifications (Alerts tab) + messages (Messages tab) in one auth-gated page (`CommsCenter`, URL-driven `?tab=`), reached via the navbar **bell + messages icons** (`CommsIcons` — per-type unread badges from `useCommsUnread`/`/api/comms/unread`, each deep-linking the right tab). Replaced the standalone `NotificationsBell` dropdown + floating `MessagesPanel`.

### TCG Companion (`/tcg-companion`)
- TCG-agnostic digital accessory kit — damage counters, condition tracking, prize counts, coin flips, dice
- Pokémon Mode ships first (`src/lib/companion/modes/pokemon.ts`)
- Publicly available (out of beta) — the old `COMPANION_BETA_MODE` passphrase gate has been removed. Entry is sign-in or continue-as-guest; the in-app feedback button is always on
- **My Cards** (`/tcg-companion/collection`) — Scrydex-backed collection; requires a signed-in account (no guests), free to browse + collect (`companion.collection` granted to the free tier). Reachable via "My Cards" in the board header + entry chooser
- Save states persisted to `companion_save_states` table
- Tier-gated capabilities via `hasCapability()`
- Components: `CompanionEntry` / `CompanionShell` / `CompanionPage`
- Themable (auth-gated surface)
- Drag/drop interactions tuned for mobile touch (recent commits fixed iOS callout suppression + bench scroll vs slot drag)
- **Playing Pokémon (Phase 0 + 1, built 2026-10-11; plan https://claude.ai/artifact/EDpHe8ANsENfGSMrbHcHgN, mockups https://claude.ai/artifact/2wuWSjC3CYwgq7FuzZtDN4):** A rules-aware table companion, then deck lists, then practice hands; never digital play (no deck held, no hidden cards dealt). **Remind, don't block** (Britton): a rule slip never stops a move. **Undo** (`withHistory` in `state.ts`, 50 steps; coin/dice logs and the save link sit outside it; a new game, a loaded game or new settings start fresh). The game in progress is kept in this browser (`src/lib/companion/autosave.ts`, `gs-companion-autosave`, 3 days, offered first on the resume list), guests too. **Rules** `src/lib/companion/pokemonRules.ts` (pure: damage in rulebook order with Weakness ×2 or Pocket's +20 and Resistance −30 on the Active only, prizes from subtypes (ex 2, Mega ex 3, older eras), Energy costs with Colorless, the `reminder*` sentences). **Turns** live on `SessionState.turn` (`START_TURNS` / `END_TURN` / `MARK_USED` / `ATTACK` with `self` damage in the same step; Energy attached and retreats by the player whose turn it is tick themselves; Paralyzed clears at the end of its owner's turn; slots carry `enteredTurn`, `tool` and the card's `rules`), saved in account saves and the local copy (older saves load without them). `src/lib/companion/reminders.ts` `remindFor` reads the board before a move and the session's dispatch shows it as a "Rule check" toast with Undo (that Undo only takes back the flagged move). UI: `TurnBar` (sticky under the nav: who goes first with Not now, whose turn, once-a-turn chips, Attack, End turn, "needs a new Active" after a Knock Out), `AttackModal` (the card's real attacks, target, damage math, card-effects ±, "also does N to itself", Knock Out line, "Attack anyway" on a slip), Checkup between turns = `ResolveModal player="both"` with End turn. Picking a card keeps its rules and sets prizes from its type (`rulesFromCard`, `prizeValue`). Tests `scripts/test-companion-{undo,rules,turns}.ts`. IP stance (Britton, 2026-10-11): fine as is until a cease-and-desist; card art off + rename are quick if one comes. Next: deck lists (Phase 2).
- **Simplify pass (2026-10-11, mockups https://claude.ai/artifact/K67ndqLNThPxZUawqMcFro):** **Icon language** (`src/components/companion/glyphs.tsx`; pure helpers `conditionLooks`, `typeDef`, `countersFor` in `src/lib/companion/looks.ts` so server pages can use them): circle = Energy or type (`EnergyDot`; Colorless = star, Special = sparkles), square = Special Condition (`ConditionTag`: skull Poison, thermometer Burn, Zzz Asleep, lock Paralyzed, spiral Confused), `CounterToken` 10/50/100 discs, `AnyEnergy`, `CardArt`; heart = HP left, cards = prizes, arrows = retreat, hourglass = new this turn. **Help** (`HelpButton`) holds the key ("What the icons mean") and the turn guide, linking each condition to its rules page. **Slot face** (`Slot.tsx`): type circle, condition tags, name, heart HP, bar, Energy pills (circle + count, dashed + when none) and a damage pill; taps route by target: damage → `CountersModal` (HP left, counters to Knock Out, damage as tokens, Add/Take off, +10/+50/+100), Energy → `EnergyPicker` (picture "Goes to" tiles, the types this Pokémon uses with why, one tap attaches and closes with Undo; warns first when this turn's Energy is used), elsewhere → `SlotActionsModal` (views main/evolve/tool/more: card + facts, full-width damage row, condition Chips, Energy with −, Add Energy, action tiles; Attack is the one primary, Knock Out the one red). `AttackModal` shows costs as Energy circles (each Radio wrapped, so it gets name/checked/onChange itself: CDS RadioGroup only wires direct children). **Card art:** only the art window shows; small slots cover-crop with TypeCard's ART_REGION, big slots (`@container pieceart (min-width: 480px)`) frame it at most ~430×234 over a blurred copy, centred above the name. Store the medium image Scrydex returns at placement/evolve; never rewrite stored image URLs. **Placing** (`PlacePieceModal` + `CardPicker`): card first as picture tiles, search any card, "Add one by name" fallback. CDS gaps raised: picture radio cards (Goes to), a segmented switch (Add/Take off).
- **Play together (built 2026-10-11, mockups https://claude.ai/artifact/Qgk32PjLmoKsytwfrAfnj4, real screenshots in the build tour https://claude.ai/artifact/9iXxGdC2JF12no7pjbqVoS, `supabase/companion-live-m1.sql` applied dev + prod; API end-to-end `scripts/.tmp/live-api-e2e.ts` 17/17 and a two-browser run passed on dev):** one companion game on two phones, at the same table or far apart on a call with real cards (never digital play: no deck or hand in the app). Host = GS Pro (p1), the opponent joins free with the 6-character code or the link `/tcg-companion?play=CODE` (no new route), signed in or as a guest. **Server-run board:** `companion_live_games` holds the state; every request loads the row, asks a pure plan in `src/lib/companion/livePlan.ts` (`planMove`/`planJoin`/`planUndo`/`planRoll`, the same reducer as the phone) and saves only if `version` hasn't moved, re-planning on a clash (`planAndSave` in `live.ts`, server-only). Coin flips and dice are rolled on the server (`liveRoll`; `use: "resolve"` keeps a flip in `pending_rolls` so Burn/Asleep resolve moves can't swap it); Undo takes back only your own last move while nobody moved since, never a flip (`undoCheck`, `kind: "roll"`); only the host resets, loads or changes settings (`hostOnly`). Seats: account = key, guest = token (sha256 in `seats`, kept in localStorage `gs-companion-live`); `companion_live_seen` stamps presence. Rules + move lines (`describeMove`) + view types in `liveRules.ts` (client-safe). Routes `/api/companion/live` (GET ready/mine, POST create, Pro) and `/api/companion/live/[code]` (preview, view `?v=`, join/move/undo/roll/end; errors in `errors.ts`). Phone side: `SessionContext` `live` mode lays unconfirmed moves over the server board, sends them one at a time (`liveClient.ts`), polls every `POLL_MS` (1.5s) while visible, pops the other player's big moves, exposes `random.coin/die` (coin/dice windows and Checkup use it), `bottom` (each phone's own side at the bottom: `--top`/`--bottom` grid areas), `startLive`/`endLive`/`leaveLive`. UI: `PlayTogetherModal` (sign in / trial prompt / share → code, Copy link, Share, Copy for Discord, waiting → joined), `LiveStrip` under the turn bar, `LiveJoin` (`?play=`), `JoinByCode` (entry, new game window, Menu), TurnBar buttons only on the turn player's phone. Hidden until the table exists (`useLiveReady`). Tests `scripts/test-companion-live.ts` (pure, 21). The view tells each phone whether it can Undo (`canUndo`, from `undoCheck`). The coin and dice logs are newest first. Known gaps: a guest who clears their browser can't retake their seat (host ends and re-shares); `/tcg-companion` isn't on the sign-in redirect allowlist, so OAuth/magic-link sign-in from the companion lands on /account (password sign-in is fine).
- **Rules pages for search (2026-10-11, SEO plan https://claude.ai/artifact/Y6p1rHtuSAQiDmJkz6cPSi):** `/pokemon-tcg/rules` hub + damage-counters, special-conditions, weakness-and-resistance, prize-cards, how-to-play (`src/data/tcg-rules.ts` `RULE_PAGES`, `ruleUrl`, `RULES_CHECKED`; `RulesArticle` with breadcrumb, FAQ, related links and Article/FAQPage/BreadcrumbList JSON-LD; on-page tools `CounterTool`, `DamageCalculator`; `ruleMetadata()` restates the folder's share image because a page's openGraph drops it; styles `src/styles/tcg-rules.css`). `/tcg-companion` is noindex, follow; `/pokemon-tcg-companion` is the page that ranks (marketing-apps entry rewritten, HowTo JSON-LD). Deck guide titles shortened with a separate `h1`, www canonicals (`src/lib/decks/seo.ts`). SE Ranking: group "Pokémon TCG (rules + companion)" in the GameShuffle project (27 keywords with target pages).
- **Card checklists (2026-10-12):** `/pokemon-tcg/checklists` hub + one page per list (`src/data/tcg-checklists.ts` `CHECKLISTS`: sections of cards with number, label and artist, each list with its sources and checked date; `checklistUrl`). First list: the 30th Celebration Pikachu (023-052 with all 30 artists, Pikachu ex 053/054; numbers from Serebii, artists from two checklists that agree). `ChecklistBoard` (client): CDS Checkbox per card, ticks in localStorage `gs-checklist-<slug>` (no account, nothing sent), Progress, "Only what I still need" switch, "Copy what I need" for trading. ItemList + FAQPage + BreadcrumbList JSON-LD, in the sitemap. They double as the shop's digital lists; `ShopChecklistLine` (placement `checklist`) shows only once visitors can see the shop. Second list (2026-10-12): Ascended Heroes commons and uncommons (153: 84 C, 69 U, sections Pokémon / Trainers / Special Energy, rarity on each row; names and rarities agree across Limitless, PkmnCards and the Pokémon TCG API and match our catalog on all 91 it holds, artists from the API only), the Set Builder 100's digital list.

- **CDS pass (2026-10-12):** the new-game window's formats are CDS Cards (pressable, selected state) laid out like the site's other icon tiles (2026-10-13: a rounded-square gradient tile above the title, 2×2 on phones and 4 across wider; a circle means Energy in the companion, so never a circle), custom rules CDS Selects + Switches; save window CDS Radio + Input; feedback form CDS Select/Textarea/Input/Buttons (the corner trigger is a CDS Button); coin/dice windows CDS Buttons + Chips; prize counter `IconAction`s; theme picker CDS Chips with a texture swatch icon (`.companion-theme__chip-fill[data-style-preview-theme]`); player name a ghost Button + CDS Input; Resolve and the checkup actions CDS Buttons (tinted by condition); resume list Buttons + a delete that asks first. Still ours (CascadeDS gaps on the fix list): board slots, coin, die, the card picker and Energy picture tiles, the damage tray's steps.
- **Deck lists (built 2026-10-11, `supabase/tcg-decks-m1.sql`):** a player's own Pokémon TCG lists in `tcg_decks` (owner RLS; `cards` jsonb of `{count, name, set, number, section, cardId}`; legality and "you own N" are worked out on read, never stored). Pure rules `src/lib/tcg/deckList.ts` (tests `npx tsx scripts/test-tcg-decks.ts`, which also reads every guide in `content/decks` and checks each heading's count against its table): `parseDeckText` reads Pokémon TCG Live / Limitless pastes (section headers, `4 Pikachu ex ASC 57`, name-only lines, basic Energy spellings merged, unreadable lines returned as `skipped`), `SET_CODES` maps set codes to Scrydex expansion ids (`cardIdFor` → `me2pt5-57`), `checkDeck` = 60 cards, 4 of a name (basic Energy exempt), 1 ACE SPEC, a Basic Pokémon, marks H/I/J (`STANDARD_MARKS`, an older printing with a Standard reprint passes); unmatched cards make it "check by hand", never illegal. `exportDeckText` writes TCG Live's format (basic Energy as `SVE n`), `parseGuideTables` turns a guide's tables into lines. Server `src/lib/tcg/decks.ts`: matching = local catalog first (0 credits), Scrydex lookups only when saving (`spend`, max 30 per paste), then a local name match; ownership from `gs_user_cards` (Pokémon by card id, Trainers and Energy by name); free accounts keep `FREE_DECK_LIMIT` (3), GS Pro unlimited. API `/api/tcg/decks` (GET list, POST paste or `{guide}`), `/check` (no save, rate limited), `/[id]` (GET, `?cards=1` = `{name, cards}` for the companion, PATCH, DELETE). Surfaces: My Stuff ▸ My Collection ▸ **Deck lists** (paste → checks → name → save; a deck window with status, Play in the companion, Copy for TCG Live, rename, delete, what you own with a TCGplayer link), **Save to my decks** on every deck guide (`SaveGuideDeck`; signed out = sign in and come back), and the companion: `?deck=<id>` gives this phone's player that deck (`StartDeck` in CompanionPage), the new-game settings have a deck Select per player (`DeckChoice`; Play together: only your own seat), and the card picker lists the deck's cards first ("From {deck}"). Decks are kept per phone for the game (not saved with it). Without the table the tab says "coming soon".

### Theming (Marketing vs App Split)
- `src/lib/theme/app-routes.ts` — `APP_ROUTE_PREFIXES` + `APP_ROUTE_PATTERNS` + `isAppRoute(pathname)`. A public landing inside an app namespace (`/mod/join`, reached signed out) goes in `PUBLIC_EXCEPTIONS` so it stays light (without it the signed-out navbar Sign up button lost its contrast)
- **Marketing routes** (everything else) → forced `data-theme="light"` regardless of cookie/OS preference. Consistent brand for visitors
- **App routes** (auth-gated surfaces) → cookie + OS preference honored. User can theme via `/account?tab=profile` → ThemeToggle
- Middleware writes `x-pathname` header on every request; root layout reads it via `headers()` and branches
- `<RouteThemeSync>` client component (in root layout body) re-applies the right theme on client-side navigation — React doesn't reconcile `<html>` attribute changes after hydration, so this imperatively touches `document.documentElement`
- Theme cookie: `gs-theme` = `'light' | 'dark' | absent`. Absent = follow OS via `prefers-color-scheme`
- CDS keys its dark-mode rules on `html.dark` class — root layout writes both `data-theme` attr AND `dark` class
- `globals.css` has overrides for CDS components (chip, skeleton, datepicker indicator) whose dark variants use primitive tokens (`--gray-800` etc. that don't flip between themes) — without these, marketing pages leak dark styling for dark-OS visitors
- Adding a new auth-gated route? Add to `APP_ROUTE_PREFIXES` (or `APP_ROUTE_PATTERNS` for dynamic-segment cases)

### Brand Theming (customer-facing channel identity)
- **Personalization principle (the rule for where themes apply):** GameShuffle stays **brand-themed** for platform surfaces (marketing, the `/account` app chrome, the `/communities` hub). **Any surface ABOUT a user or something they created wears THEIR theme** — profile (`/u`), their community (`/c/[slug]`), stream (`/live`), hosted tournaments, overlay. Single source of truth: **`getOwnerThemeVars(ownerUserId)`** in `src/lib/theme/owner-theme.ts` (server-only) → a `CSSProperties` of `--brand-*` (their brand-theme preset) **plus `--profile-accent`** (their personal accent, `src/lib/profile/accents.ts`) when set; spread it on the surface's root `<main style>`. Guarded/additive. New owner surfaces should apply it.
- Separate from the light/dark split above. A streamer picks a **brand theme** on the **Theme** tab; it re-skins their customer-facing surfaces only (OBS overlay, public `/live`, public profile `/u/[username]`, community `/c/[slug]`) — NOT the account dashboard.
- `src/lib/theme/brand.ts` (client-safe) — `BrandTheme` presets (built on the wheel palettes) + `brandCssVars(theme)` → `--brand-primary / --brand-accent / --brand-gradient / --brand-on`. `--brand-ink` (globals `:root`, flips lighter under dark) is the contrast-safe brand color for *text* on neutral surfaces.
- `src/lib/theme/brand-server.ts` (server-only) — `getBrandThemeForOwner(userId)` / `getBrandThemeForCommunityId(id)`; reads `gs_communities.brand_theme` (migration `supabase/brand-theme-m1.sql`).
- Surfaces apply it by setting `--brand-*` on a `display:contents` root wrapper (custom props inherit even to `position:fixed` overlay pieces). CDS primary CTAs adopt the brand by remapping `--bg-primary` / `--text-on-primary` per surface.
- `'default'` = the site brand (emits no overrides), so the feature is purely additive. Foundation for a planned personalization + trust-&-safety layer — see `specs/gs-pro-updates/gs-personalization-trust-safety-spec.md` (Cloudflare R2 `gameshuffle-ugc` bucket provisioned for future UGC).

### Wheel Spinner (free tool + Pro overlay)
- Shared rendering in `src/lib/wheel/` — `geometry` (slice math), `themes` (color themes + `FillStyle` solid/gradient/stripes/dots), `color` (`shade()` helper); `src/components/wheel/WheelGraphic.tsx` draws it. `WheelStylePicker` is shared by both surfaces.
- **Free tool** — `/wheel-spinner` (`WheelSpinner.tsx`): client-only, rAF-driven idle spin + spin, Web-Audio tick sounds, localStorage. Listed on the `/tools` hub.
- **Challenge wheel presets** (`src/data/wheel-presets.ts`, 10 wheels incl. Halloween game night + Spooky Mario Kart, labels ≤16 chars so a slice never truncates): "Load a ready-made wheel" on the free spinner and "Start from a preset" on the Pro Wheels tab (opens in the editor). `WheelGraphic` labels run across the slice up to 6 slices and along the radius past that (they collided on crowded wheels).
- **Pro overlay** — data layer in `src/lib/wheels/` (`types`/`store`/`spin`); streamer wheels in `WheelsTab`, spun from the Hub or `!spin` / `!wheel` (`src/lib/twitch/commands/{spin,wheel}.ts`), rendered by `WheelOverlay` on `/overlay/[token]`. Theme + fill style snapshot onto each spin so the overlay matches the creator. Tables: `gs_wheels`, `gs_wheel_entries`, `gs_wheel_spins` (migrations `supabase/wheels-m1/m2/m3/m4.sql`).
- **Winner announce is deferred to the overlay** — `!spin` records the spin but does NOT announce (chat would spoil the result before the wheel lands). When `WheelOverlay` finishes animating, `OverlayClient` calls `/api/twitch/overlay/[token]/announce-spin`, which posts the winner to chat exactly once (atomic `gs_wheel_spins.announced_at` claim, owner's-latest-spin only). Hub-triggered spins announce the same way. Caveat: the announcement requires the overlay to be loaded.
- **Overlay Layout placement** — the wheel is a positionable Apps piece in `OverlayLayoutTab`; on the live overlay it reads `placementStyle`/`isPlacementEnabled` (`.gs-wheel--placed`), defaulting to centered so untouched layouts are unchanged.

### Polling (GS Pro) — cross-platform live polls
- **One poll object, many surfaces.** A community runs one OPEN poll at a time; whether it's created from the dashboard, Twitch, or Discord it's the SAME poll, and every vote path feeds one derived tally. Spec: `specs/gs-pro-updates/gs-polling-spec.md`.
- **Engine:** `src/lib/polls/{types,store}.ts` (service-role) — `createPoll`/`openPoll`/`closePoll`/`castVote`/`tally`/`getOpenPollForCommunity`/`sweepDuePolls`. Option ids are 1-based strings so `!vote 3` maps straight through. Opening a poll closes any other open one in the community. Tables `gs_polls` + `gs_poll_votes` (`supabase/gs-polls.sql`; realtime-published).
- **Authoring:** Polls tab (`PollsTab`, Streaming ▸ Live games) + APIs `/api/polls` (list/create) & `/api/polls/[id]` (GET tally, PATCH open/close). Pro-gated, community-scoped. Optional auto-close timer → `/api/cron/polls-sweep` (every minute).
- **Viewer voting:** `/live` card (`LivePollCard`, anon `useAnonViewerId` key, `/api/polls/[id]/vote` — per-IP in-memory rate limit + `/api/polls/community/[id]` read); Twitch `!poll`/`!vote` (`src/lib/twitch/commands/polls.ts`, registry-driven, votes keyed by `gs_identity`); Discord `/gs-poll` open/close + button voting (`src/lib/discord/commands/polls.ts`, guild→owner→community, Pro+Manage-Server gated — **slash command needs `npx tsx scripts/register-discord-commands.ts`**).
- **Overlay:** `PollOverlay` is a placement-editable overlay piece (Tools palette); the `/overlay/[token]/latest` payload carries the community's open poll + tally, rendered via `placementStyle`.
- Follow-ons: shared rate-limit store (Upstash) for scale, Supabase Realtime instead of interval polling on `/live`+overlay, live-updating the Discord poll message on each vote.

### GameShuffle Originals (our own games) — Secret Deal engine
- **Console games on a live night** (`NIGHT_GAMES` in `src/lib/nights/games.ts`): Mario Kart 8 Deluxe/World, every Mario Party, Smash, plus the couch-multiplayer Kirby Air Riders, Mario Kart 64, GoldenEye 007 and Perfect Dark (placements only, no card deck; each behind its flag). Online-only games (Splatoon, Overwatch, Marvel Rivals) stay out.
- **Activities** are games in a live night's lineup played on phones + the TV instead of a console: `NIGHT_ACTIVITIES` in `src/lib/nights/games.ts` (`kind: "activity"`, unit `round`). They appear automatically in the lineup picker, game-night modules, "Add a game" and seasons, and finish through `recordResults(..., "activity")` so they count on the night scoreboard, MVP and season like any game.
- **Secret Deal engine** (`src/lib/party/activities.ts`, schema `supabase/party-deals-m1.sql`): `party_deals` (each seat's private payload per round + one `seat_index null` table row with the answer/phase) and `party_votes` (generic one-vote-per-voter-per-topic, reused by later vote-and-reveal games). The server filters every view; **during an activity the host sees only their own seat** (unlike missions).
- **Odd One Out** (first Original): rules in `src/lib/originals/oddOneOut.ts` (pure, client-safe), word packs in `src/data/originals/odd-one-out.ts` (our own lists, no titles/brands). Holiday packs (Odd One Out and Most Likely To "Halloween") carry `seasonal: true`: picked by name only, never dealt by Mixed. Phone UI `OddOneOutPanel`; actions `oo_round/oo_vote/oo_reveal/oo_guess/oo_judge/oo_finish`.
- **TV view** `/party/[code]/tv` (`PartyTvView`, chrome-free): always requests `?view=tv`, which forces the public view even on the host's laptop.
- **Pass-the-phone** version at `/game-nights/tools/odd-one-out` (companion tool, no account, no DB) with a "play on everyone's phones" button that starts a live night.
- **Hidden Agendas** (second Original) ride on missions: `AGENDA_CARDS` in `src/data/originals/agendas.ts` (ids `ag-`, a Mario Kart deck + an any-game deck). Host deals one per player (`agenda_deal`) or turns on auto-deal per game (`agenda_auto`, `party_nights.config.agendas`). `canSee` keeps them secret even on open nights and from a playing host until claimed or until the game's results are saved (the reveal, also on the TV); confirmed agendas score as `party_points.source = 'agenda'`.
- **Most Likely To** (third Original, first on the vote-and-reveal engine): rules `src/lib/originals/mostLikely.ts`, prompts `src/data/originals/most-likely.ts`, phone `MostLikelyPanel`, actions `ml_round/ml_vote/ml_reveal/ml_finish` (topic `likely` in `party_votes`, no per-seat deals). Anonymous votes (counts only), self-votes allowed, +1 for matching the room's pick. `activityView`/`runActivity` dispatch by slug. One-device version at `/game-nights/tools/most-likely-to`.
- **Tier Wars** (fourth Original, vote-and-reveal): rules `src/lib/originals/tierWars.ts`, topics `src/data/originals/tier-wars.ts` (our text topics + every Tier List Maker template, art included). Everyone ranks 6 items S–D (`tw_vote` ballot must rank all), the reveal builds the room's tier list (mode, tie → nearest the average), +1 per item matching the room, "hottest take" called out. Phone `TierWarsPanel`, TV renders the room's tier rows.
- **The Gauntlet** (live-night format): `party_nights.config.format = "gauntlet"`; builder at `/game-nights/tools/the-gauntlet` (`GauntletBuilder`, 4–8 events from `NIGHT_GAMES`, ordered with `SortableList`). Phones, TV and recap say "Gauntlet champion" instead of MVP; scoring is the normal night scoreboard.
- **Who Said It?** (stream game, Pro): `!whosaid` / `!whosaid reveal` (`src/lib/twitch/commands/whosaid.ts`) on the polls engine (`gs_polls.kind = 'whosaid'`, `answer_option_id`). Speakers come from `!quote add <text> - Name` (`parseSpeaker` in `src/lib/originals/whoSaid.ts` → `gs_default_command_responses.said_by`). Schema `supabase/whosaid-m1.sql`.
- **Wheel of Consequences**: `src/data/originals/consequences.ts` (handicaps for the winner, perks for last place, ids `wc-h-`/`wc-p-`, wheel labels ≤13 chars). Host `consequence_spin` once the next game has started (`consequenceTargets` = last finished game's winner/last place); dealt as player-scoped rule cards, cleared by `next`. The TV plays the overlay `WheelOverlay` (seeded by the card row id via `wheelFor`, scoped `.party-tv .gs-wheel` CSS).
- **Call It**: host switch (`party_nights.config.calls`); players `call_set` the current console game's winner (`party_votes` topic `call`, round 1); a right call is +2 night points (`rightCalls` in `nightPoints`) and `party_points.source = 'call'` for accounts (card_row = the call id).
- **King of the Couch**: one crown per host's group (`supabase/crowns-m1.sql`: `party_crowns` + `party_crown_changes`). Rules in `src/lib/originals/crown.ts` (`decideCrown`: first game claims it; if the holder plays and someone else wins, the winner takes it; a holder win is a defense; CPUs never hold it). Judged in `recordResults` via `applyCrown`, once per game (`last_game_id`). Shows on the phone table list, TV scoreboard and recap.
- **Draft Night** (activity `draft-night`): rules + rosters in `src/lib/originals/draft.ts` (MK8DX/MKW characters, every Mario Party roster, Smash, Kirby riders, Overwatch and Marvel Rivals heroes with portraits; each behind its game's flag). Host `dn_start` (roster, 2–5 picks); snake order; `dn_pick` by whoever's on the clock; `dn_auto` after the 45s clock (any phone may call it, the server checks). The last pick ends the draft and marks the game done; `draftPools` feeds a `pools` field on every later view (phones + TV). Pools last the night (no season tables).
- **Chaos Cup** (live-night format `chaoscup`, Mario Kart): modifiers in `src/data/originals/chaos-cup.ts` (`cc-i` item rule, `cc-r` race setting, `cc-l` leader handicap). Host `cc_roll` (offer stored at `party_nights.config.chaosOffer`), then `cc_pick` or `cc_vote` (a community poll via `startChaosVote`; `resolvePartyVote` deals the winner). `dealChaos` puts it in play as a rule card for that race (leader handicap → top of tonight's scoreboard). Builder at `/game-nights/tools/chaos-cup`.
- **Shuffle Dice** (prototype, noindex): rules `src/lib/originals/shuffleDice.ts`, one-device tool at `/game-nights/tools/shuffle-dice` for playtesting before a live/chat version.
- **The Daily Shuffle** at `/daily` (`DailyShuffle`, rules `src/lib/originals/daily.ts`): guess today's character in 6. **Rotates by weekday** (Pacific days: a new puzzle at midnight Pacific, `dayKey` = `gsDay`) in dated eras (`ROTATIONS`, `rotationFor(day)`): MK8DX Sun/Mon (and Thu until Oct 8, 2026), MK World Tue/Fri, Mario Party (Jamboree roster) Wed/Sat, Smash Ultimate Thu from Oct 15, 2026 (`smash-fighter`: series, first Smash, debut year, weight class, third party; facts + clues in `src/data/originals/daily-facts-smash.ts`, clues drafted by `scripts/draft-daily-clues-smash.ts`). **Only ever append an era with a future start day**: answers count runs day by day, so a past day never changes. A **starter clue** gives one broad column free before guess one (`starterFor`, `TraitDef.starter`; share text "Started from …"). Dev only: `/daily?day=YYYY-MM-DD` previews a day. **Hero puzzles (built, not scheduled):** `overwatch-hero` (role, species, origin with same-continent close, affiliation, released within 1 year) and `rivals-hero` (role, species with Human~Mutate~Mutant close, team, comic debut, joined season), facts + clues in `src/data/originals/daily-facts-heroes.ts` (object order is the roster: never reorder or insert), portraits as `img`, `reveal: "blur"` (portraits fill their square, so a blurred look replaces the silhouette). Per-puzzle `noun`, `closeNote`, `arrowNote`; per-trait `within` / `near`. Dev only: `/daily?puzzle=<id>` plays an unscheduled puzzle and saves nothing. Scheduling = append an era with a future start day. Each guess fills a row of **checked facts** (`src/data/originals/daily-facts.ts`, 75 characters researched on Super Mario Wiki Oct 2026; typed columns in `PUZZLES`, reworked 2026-10-12 because Species and First series read like trivia: MK8DX = weight class (ordered, ↑ heavier), **Crew** (`CREWS` in the facts file: Mario's crew, Bowser's crew, Kongs, Wario & Waluigi, Guests, Everyone else; edge calls Lakitu/King Boo/Monty Mole with Bowser, Birdo/Pauline with Mario, Mii a guest), **First Mario Kart** by name (`firstKart(year)`, ordered, one game apart = close), **Variant** (Baby, Metal, Pink Gold, Tanooki, Cat, Dry Bowser, Peachette), debut year; MK World swaps Variant for **Home series** (`home` in the facts); Mario Party = Crew, **Mario Party games** (`PARTY_GAMES_PLAYED`, kind `count`, within 2, more/fewer; Jamboree has NO character Dice Blocks, everyone rolls 1-10, so the approved Dice Block column was replaced), **First Mario Party** by name (`firstParty(year)`), in Superstars, debut year. The free starter skips a value fewer than 4 characters share (`STARTER_MIN_SHARED`) and a starter that returns null. Cells are match / close (years within 3, ordered columns with `within`) / miss with ↑↓ arrows (`compareTrait`). Break mode's fact games key on these labels (`src/lib/break/games/facts.ts`), so a renamed column needs its entries there. A clue per puzzle per character (`src/data/originals/daily-clues.ts`, `clueFor`; drafted 2026-10-12 by `scripts/draft-daily-clues-by-game.ts` from checked fact sheets: Mario Kart history per racer, Mario Party games + Jamboree Buddy abilities, Smash fighter numbers + Final Smashes, then read through; the facts file's shared `clue` is only a fallback) unlocks after guess 3 and the answer's silhouette on the last two guesses. **Never reorder or filter a roster**: the order feeds the answer shuffle. Each puzzle walks its own seeded shuffle (the nth run of that puzzle → the nth character, no repeats until the roster cycles). Puzzle numbers count days from `DAILY_EPOCH` 2026-09-29. Signed out, progress + streaks live in localStorage (`gs-daily-shuffle`); signed in, `/api/daily` saves one row per account per day in `daily_results` (`supabase/daily-results-m1.sql`; `puzzle` = the day's puzzle id; one result per day whatever the puzzle, and streaks span every game). The server re-judges the guesses (`judgeGame`) so a result can't be made up; the first result per day stands; stats + streak are derived (`statsFrom`), never stored. Profiles show a Dailies section (streak cards per daily, see below). Share text uses Wordle-style squares.
- **The Weekly Challenge is now usually a Chat Brain survey** (`supabase/weekly-survey-m1.sql`): `ensureWeek` claims the oldest family-safe Chat Brain draft and opens it for the week (`kind = 'survey'`, `prompt_id`), falling back to a Tier War when the queue is empty; staff swap the question in Platform ▸ Weekly (`scheduleSurvey`). Players give their answer (into `brain_answers`, source `weekly`, changeable via `upsertUserAnswer`) and up to three optional guesses at the top answers (0-3; none is stored as null, the column allows 1-3 or null, so "locked in" = an answer saved). **One answer per person:** the week's question is also an open Chat Brain question, and answers are filed under an account (Weekly, site) or a chat identity (Discord, Activity), so `personKeys`/`personAnswer` in `src/lib/chatbrain/store.ts` look under the account plus every linked identity: `submitAnswer` refuses a second answer, `upsertUserAnswer` changes the one that exists wherever it was given, and the Weekly (web, Activity, Discord form) prefills an answer already given in Chat Brain (`answeredInBrain`); Monday's `revealWeek` uses the staff-published board or groups and publishes it automatically (Claude, else spelling), then `scorePredictions` scores each guess on the board. The game-night agenda stays as a bonus. Details of the original Tier War version follow.
- **The Weekly Challenge** at `/weekly` (`WeeklyChallenge`; rules `src/lib/originals/weekly.ts`, store `src/lib/weekly/store.ts`, schema `supabase/weekly-challenge-m1.sql`): one challenge for everyone per week, Monday at midnight Pacific (`weekOf` = `gsWeekStart`; `revealAt` = Monday midnight Pacific). (1) An online **Tier War**: signed-in players rank six items S–D (`POST /api/weekly`); the crowd's ranking stays hidden until the next Monday, when `revealWeek` scores 1 per item placed in the crowd's tier (same rule as a live Tier Wars room) and ranks everyone (ties share a place). (2) A **shared agenda**: `ensureWeekly` (party extras) makes every live night's weekly mission the site's any-game agenda (`shared: true`, no reroll); table-confirmed completions (`party_points` ref `weekly:…:<week>`, matched on `card_id`) add +3 at the reveal. Picks are automatic and deterministic (`autoPick`, a fixed shuffle of topics with ≥6 items and the 12 any-game agendas); staff swap them on **Platform ▸ Weekly Challenge** (`/api/admin/weekly`; next week any time, this week only before anyone plays). The reveal is lazy (first read after Monday, claimed open → revealing) plus `/api/cron/weekly` (Mon 00:10 Pacific: scheduled 07:10 and 08:10 UTC, the off-hour run finds nothing to do), which also posts to Discord servers that route the opt-in `weekly` category (`postAnnouncementToCategory({ requireRoute: true })`, claimed via `discord_posted_at`). Top-10 weeks show a "Weekly top 10" badge on `/u` (`topTenFinishes`). **In Discord** (`src/lib/discord/commands/weekly.ts`, free): `/gs-weekly` (Manage Server posts the card publicly, else ephemeral; the Monday cron posts the same `weeklyCardMessage` with Play / Last week buttons). Play needs the Discord user's linked account (`users.discord_id`, else a sign-in-with-Discord link): survey week → a 4-input modal prefilled from the entry (`weeklym:{week}` → `saveSurvey`); Tier War week → an ephemeral Components V2 message (6 string selects + Lock in) whose ballot rides in the custom ids (`weeklyt:{week}:{item}:{ballot}`, one char per item, `-` = unranked; `weeklyts:` → `saveBallot`), so nothing is stored mid-ranking. `{week}` is the week number, guarding a ranking that crosses Monday. **Run `npx tsx scripts/register-discord-commands.ts` to add `/gs-weekly`.** Homepage module `HomeToday` (the dailies + the Weekly). Help: `/help/apps/weekly-challenge`. Stream version (`!weekly`, overlay card) is planned for later.
- **Number Bingo** (activity `number-bingo`, the couch version of Stream Bingo): rules `src/lib/originals/bingo.ts` (75-ball cards, `bingoLine` checks a claim against the numbers actually called). Cards are per-seat deals; the host `bg_call`s numbers (big on the TV with a called board), players mark their own cards (marks stay on the phone), `bg_claim` is verified server-side; rounds won become placements. Win **patterns** (`PATTERNS`, `patternHit`): any line, four corners, the X, picture frame, blackout, or a series that steps through them (`seriesPattern`); the host picks per round.
- **Stream Bingo** (GS Pro, the stream version): engine `src/lib/bingo/stream.ts` (service role) on `stream_bingo_games` (one open per community; pattern, series step, `called int[]`, timer, token prize + the streamer's own prize text, winner) + `stream_bingo_cards` (one per viewer per game), schema `supabase/stream-bingo-m1.sql`. Streamer runs it from the **Bingo** tab (Streaming ▸ Live games, `StreamBingoTab`, `/api/bingo` + `PATCH /api/bingo/[id]`) or chat (`!bingo start [pattern|series] [tokens] [prize]` / `call` / `auto <30-600>|off` / `end` / `status`, routed inside the existing `!bingo` board command by `STREAM_BINGO_SUBCOMMANDS`; viewers `!bingocard`). Viewers take a card + claim on `/live` (`LiveBingoCard`, `/api/bingo/community/[id]`, `/api/bingo/[id]/card|claim`; Twitch sign-in via `AuthPromptModal`; marks stay in the browser). A claim is re-checked server-side; the status flip is the lock; tokens mint through `awardMint` (the streamer's allowance; a shortfall doesn't undo the win) and the win posts to chat. **No cron:** `tickAuto` makes timer calls on the reads the overlay and /live already poll, guarded on `last_called_at` so racing readers can't double-call. Overlay piece `number_bingo` (`NumberBingoOverlay`, in the latest payload). Help: `/help/streaming/stream-bingo`. **Doors and custom patterns (2026-10-10, `supabase/stream-bingo-m2.sql`):** three steps said the same way on the streamer's tab, `/live` (`.bingo-steps`, the viewer's current step highlighted) and the overlay: open the hall, call numbers, first real bingo wins. A game opens with a sign-up window (`doors_close_at`, default 2 minutes, 30s-10min or none): cards only while the doors are open (`doors_closed` after), no calls until they close (`doors_open`); the first read after closing claims the moment (`last_called_at` null → the closing time) and posts "the doors are closed" to chat once; the timer starts then. "Close the doors now" (`PATCH { action: "doors" }`, `!bingo doors`). Countdown on the overlay ("The bingo hall is open"), `/live` and the tab. **Custom patterns:** `stream_bingo_patterns` (per community, max 20, names unique whatever the case, at least two squares besides the free centre; `cleanCells`), drawn in a 5×5 modal on the tab (`/api/bingo/patterns` POST/DELETE), picked as `custom:<id>` or by name in `!bingo start`; a game copies the squares (`pattern_cells`, `pattern_name`, pattern `custom`) so editing a pattern never changes a game; claims use `cellsHit`. `BingoShape` draws any pattern small (overlay, /live, tab).
- Free at the table; Pro later for chat-as-a-player, overlay reveals, custom packs.

### The dailies (Daily Shuffle, Daily Dex, Weekly), October 2026 release
- **Daily Dex** `/daily-dex` (`DailyDex`, `src/lib/originals/dailyDex.ts`): guess today's Pokémon from all 1,025; 5 guesses + a last chance that shows the first letter and a square per letter; arrows on generation/height/weight; its own streak. Puzzle #1 = `DEX_EPOCH` 2026-10-16 (in `daily.ts`, never move it). Facts `src/data/originals/daily-dex-facts.ts` (generated by `scripts/build-daily-dex.ts`, append-only), nods `daily-dex-nods.ts` (keep ~90 days ahead; Platform ▸ Originals counts), Pikachu first comes on day 564. Kept out of `PUZZLES` (bundle size, break mode). Results share `daily_results` with puzzle id `pokemon-dex`; every reader filters by game (`ofGame`). Pokémon imagery rule: `TypeCard`/`TypeTile`, no art. Tests `npx tsx -r ./scripts/server-only-shim.cjs scripts/test-daily-dex.ts`.
- **Leaderboards** (`src/lib/daily/boards.ts`, `guests.ts`, `boardRules.ts`, `DailyBoard.tsx`, `supabase/daily-boards-m1.sql`): day/week/month boards per daily; points 10/8/6/4/2, last-chance save 1; tokens per point + week/month prizes (levers `daily_*`), guests join with a name (tokens held 30 days for an account to claim). Players `u:`/`i:`/`g:`. **Where they played is on the picture** (2026-10-19, picks https://claude.ai/artifact/WEZM2GLEQWxbLA4hDtgCaQ): `PLACES` in `DailyBoard.tsx` leaves signed-in site players (`u:`, tag null) a plain picture (no badge: the filled bubble already says it; a GS badge was tried and dropped, Britton 2026-10-19), gives Discord Activity players (`i:`) a Discord badge (CDS Avatar `badge`), and guests (`g:`) a dashed ring; a key under the board names only the marks it shows, and screen readers hear "(on GameShuffle)" / "(played in Discord)" / "(guest)" with the name. No text tags beside names any more (they squeezed names to three lines on phones). Britton sees it as a race between the places people play: Facebook (the Instant Game) becomes one more `PLACES` entry once its players are told apart from site guests. Phones also give the name room: 8px dots, 6px cell padding, "Pts".
- **Together** (`src/lib/originals/dailies.ts`, `Dailies.tsx`, `DailiesCards.tsx`, `/api/dailies`): a today bar on each daily page (CDS SubNav pills: **Shuffle, Dex, Fire Round** as 3 cells, each cell's colour from `--dailies-c1..3` set inline because SubNav items take no class; since 2026-10-19 the **Weekly is a plain line under the bar**, `DailiesWeekLine` + `weeklyLine()`: "N days left" / "new this week" / "locked in · results Monday", not on the Weekly's own page), Next up on end screens (Shuffle → Dex → Fire Round → Weekly), "Copy today's results" (🟪 Shuffle · 🟧 Dex · 🟥 Fire Round `· 1,240 · 14 right` · 🟩 Weekly) + "Copy just this one", streak cards on end screens and a profile Dailies section (Fire Round's: day streak, best, played, top score; days marked `played`). Fire Round is never in the Discord Activity (`dailiesShown(current, { fire: false })`, the Activity's `onPick` type leaves it out; `dailiesView(…, { fire: false })`). Each game reports itself to localStorage `gs-dailies`; signed in, `/api/dailies` fills in. A new daily = a `DAILIES` entry + reporting. Activity: the Daily tab plays both (`pickDaily`), channel results card + morning podium per daily (`CardGame` themes in `src/lib/discord/cards.tsx`, `supabase/dailies-discord-m1.sql`), `activity:dex` button.
- **Solve times** (2026-10-16, `supabase/daily-times-m1.sql`, `DailyStart.tsx`): the first hint and guess box wait behind **Start today's puzzle** (`useDailyStart`, also in dev previews); Start POSTs `/api/daily/start` (account, else the hashed guest id from `x-daily-guest`; the Activity maps it to `/api/activity/daily/start`) and `markStart` keeps the first Start per player, game and day in `daily_starts`. `recordScore` stores `daily_scores.seconds` (Start to save, capped at 2 days); `timesFor` = today's time, best solved time, count of timed solves (`times` on `/api/daily` GET/POST). The end screen says "Solved in 0:42 · your fastest yet / your best is 0:31" (`DailySolveTime`, `pickTimes`: the server's time, else this browser's from localStorage `gs-daily-starts`), shares carry " ⏱ 0:42" when solved (`shareText(…, seconds)` and the combined line), streak cards show Fastest, and the day board shows a Time column and ranks players level on points by solved time (`gs_daily_board`); week/month boards, points and tokens ignore time. Before the SQL runs everything falls back: no server times, no Time column.
- **Guess feedback:** a toast per guess (`guessFeedback`) and the new row lights up and scrolls into view. The guess box (`.daily__guess`) sticks under the nav inside the game, styled like Fire Round's bar, so nobody scrolls back up for the next guess (Britton, 2026-10-21); rows keep a scroll margin so a new row never lands under it, the Discord Activity offsets it under its pinned tabs, and with a mouse (`pointer: fine`) the cursor goes back into the box after each guess (not on touch screens, where it would raise the keyboard over the new row). The Dex's list is A to Z and shrinks to what still fits (`stillFit(puzzle, hints, ["measure"])`: height/weight left out on purpose, the exact rule gave the answer away by guess 2-3; `scripts/.tmp/dex-narrow-sim.ts`), with a Show all switch; the Shuffle's list is A to Z, all 130. Touch screens get 16px fields site-wide (end of globals.css) so iOS doesn't zoom on focus.
- **Help with tokens** (`src/lib/originals/dailyHelp.ts`, `src/lib/daily/help.ts`, `DailyHelp.tsx`, `supabase/daily-help-m1.sql`): on the last chance, letters (up to 3, two always hidden; the Shuffle's first gives letter + length) for 100 and extra guesses (up to 2, bought before the last guess) for 250; levers `daily_help_*`. `gs_daily_help_buy` locks the wallet and records purchase + `daily_help` spend together. `judgeGame(..., extra)`: saves look up bought guesses (`u:` site, `i:` Activity). A solve with help is a save (1 point). Share text adds 💡/➕. Guests are offered an account.
- **Invite links** (`src/lib/referrals/store.ts`, `src/lib/originals/referral.ts`, `supabase/daily-referrals-m1.sql`): each account has a fixed 6-character code (`referral_codes`); shared results carry `?r=CODE`; a new player finishing pays the referrer 100, signing up 500 more, the friend +250 (`earn_referral`, levers `referral_*`); once per person, first link wins, never your own account/network, 10 paid a day, 3 new per network a day. Friend's note above the game, Invite card on end screens, Account ▸ Invite friends. Tests `scripts/.tmp/referrals-e2e.ts`.
- **Card of the day** (`DexCardOfDay.tsx`, `/api/tcg/showcase?dex=`): after a Dex game, the answer's showcase TCG card (`SHOWCASE_CARDS`), Add to My Cards + our TCGplayer store. Keep cards ~90 days ahead: `npx tsx -r ./scripts/server-only-shim.cjs scripts/populate-pokemon-showcase.ts --dex-days 90 --run` (dev), then `--warm --run` for production. Website only until a Discord URL mapping for images.scrydex.com exists.
- **Yesterday's top 5 video** (2026-10-16, picks https://claude.ai/artifact/FZdGXncTrRMXTQGv5x6SPE, all as recommended; `supabase/daily-winners-m1.sql`): `/social/winners` (staff only in production, default day = yesterday Pacific) plays one video for both dailies: intro, each daily with at least `MIN_FINISHERS` (3) solvers counted down from 5 to the crowned winner (only solvers, board order: points then time; staff and admin accounts are left out and places renumbered, `staffPlayers` + `renumberPlaces`, since they see answers ahead; they stay on the boards), its answer (Shuffle art, Dex type tile), guess dots, solve time and streak, then "Make today's top 5". Each game's colors: the Dex's warm ground fades in over the Shuffle's violet. Rules `src/lib/originals/winners.ts` (timeline, caption), data `src/lib/daily/winners.ts` (`dayStandings` + `solvedDays` for streaks the way streak cards count them, opt-outs show as "A player"), stage `WinnersStage.tsx` + `social.css` (`.winners-*`). Britton screen-records it on his phone from the start screen (Copy caption, Play); `scripts/.tmp/rec/social-winners.cjs <day> <outDir> [mask]` records 1080×1920. On the dev server `?rows=` (base64url JSON of rows per game) stands in for the boards, so a day read from production can be filmed locally. The 9am Pacific job (`/api/cron/discord-daily-summary`) sends accounts in the video a `daily_top5` note (Daily games group), once per game and day (`daily_winner_notes`). Settings ▸ Privacy ▸ "Show me in winners videos" (`daily_winner_optouts`, under the leaderboard switch; hidden until the SQL runs). Privacy Policy mentions the posts.
- **Tool moment videos** (2026-10-17, plan + picks https://claude.ai/artifact/EvFLS4Qbf4FJHcjKKDVsbq, all as recommended; download page https://claude.ai/artifact/2FW1DfqVdnjau7DHbH7P7a): short 9:16 posts of our randomizers and free tools tied to something happening that day, made in weekly batches. `/social/clip` (`ClipStage.tsx`, `.clip-stage` in social.css; staff only in production) frames a same-origin page in a phone window (rendered at 390px, or `?pw=` 320-430 to show it bigger, and scaled, site chrome hidden, so the CSP's `frame-src` includes 'self'), with a hook on top and a pill at `cta_at`: the window shrinks up and the pill lands under it (Britton, 2026-10-19: nothing covers the page). The recorder adds a tap dot (`finger`, scrolls its target into view first), captions in the hook's place (`caption`), styles inside the page (`css`) and Fire Round turns (`fireTurn`); the Fire Round announce video is clip `fire-announce` (answers from `scripts/.tmp/fire-announce-prep.ts`). `scripts/.tmp/rec/social-clip.cjs <id> <outDir>` films it from the definitions in `scripts/.tmp/clips/defs.cjs` (both gitignored): it seeds localStorage, drives the page through the iframe (tap, chip, Select option, type, glide, roll until, wait for), and a take is kept only when the real result fits the post (`accept`); nothing is rigged.
- **Phone layout (2026-10-19, picks https://claude.ai/artifact/YPqRyCB9giAgygW69z2HTk, all recommended):** a solved Shuffle went from 5.5 phone screens to about 3.3, the Dex from 6.2 to 3.6 (footer included). `BrowseHero compact` + `subShort` (phones only: less padding, a 26px curve, one line under the title; `.daily-page` tightens the page). How to play is three short lines inside the Start card (`DailyStartCard` takes children, `DailySteps` is a list now) and the parade keeps one lane on phones. The result: the answer beside the verdict (`.daily-result`), Copy and `DailiesNextButton` on one row (`.daily-actions`; the Weekly still uses the `DailiesNext` card), this game's streak only as a strip (`DailiesYours only`, `DailiesCards strip`), one sign-in line (`DailiesInvite nudge={false}`), the guest's "Put me on the board" inside the leaderboard (`DailyBoard join`, its Turnstile appears once a name is started), the support line at the bottom, Chat Brain without its eyebrow (`eyebrow={null}`). The Dex's Card of the day is smaller on phones and its credits replace the fan-made line at the page foot (`DexCardOfDay credits={false} onCard`). These content changes apply at every width; only the header, spacing and parade are phone-only. Since 2026-10-20 the end screen's buttons (`.daily-actions`, Fire Round's result actions, the Card of the day's two buttons) take the full width one per line on phones instead of sitting at two ragged widths, and the shared result page's head-to-head spans the panel (the panel lines its children up at their own width, so a wide child needs `align-self: stretch`). Mockups were made by rearranging the live DOM (clone and hide, never move React's nodes).
- **Share images** per game in its own colors (`scripts/render-originals-og.tsx`: daily violet, dex warm, weekly teal, chat-brain magenta, plus the homepage image); headers use `BrowseHero` accents `daily`/`dex`/`weekly`/`brain`.

### Fire Round (solo trivia, built 2026-10-19)
- Picks https://claude.ai/artifact/N8ZZzJUvEkJcBf3J3eEeDT (all 11 as recommended; Britton's note: a daily round to compete on every day plus standard rounds any time, for retention). **`/daily-trivia`** (moved from `/trivia/fire-round` on 2026-10-19, which 308s here; `FireRound`, `FirePlay` in `src/components/fireRound/`, styles `src/styles/fire-round.css`, header accent and glyph field `fire`), short link `/fire`. Flag `FIRE_ROUND_PUBLIC` (the dailies' bar and Next up, the homepage Today cards, the /trivia Play solo card, "Or play them solo in a Fire Round" on each `/trivia/questions/<topic>` page with `?category=`, which preselects More rounds when that category has questions, the dailies' How it works, the sitemap).
- **Beside the dailies (2026-10-19, picks https://claude.ai/artifact/WhFgrMQv8oVHQ4e5cvfXRx, all recommended):** the page opens with the today bar (Fire lit; the bar hides while a round runs), today's round in the dailies' Start card (`DailyStartCard` takes `head`, `label`, `note`, `primary`, `className`; `DailySteps` icons `type`/`heat`/`board`), then More rounds, any time (flat). `FireRound` reports itself to the dailies (`reportDaily("fire", …)` from `fireHome`, which now returns `history`: streak, best, played, top, recent) and signed in `/api/dailies` adds it (`fireDailiesEntry` in the store). Results: `DailiesShare` (combined line), the streak strip, Next up via `DailiesNextButton`. Copy aimed at search ("daily trivia" 12,100/mo, "daily trivia quiz", "multiple choice trivia", "rapid fire questions"): title "Fire Round: free daily trivia quiz", eyebrow "Daily trivia · A GameShuffle Original", six FAQ questions (the categories answer links to the question pages), breadcrumb JSON-LD Home › Trivia › Fire Round (`MarketingJsonLd` breadcrumb `parent`). Share image = `fire` in `scripts/render-originals-og.tsx` (motif `choices`). Production's categories on 2026-10-19 were the 12 pack ones (no Mario Kart, Smash or Pokémon until game packs are approved), so copy names only those.
- **Winners video:** Fire Round is the third part of `/social/winners` from round #1 (`WinnersGame` includes `fire`; rows carry `score` and `bestStreak`, `guesses` = right answers; the stage shows the score with a trophy, "N right", and the best streak with a bolt; each later game's ground fades in over the first, `.winners-warm` per section). Its notes need `supabase/fire-round-m3-winners.sql` (adds 'fire' to `daily_winner_notes`' check); until then they're skipped quietly.
- **Rules** `src/lib/originals/fireRound.ts` (pure; tests `npx tsx scripts/test-fire-round.ts`, ~2,700 checks): lengths 1/3/5 minutes or Until 3 misses (a miss or a skip costs a heart); typed right 100, right with the four choices open 40, heat ×1.5 from 3 in a row and ×2 from 6 (`heatFor` on the streak before the answer), wrong or skip 0 and the heat resets; a close typed answer (`judgeAnswer` says ask) opens the four choices for a second chance; `boardPoints` = 1 per 200 scored, at most 10. Categories `FIRE_CATEGORIES` (pack categories, picture rounds, and game categories from the game packs' `game_slug`), shown from `MIN_CATEGORY` (12) playable questions. **`FIRE_EPOCH` (round #1) is 2026-10-20: move it to the real launch day before release; never after.**
- **Engine** `src/lib/fireRound/store.ts` (server; `supabase/fire-round-m1.sql`): the server holds the clock and the answers. `loadPool` = approved, family-safe pack questions that can show four choices: word answers need `trivia_questions.wrong_answers` with `wrong_status` approved; numbers get theirs by rule (`numberDistractors`), pictures from their roster (`rosterDistractors`); cached a minute. `todaySet` claims `fire_days` once a day (60 questions so even a speedrun can't run out, difficulty mix, no category twice in a row; only the first 20 of each recent day's set count as used, and the last 30 days are skipped while the pool allows), the same for everyone in the same order. Today's round is always 1 minute. `startRun` (today's round: the first one a player starts today `counts`, unique index; later ones are practice), `act` (typed / choice / choices / skip / stop, version-guarded on `fire_runs.version`), `finish` (today's counting round goes on the daily boards through `recordFireScore`; guests only once they've put a name on the boards, then `boardTodayFor`), `fireHome`, `sweepFireRuns` (the polls sweep ends runs whose clock ran out). Free play lines up the category's questions the player hasn't had in 30 days first (`fire_seen`). Picture questions load through `/api/fire-round/pic/[id]` (the file name is the answer). Players: the account, else the dailies' guest id (`x-daily-guest`, `src/lib/fireRound/player.ts`); a guest who signs in brings their runs (`moveFireRuns` from `claimGuest`). APIs `/api/fire-round` (GET home, POST start / board) and `/api/fire-round/[id]` (GET, POST moves).
- **On the daily boards:** `BoardGame` includes `fire` (`daily_scores.score` keeps the round's score, `guesses` its right answers; `gs_daily_board` ranks by points, then score). `DailyBoard game="fire"` shows Right and Score on the day, points over a week or month; tokens and week/month prizes as the dailies. The winners video stays on the two dailies (`WinnersGame`).
- **Staff:** Platform ▸ Trivia ▸ **Four choices** (`FireChoicesPanel`, `src/lib/fireRound/choices.ts`): Add the starter wrong answers (`src/data/originals/trivia-pack-wrong.ts`, written for every word-answer pack question, filled in as drafts; Add the starter pack does it too), Draft wrong answers with Claude (30 at a time), approve or edit (`updateWrong` refuses a wrong answer the judge would accept), and how full each category is against ~100. Header **Draft for a category**: Claude drafts new questions with their wrong answers (`draftCategory`), all drafts to check. Analytics: `Fire Round Started` / `Finished` / `Shared`.

### Daily Duel (launched 2026-10-22, picks https://claude.ai/artifact/5fdauqQ8MPof41oWXYcuPk, both rounds all as recommended)
- The idea (Britton): Pokémon blended with Yu-Gi-Oh: the trainer of the day duels you with the Pokémon they used in the games, as cards that summon their Pokémon (the card's art as a hologram); rules cut to six lines; events from the games and anime crash the duel; a random six that always has an answer; "how fast can you solve it" counts too. Full elimination (knock out all six), real Energy (one a turn from the day's row, put on any card, it stays), Weakness ×2 only, numbers only (card text is flavor), events about every third turn with the next one shown a turn ahead, no coin flips, same pack/row/events for everyone, practice only after the official duel, easy Monday to hardest Sunday, boards by turns vs par, then cards left, then time. Later (GS Pro): duel with your own cards, chat duels on stream.
- **Engine + server (built 2026-10-21; `supabase/daily-duel-m1.sql` on dev + prod).** Trainer of the day `trainerFor` (tier by weekday: Mon/Tue gym, Wed/Thu elite, Fri rival, Sat boss, Sun champion; rotating by person without repeats, see Bigger roster below). Code in `src/lib/originals/duel/`: `types.ts` (shared), `engine.ts` (pure; `applyStep` takes `{next}` or `{attach, act}`; `viewOf` shows only this turn's Energy and the next 3, this turn's event and the next; 13 events plus the trainer's signature; turn 40 is a loss), `solver.ts` (beam search, width 300; `buildDay` accepts a pack only when it's beatable, the obvious play loses, and par sits in `PAR_BAND` (gym 4-9, elite 7-12, rival 9-14, champion 12-18); packs strength-matched to the team with `packCap`; a trainer with no puzzle hands the day to the next in its tier), `rules.ts` (`DUEL_EPOCH` 2026-10-22, #1 Larry; never move it; `duelPoints`: par or better 10, then 8/6/4/2, a loss 0; `duelShareText`, squares only), `copy.ts`, `view.ts` (DuelHome, DuelRunView, DuelPlayView), `store.ts` (`duel_days` snapshots each day's cards and trainer so it never changes; `duel_runs` one official run per player and day, version-guarded; practice only once the official duel is over; boards via `recordDuelScore`: turns in `guesses`, cards left in `score`, time in `seconds`, so the existing ranking is the duel's). APIs `/api/daily-duel` and `/api/daily-duel/[id]` (404 in production while `DUEL_PUBLIC` is off). Days build themselves: the every-minute `/api/cron/polls-sweep` calls `ensureDays()` (today and tomorrow, built if missing, so midnight and the homepage card, which only reads a built day, never wait on a player); a request for a missing day also builds it (`getDay`). A build takes about a second. `npx tsx -r ./scripts/server-only-shim.cjs scripts/build-duel-days.ts --days 14 [--dry]` builds or previews days on the `.env.local` project; `scripts/.tmp/duel-days-sql.ts <out>` writes days as SQL inserts for another database. Tests `npx tsx -r ./scripts/server-only-shim.cjs scripts/test-daily-duel.ts` (104); dev end to end `scripts/.tmp/duel-api-e2e.ts` (25), `duel-board-e2e.ts` (10). Rule calls: Energy can go on the card Team Rocket holds (only swapping to it is refused); sleep and the stubborn ace stick to that card; weather replaces weather; Revive brings back your knocked-out card with the most printed HP at half HP; the Energy row is weighted by how many of the six need each type. Dailies entry `duel` (gold on navy, 🟨), share kind `duel`, `DailyBoard game="duel"`. Events `Duel Started` / `Duel Finished` / `Duel Practiced` / `Duel Shared` (Plausible goals needed).
- **Data (built 2026-10-21):** 74 trainers in `src/data/originals/duel/trainers.ts` (`DUEL_TRAINER_PLANS`: species in battle order, `owner` = the name on the trainer's own cards, `pin` hand picks; card ids from `team-picks.generated.ts`), each team checked on Bulbapedia via Wayback snapshots with a source comment: 30 gym, 23 Elite Four, 10 rivals, 11 Champions; Grass-starter versions; Brock left out (no gym team of 3-4). `pool.ts` (`DUEL_POOL`, 283, APPEND ONLY): English, one print per species, 60-340 HP, about 16% ex, no trainer's own cards and nothing on a team. `cards.generated.ts` (`DUEL_CARDS`, 556) from `tcg_cards` via `scripts/build-duel-cards.ts`; rules in `cardRules.ts` (Fairy plays as Psychic, "130+" counts as 130). `scripts/populate-duel-cards.ts`: plan by default, `--run` picks (trainer's own card > illustration rare > holo; only picks stored), `--pool`, `--only`, `--warm [--run]` loads every card into another database (production at release, 1 credit per missing card). Dev spend 242 credits. Tests `npx tsx scripts/test-duel-data.ts` (~7,500). Never change a pick or reorder the pool once a day has used it.
- **Page (built 2026-10-21):** `/daily-duel` (`DailyDuel`, `src/components/originals/duel/`: DuelStart, DuelArena, DuelBits, DuelHolo, DuelEvent, DuelZoom, DuelEnd, duelLook.ts; styles `src/styles/daily-duel.css`; in the sitemap and indexed since launch; share image `duel` in `scripts/render-originals-og.tsx`). Start card (trainer, both teams as zoomable `CardThumb`s, the trainer's own event, Start = the official duel and its clock, three `DailySteps` with icons `energy`/`move`), then the arena: one dark world in any theme; phones stack it, from 1024px it's side by side (trainer left, you right) so a turn fits a laptop screen. A turn = place the Energy (Give button or tap a waiting card; "Move it" until sent), then a move / swap (tap a waiting card, then confirm, so a mis-tap can't cost a turn) / Wait. The trainer's moves show under their card with the damage they'd do; Professor's advice marks the next one. `framesBetween` animates the server's answer (knockout frame 800ms, hits from HP drops, summons from field changes; reduced motion skips it). Focus returns to the picker heading or this turn's Give button, and an aria-live line reads the turn. The clock shows only on the official duel (server start time). Result (`DuelEnd`): one boxed panel (turns, par, time, cards left, both sides), Share (`DailiesShare` / `ShareButton` with `duelRun`), Practice (no board, points or streak), Next up, You vs them from a friend's `/r` link (`ChallengeVersus` kind `duel`; `/r/<code>` opens today's duel under the friend's card), streak strip, board, Add these cards to My Cards (`/api/tcg/collection`), our shop, support line. The today bar has four cells (`.dailies-bar--4`: colored top border on phones, 2×2 below 360px); `DUEL_SHOWN` follows `DUEL_PUBLIC` (on since launch); the Discord Activity leaves the Duel out (`duel: false`). Events also `Card Collected {from: daily-duel}`, `TCG Store Clicked {from: daily-duel}`. CDS Buttons need `height: auto` for multi-line tiles (CDS fix list). The engine logs "heals 0" for a signature heal at full HP; the page shows "is already at full HP".
- **Lighter turns (built 2026-10-21, picks https://claude.ai/artifact/VL1NYSQ6NLRwxmMymXQq1y, all as recommended; Britton's note: a taller hologram).** Today's Energy starts on your card on the field (a white-ringed dot, `EnergyDot fresh`), so most turns are one tap: a move, Wait or Swap sends `{attach, act}`. Tap a waiting card to put the Energy there instead; tap it again or press "Back to {card}" in the Energy row to bring it back. Swap is a toggle beside Wait (`[data-swap]`, aria-pressed): the cards that can come in glow (`.duel-thumb--swap`) and one tap swaps; Swap again, Cancel or Escape leaves it. Move buttons show name, damage, cost dots and ×2 only (the reasons live in aria-labels); attacks sit on top (one alone spans the row), Wait and Swap below. The middle is a scoreline (`scoreRows` in duelLook.ts: one row per move with ×2/KO badges, Asleep/Stubborn, "Swap in X" and the sandstorm; Energy, waits, cards coming out and event lines are left out), the full log behind a "Turn log" IconButton (CDS Modal), the event banner (title plus `DuelEventView.short`, or `eventOutcome` when the event did nothing), short status chips with full sr-only sentences, a one-line "Next turn" chip and the Energy row (icon dots, "on {card}", the next three as rings). Every Energy dot carries its type's icon (`TYPE_ICON`, TypeCard's set). The picker says one line ("Against Whiscash · weak to Grass · 140 HP"); turn 1 shows one hint under the buttons. Exchanges play as beats (`duelBeats.ts`: your move's name on your hologram and its damage popping on theirs, their next card, their reply, then the server's own copy; 650ms, a knockout 850ms); scoreline rows appear with their beat, a tap on the arena or Enter/Space/Escape skips to the end, reduced motion has no beats, and screen readers hear the whole turn in full sentences once the beats end. Log lines carry optional `move`, `type`, `why`, `amount` and `ev` fields; older stored lines read from their text. The hologram is the card's art window at 16:10 on phones, 2:1 on tablets and 16:9 side by side; at 390px a typical turn is about 1010px tall. Scratch scripts: `scripts/.tmp/rec/duel-dry.cjs` (plays today's line), `duel-built-shots.cjs <out> mobile|desktop`, `duel-interact.cjs [reduced]` (23 checks), `duel-measure.cjs`, the video `duel-video.cjs` (a scripted Escape key hides the page in headless Chrome: close windows with their button).
- **Bigger roster and Boss day (built 2026-10-21, picks https://claude.ai/artifact/GPbG7XLVeDeTJfkCGd1pTg, all as recommended; pass 1).** Saturday is Boss day (`PAR_BAND.boss` [10, 15], `MAX_EX` 2, `START_SLACK` 50, `TIER_LABEL` "Boss", fallback champion, elite, rival, gym). The rotation goes by person (`personOf` = `DuelTrainer.person ?? personKey(name)`): days of a tier walk a seeded shuffle of its people (`duel-people:<tier>:<cycle>`) with no repeats until everyone has had a day, and on cycle c a person brings team `c % teams` in roster order (add a person's later teams after their earlier ones), so combos show one team per visit. Persons are per tier (Giovanni is a gym person and a boss person). 148 trainers (74 added: 43 gym incl. the Alola kahunas, 7 elite, 3 champions, 10 rivals, 11 bosses), each team checked on Bulbapedia via Wayback (source line per entry). Gym teams are the first Gym battle in the game named, 2 to 5 Pokémon; Blueberry Academy's Elite Four bring 6; Shauna 3 and Brendan/May 4 stay rivals; Giovanni, Archie, Maxie and Lysandre use their Team Rainbow Rocket teams (their own games give 4). Pins give Falkner, Whitney, Roxanne, Cilan and Katy harder-hitting prints (the obvious play beat the gentle ones). Team picks never take a `DUEL_POOL` card; pool fill in `populate-duel-cards.ts` is opt-in (`--fill`); the script saves its picks when it hits `--max`. Sweep: `scripts/.tmp/duel-sweep3.ts <tier>` (3 days per trainer); pin lookups `scripts/.tmp/duel-search.ts <name> [--keep ids]`. `duel_days.tier` allows `'boss'` (daily-duel-m1.sql drops and re-adds the check, re-runnable; applied on dev 2026-10-21). 156 Scrydex credits on dev. Pass 2: second teams per game (combos), pool growth; World Tournament teams later.
