- **Combos, week guard, bigger pool (built 2026-10-21, same picks; pass 2).** The rotation never puts one person on two days less than `PERSON_GAP_DAYS` (7) apart across tiers: each weekday tier is a stream through its people and a person due inside a week of their last day in any tier is held back until it's clear (rules.ts explains it; buildDay's stand-ins keep the guard through `peopleNear`; checked over 400 days with `scripts/.tmp/duel-schedule56.ts <start> <days>`). 133 second teams from remakes and rematches (FireRed/LeafGreen, HeartGold/SoulSilver, Let's Go, Emerald, ORAS, Platinum, BDSP, Black 2/White 2 without the World Tournament, USUM, Sword/Shield Champion Cup, Scarlet/Violet Academy Ace Tournament), each a separate entry with the person's `person` key, appended after their earlier teams and checked on Bulbapedia via Wayback like the rest: 280 entries (gym 143 for 76 people, elite 68 for 35, rival 28 for 20, boss 17 for 11, champion 24 for 14; `scripts/.tmp/duel-roster-stats.ts`). Cheren's Black 2/White 2 Gym team was left out (60 HP Patrat and Lillipup can't make a puzzle); Brock's FireRed team is pinned to a 70 HP Geodude and 120 HP Onix. The pool grew to 450 (48 Grass, 44 Fire, 50 Water, 44 Lightning, 48 Psychic, 48 Fighting, 42 Darkness, 40 Metal, 38 Dragon, 48 Colorless; 16% ex), append only. About 262 Scrydex cards stored on dev. Sweep (3 days per entry): elite, rival, boss and champion all in band; gym 12 of 432 days at par 10 (kept as the closest), 5 days with no puzzle handed to a stand-in (Lt. Surge and Cilan once each, Brock before the pin).
- **Trainer pictures (built 2026-10-21, picks https://claude.ai/artifact/4YeQz1MVRnuBLkSHmkMCmp, all as recommended).** Each person's picture is the art window of their own Supporter card (regular print), else their ace's card art (the last card of the team on screen), keyed by person so every team shares it: 118 people with a card, 29 with their ace. Data: `src/data/originals/duel/trainer-cards.ts` (`DUEL_TRAINER_CARDS`: card id, name, set, illustrator, hand-set `face` [x, y, zoom] in the art window; `{ card: null }` = ace; `SUPPORTER_ART` art windows per era measured on Scrydex images; `ACE_ART` per-ace windows and faces, full-art aces use `FULL_ART`) and `trainer-art.generated.ts` (each image as Scrydex returned it plus its measured shape, written by `build-duel-cards.ts`, which downloads about 150 images from images.scrydex.com per run to measure them, no API credits). Cards stored by `populate-duel-cards.ts --trainers --run` (1 credit per card not yet stored; 119 on dev); `--warm` loads them too. Card choices: Korrina uses Furious Fists (Korrina's Focus has a Battle Style mark), Cyrus uses Cyrus's Conspiracy, Crown Zenith holos (autographs across the art) swapped for earlier prints (Leon, Nessa, Raihan, Hop, Bede, Bea), Lorelei gets her ace (she's only on Misty & Lorelei), Bruno's only regular print shows his back; illustrators from the Pokémon TCG API, 16 read off the card's "Illus." line. `src/lib/originals/duel/pictures.ts`: `trainerPicture(trainer, ace)` (stored days of any age get the current picture; an unknown person gets the ace), `pictureCredit`, `pictureAlt`, `artCrop` (the crop rule; `TrainerArt` + `src/styles/duel-art.css` do the same sums with container units: `banner`, `round`, `tile`). `DuelTrainerView.picture` (the day isn't re-stored). Shown on: the start card banner (alt names the card; side by side on wide screens), a round portrait in the trainer's bar, the "Next turn" chip and the event tile for their signature event, a large portrait above the result, the share card (wide panel, tall banner, cut with sharp from images.scrydex.com only; `DuelPart.picture`, older shares find it by name) and so the `/r` preview, the morning winners video (the Duel as a fourth section once public: board order, the banner, "Against X · Par N", rows of turns, cards left, time and streak from `duelWonDays`, wins only; dev rows via `?rows=` with `duel`; the 9am notes come from the existing code) and the homepage Today row's Duel card (`DUEL_SHOWN`; `duelDayTrainer` reads a built day, never builds one). Credit at the foot of `/daily-duel` and duel `/r` pages (`DuelPictureCredit`: "Bertha's picture: Bertha's Warmth (Rising Rivals), illustrated by Ken Sugimori."). "Add these cards to My Cards" adds the trainer's card. Faces checked on contact sheets (`scripts/.tmp/tp/sheets.ts <out> art|round|banner|all [person,…]`); shots `scripts/.tmp/rec/duel-pics-shots.cjs <out> mobile|desktop`, `winners-duel-shot.cjs`.
- **Hard but findable (2026-10-22, Britton: "I found myself actually having a hard time, and I feel like that's the appeal").** `buildDay` also needs `lookaheadPlay` (solver.ts: a player who reads one turn ahead, trying every Energy spot and every move, wait and swap, keeping the one that does the most for the least, and sending out the card whose first turn scores best after a knockout) to win from at least `FAIR_LEADS` of the six leads: 2 on gym days, 1 otherwise (`fairLeads`, `DuelDayBuild.fair`). Packs that fail count as too hard (the strength cap loosens); when no trainer in the order has a findable pack, any beatable pack is taken. #1 Larry was findable from 5 leads and is unchanged; 9 of the next 14 days built before the rule were findable from none (only a near-perfect line won). Stand-ins: Kukui (Nov 1) and Poppy (Nov 4) had no findable pack. `build-duel-days.ts` prints "findable from N"; scratch checks `scripts/.tmp/duel-replay.ts <day>` (the winning line turn by turn) and `duel-fair-before.ts`.
- **The challenger (built 2026-10-22, picks https://claude.ai/artifact/RqcHdCgb2M4wxnLVs1NJ2E, all as recommended; Britton: no "New challenger" badge, the headline says it).** "Larry challenged you to a duel" on the pages that lead into the Duel (`src/components/originals/duel/DuelChallenger.tsx`, styles `src/styles/duel-challenger.css`, the Duel's navy and gold in every theme): `ChallengerBand` leads the homepage's Today in place of the Duel's small card (server data from `duelChallenger(gsDay())`, never builds a day), `ChallengerStrip` sits under the today bar on the Shuffle, Dex and Fire Round pages (`DailiesBar`; `GET /api/daily-duel?view=challenger`, public, cached a minute), and the Duel page's start card is the challenger card (picture to the edges, the headline, title and game, the bio, both rows of cards, the trainer's own event, Battle now with swords via `DailyStartCard icon`). Once today's official duel is over (the band reads this browser's own record, `useTodayDuelResult`; the strip reads the dailies), both show "You beat Larry in 11 turns" and `tomorrowLine` ("Tomorrow: a rival", by tier, never a name). Next up on the other dailies' end screens puts the Duel first while it's unplayed (`nextUp`). Bios: `src/data/originals/duel/bios.ts` (`DUEL_BIOS` by person, one sentence from the games, fitting every role a person has; the first three weeks' 21 trainers so far, the rest of the 147 to write), `DuelTrainerView.bio`. Event `Duel Challenger Clicked` {from, played} (Plausible goal needed). Still to build from the same picks: a daily share image with the challenger, yesterday's solve (next day only, Britton's call) with game-written why-lines, controls, the first turn you split from par, and a morning solve video. Idea from Britton: a page of every trainer (a glossary).
- **Card text that plays, and clean prints (2026-10-22, after a player expected Kyogre's Dual Splash to work).** The duel plays printed numbers only, so attacks with no printed damage were dropped while still showing on the card. Two fixes, both for days not built yet (a built day keeps its own copy of its cards): (1) `textDamage` in `cardRules.ts`: an attack whose whole text is "This attack does N damage to 1 / 2 / each of your opponent's Pokémon" (or the old "Does N damage to each Defending Pokémon"), the reminder in parentheses aside, plays as an N hit on the card in play; any condition, cost, coin or bench-only target stays out. (2) `cleanAttacks` (every attack plays) ranks first in the picker's `score` and the pool's `prefer`, and `populate-duel-cards.ts --clean [--run] [--max]` re-picked every unclean team slot and pool card: stored cards first, else one search (151 credits): 226 team slots and 92 pool cards swapped, 8 pool cards with no clean print left (442 now). Kept on purpose: 4 pinned slots, the aces of the 8 trainers whose picture is their ace (Clair, Drayden, Valerie, Juan, Lenora, Burgh, Viola, Lorelei: hand-framed art), and 3 slots with no clean print anywhere (Luvdisc twice, Galarian Slowbro). 15 slots lost the trainer's own card (Gym Heroes era prints whose attacks are effects). Of the 225 cards that had dropped attacks, 187 dropped attacks were effects with no damage (draw, heal, conditions): a later round could give the engine simple effects. Scratch check `scripts/.tmp/duel-dropped.ts`.
- **Launch (2026-10-22):** `daily-duel-m1.sql` applied on production; `DUEL_EPOCH` 2026-10-22 (#1 Larry, Elite Four, par 9); `DUEL_PUBLIC` on (today bar's fourth cell, homepage Today card, sitemap, share cards, the morning winners video from #1's morning). Card rows aren't loaded into production's `tcg_cards`: the game reads its own data files, and Add these cards to My Cards fetches each card from Scrydex the first time someone adds it (`populate-duel-cards.ts --warm --run` with production's env would pre-load them, up to 1,126 credits). Still Britton's: the Plausible goals (`Duel Started` / `Finished` / `Practiced` / `Shared`). Dev's stored days from Oct 22 to Nov 3 were built before roster pass 2 and still carry number 0 for Oct 22-27 (the API counts the number from the date, so pages show the right one). Later: GS Pro duel with your own cards, chat duels on stream.

### Halloween release (built 2026-10-19, for Oct 23)
- **Halloween party games** `/game-nights/halloween-party-games` (picks https://claude.ai/artifact/PLfj9v7sXez1gyswTAxR49; `src/app/game-nights/halloween-party-games/page.tsx`, styles `src/styles/seasonal.css` for seasonal pages, in `HERO_ROUTES` and the sitemap, share image from `render-page-og.cjs`): one row panel of eight free games, each opening on its Halloween set (truth or dare, Halloween party bingo, the Halloween game night wheel via `/wheel-spinner?preset=halloween-game-night`, Halloween trivia, Odd One Out and Most Likely To via `?pack=halloween`, Halloween Treats tier list, the Spooky Mario Kart wheel), a plan for the night, who each suits, FAQ (FAQPage + breadcrumb JSON-LD). The trivia row links the Halloween question page once 12+ Halloween questions are approved, else /trivia. Deep links added: the free wheel reads `?preset=<id>` over the saved wheel (`WheelSpinner`), Odd One Out and Most Likely To read `?pack=<id>`.
- **Halloween trivia pack** `src/data/originals/trivia-pack-halloween.ts`: 100 questions (category `Halloween`, 39 easy / 49 medium / 12 hard, a source each, US spelling) plus `HALLOWEEN_WRONG` (three wrong answers per word answer), folded into `TRIVIA_PACK` and `PACK_WRONG`, so Platform ▸ Trivia ▸ Add the starter pack + Four choices bring them in as drafts. Checked by `scripts/test-fire-round.ts` and `scripts/test-trivia-rules.ts` (every right answer judged correct, no wrong answer judged correct). Question page `/trivia/questions/halloween` (topic with `categories: ["Halloween"]`, `max: 50`).
- **Seasonal categories** (`src/lib/trivia/seasons.ts`, `SEASONS`, `categoryInSeason`): a holiday pack plays only in its season (Halloween = Oct 1 to 31, Pacific) and stays out of everything mixed the rest of the year: Fire Round (the `halloween` category has `season`; `inCategory(MIXED, q, day)` leaves out-of-season questions out of Mixed and today's round; an out-of-season category link plays as Mixed), trivia night round filling (`pickForRound`, unless the round is titled Halloween/spooky: `roundCategories`), break-mode trivia and the Discord Activity practice rounds. Christmas gets a `SEASONS` entry when its pack lands.
- **Tournament browse fix**: `src/lib/events/search.ts` kept only 30 days of finished tournaments and sorted them oldest first, so after the first fetch the All and Completed tabs emptied (dev) or hid results (prod) while the tab counts, from `loadTournamentRows`, counted a year. Now tournaments read in two parts like the loader: coming (open + in progress, soonest first, stale left out) then a year of results (`RESULTS_DAYS` 365, newest first); the keyset cursor carries `seg` (`cursorSeg` on `/api/events/browse`). Paging check: `scripts/.tmp/browse-paging.ts`.

### Christmas set (built 2026-10-19, for mid-November)

Question page `/trivia/questions/christmas`; the holiday topic pages' "Play as a Fire Round" use their category (`FIRE_CATEGORY`).

### Game trivia packs (built 2026-10-19, picks in the search/trivia round)
- Six packs of 100, written by Claude to the house style and approved on Britton's say-so (he has no time to review): **Pokémon** (`trivia-pack-pokemon.ts`, tag `pokemon`), **Mario Kart** (`-mario-kart.ts`, `mario-kart`), **Super Smash Bros.** (`-smash.ts`, `super-smash-bros`), **Super Mario** (`-mario.ts`, `mario-series`: plain `mario` would prefix-match every Mario Kart and Mario Party slug), **Zelda** (`-zelda.ts`, `zelda`) and **Kirby** (`-kirby.ts`, `kirby`), each in `src/data/originals/`, category `Video games`, about 34 easy / 50 medium / 16 hard, three wrong answers per word answer (`*_WRONG`, folded into `PACK_WRONG`). `PackQuestion.gameSlug` carries the tag into `trivia_questions.game_slug` (`importPack`). Each pack avoids repeating the main pack's questions and the other packs' (checked by hand plus the duplicate-prompt check). Names and facts only, no art (Pokémon imagery rule).
- **Where they play:** Fire Round's game categories (`pokemon`, `mario-kart`, `smash`, new `mario` (mushroom), `zelda` (triangle), `kirby` (stars); a category shows once it has 12 playable questions), the question pages (`/trivia/questions/pokemon`, `mario-kart`, `smash`, `mario`, new `zelda` and `kirby`, retitled with "Quiz" where search volume favors it: Pokémon 33,100 "pokemon quiz", Mario 390, Zelda 320 either way, Kirby and Smash quiz; Mario Kart and Nintendo keep "trivia"), and break mode's **Game Trivia**, which now also draws a series pack while any game in the series is on stream (`SERIES_PACKS` in `gameTrivia.ts`: e.g. `super-smash-bros` for `super-smash-bros-ultimate`, `kirby` for `kirby-air-riders`). Break mode's general **Trivia** now leaves game-pack questions to Game Trivia.
- **Topic pages:** a question with a `game_slug` counts only on pages that list it (or the broad Video games page), so the Mario page isn't Mario Kart; untagged questions still match by keyword. Pages take questions from each game in turn (`topicQuestions`), so Nintendo and Video games mix the packs. `max: 50` on the pack pages.
- **Checks (scratch, gitignored):** `scripts/.tmp/pack-check.ts <pack>` (counts, wrong answers, duplicate prompts, every right answer judged correct, no wrong answer judged correct; an "ask" only opens the choices), `pack-dev-import.ts <game_slug>` (import + approve on dev), `pack-dev-prune.ts <game_slug>` (drops dev rows a pack edit replaced), `pack-prod-sql.ts <pack> <out> <part>` (25-row inserts for production through Supabase, already approved). Break-mode chat ignores a lone W, L or F and answers over 40 characters, so `test-break-games.ts` fails on those: keep answers chat-typable.
- **Production** gets all seven new packs (Christmas + six) approved at the next release. That takes the pack past 1,000 questions, which is why the paging fix below must ship with them.

### Welcome page (built 2026-10-19)
- Picks https://claude.ai/artifact/2ch3gzGozTx2TZuZSQ8zj4 (all 12 as recommended). `/welcome` (`src/app/welcome/page.tsx`, styles `src/styles/welcome.css`, indexed as "What is GameShuffle?" with FAQPage + breadcrumb JSON-LD, share image from `render-page-og.cjs`): one sentence, then **six doors** in the hero (a quick game today, game night at home, playing online with friends, I stream, I run tournaments, Pokémon TCG) that jump to `#today` `#home` `#online` `#stream` `#tournaments` `#pokemon`. Each section: eyebrow, heading, one line, three things, one button, and a **live demo**: the homepage hero demos (`KartDemo`, `SmashDemo`, `DailyDemo`, `PollDemo`, `BracketDemo`… now exported from `HeroShowcase.tsx`) through `WelcomeDemo` (drawn finished until scrolled to, then plays once, Play again; reduced motion stays at rest), and the real `CounterTool` for Pokémon. Sections alternate sides from 900px (`welcome-path--flip`). **Numbers** (`src/lib/welcome/numbers.ts`, 5-minute cache): people who played today's dailies, tournaments running, public game nights this week, each hidden below its floor (10 / 1 / 2). End: "A free account adds" (signed out), `BackingPair from="welcome"`, four questions (`FaqList`).
- **New accounts land here:** `/signup` sends email and OAuth sign-ups to `/auth/callback?redirect=/welcome?new=1` unless a `?redirect=` was given (`signupRedirectSuffix`; `/welcome` is on the redirect allowlist). `welcomeViewer()` (`src/lib/welcome/viewer.ts`): an account made in the last 7 days gets **Three first steps** with ticks (pick your games = `favorite_games`, a picture or gamertags, today's Daily played); an older account arriving with `?new=1` (signed in through the sign-up page) is sent to `/account`. Dev only: `?preview=new` shows any signed-in account the steps.
- **Doors in:** `NewHereLine` (`src/components/welcome/NewHereLine.tsx`): a flat `aside` line under the tool on every randomizer (inside `RandomizerNudge`), the three dailies and deck guides, signed-out only, once per browser (localStorage `gs-welcome-line`, set once it has been on screen, closed or followed). Also "New here? Start here" in the footer (Company), the signed-out phone menu, and the homepage hero's `note` (new `BrowseHero` prop). Events `Welcome Door Clicked` {door}, `Welcome Path Started` {door}, `Welcome Line Clicked` {from} (Plausible goals needed). Later (his picks): remember the door someone picks, a 30-second tour video.

### Share cards (one-tap sharing, built 2026-10-19)
- Picks https://claude.ai/artifact/9JxKKcAJj1L8m5pZgqg7kD (all 10 as recommended). Every result's "Copy" became one **Share** button (`ShareButton`, `src/components/share/ShareButton.tsx`): the card is made as the end screen shows (POST `/api/share`, then its picture), because a browser only opens its share sheet right after a tap; Share hands the picture (tall 1080×1920 on touch screens, wide 1200×630 elsewhere), the squares text and the link to `navigator.share`; where there's no sheet (or it fails), `ShareWindow` (CDS Modal): the card, X / Facebook / Threads / Bluesky / WhatsApp / Reddit intents, Discord (copies), Copy text, Save picture. Events `Share Opened` {game, via} / `Share Sent` {game, place} (Plausible goals needed).
- **Cards** (`src/lib/share/`: `types.ts` client-safe words + alt text, `store.ts` server, `render.tsx` next/og): `makeShare(playerKey, request)` works out the card from what the server can check: daily guesses come from the page and are judged again (`judgeGame`, up to 2 bought guesses) and must match an account's (or a board guest's) saved result (`mismatch`); single-game cards draw the trait squares (`hintFor`), the day card a square per guess; Fire Round from the server's run (`fireShareFacts`, own runs only); the Weekly from the account's entry; times, streaks and places from the server. Who: a public account's name, picture (`accountAvatar`) and background (banner from our storage behind a 70%+ dark layer, else the brand theme darkened, else the game's colors); a private profile gets the guest card; a guest shows their board name once added. Never the answer.
- **Links:** `gs_share_cards` (`supabase/share-cards-m1.sql`, on dev + prod): a 7-character code per player, kind (shuffle/dex/fire/weekly/day), day and ref (a More rounds run id); sharing again refreshes the card and keeps the link. `/r/<code>` (`src/app/r/[code]/page.tsx`, noindex) is a challenge page (picks https://claude.ai/artifact/6LYnhCNeLorPPRQkDNFE2F, 2026-10-20, all recommended): the header is "Nova got it in 2. Your turn." (`challengeTitle` / `challengeSub` in types.ts; an older link names its day, "Nova got Monday's in 2."), then the friend's result drawn on the page (`ShareFriendCard`: name, picture, game and day, result, squares, time, streak, place, their theme band, "the answer isn't on this page"), then today's game itself (DailyShuffle / DailyDex / FireRound / WeeklyChallenge / NameEveryPokemon; a day card opens the Shuffle) inside `ShareChallengeProvider` (`src/components/share/ShareChallenge.tsx`). The context makes `DailyBoard` ask `/api/daily/board?shared=<code>` (the server looks up the sharer with `sharePlayer`, so no player key reaches the page; their row says "Shared this"), adds `ChallengeVersus` to the Shuffle, Dex and today's Fire Round end screens (You vs them, same day only, ties broken by solve time / right answers) and turns the end screen's Share into "Send yours to Nova" (`useChallengeShareLabel`). The today bar waits until you've played (`DailiesBar afterPlayed`); the invite code rides along as `DailiesProvider invite` → `captureReferral(7, fromCard)`. **A card with no name never stands in a person** (Britton, 2026-10-21; a guest who hasn't put a name on the boards, or a private profile): the title and link preview lead with the result and an invitation (`inviteTitle`: "The Daily Dex #5 in 3 guesses. Can you beat it?", "7 of 151 Pokémon named. How many can you name?"), the description is the result lines (`shareLines`) without asking again, the header is the result ("Solved in 3. Your turn.", `inviteHeader`), the friend card's heading is the game with "Shared today", and You vs them says "the shared result" with a share tile; only a signed-in name or a board name is ever shown, never "Someone" or "A friend". Name every Pokémon previews don't speak of today's puzzle. Supporter cards keep their own page. Its link preview is still the card (`opengraph-image.tsx`, `twitter-image.tsx`); `/r/<code>/card?size=wide|tall[&v=][&download=1]` serves the picture. Wired into `DailiesShare` (a `one` prop: on the site one Share, or "Share today's results" + "Share just this one" once two are done; the Discord Activity keeps its own share), the Shuffle and the Dex (also for an account that played on another device), Fire Round results (today's and More rounds) and the Weekly once answered (accounts). Tests: `scripts/.tmp/share-e2e.ts` (21 checks), `share-e2e2.ts` (day card, Nova's card), `share-render-check.tsx` (every kind, both sizes).

### Trivia Nights (phase 1: the table, built 2026-10-07)
- Plan https://claude.ai/artifact/2YaGPjzngG6dDzWkxuHpBL, mockups https://claude.ai/artifact/1ZF7Fxe4D6uCyu1AewsSVH (approved). Free to host (an account), GS Pro for AI drafting; stream/Discord (phase 2) built 2026-10-11, leagues (phase 3) built 2026-10-13.
- Schema `supabase/trivia-m1.sql` (service role): `trivia_questions` (a host's library, or the GameShuffle pack when `owner_user_id` is null; answer line accept/ask_about/reject, sources, `as_of` + `changeable`, `origin` host/ai/staff, `status` draft/approved/retired), `trivia_games` (rounds frozen into jsonb at build time, live state: `round_index`, `question_index`, `phase` lobby → question → locked → reveal → standings, `closes_at`), `trivia_teams` (no account; each phone keeps the team's `secret`; `joker_round`), `trivia_answers` (one per team per question, verdict + points).
- Rules `src/lib/trivia/rules.ts` (pure, tests `npx tsx scripts/test-trivia-rules.ts`): `judgeAnswer` (an exact answer/accept wins, then reject, then answer/accept with typos, then ask → host; numbers exact; digits and Roman numerals never count as a typo, so "Elizabeth II" isn't "Elizabeth I"; letters or text for choices), `groupAnswers` (spelling groups for one-tap judging), `pointsFor` (classic/picture points, confidence = the staked number, final wager ± bet capped at 20 and the team's score, partial = half, joker doubles a round's gains), `TEMPLATES` (Party, Office, Fundraiser, Bar night). Store `src/lib/trivia/store.ts` (library, games, teams, answers, host controls, `tick` locks a question whose time is up on any read; views never include an answer before its reveal). AI drafts `src/lib/trivia/drafts.ts` (`draftStructured`, AI feature `trivia`, saved as drafts the host approves; the studio says to check sources).
- Pages: `/trivia` (join by code, templates, your nights), `/trivia/host/[id]` (builder with the question studio, then the console), `/trivia/play/[code]` (phones), `/trivia/tv/[code]` (chrome-free, on the live-night TV look). APIs under `/api/trivia/*` (`room/[code]` for phones and the TV).
- **The GameShuffle pack** (2026-10-08): starter bank `src/data/originals/trivia-pack.ts` (`TRIVIA_PACK`, 282 questions: 10 text categories + picture rounds from our own Smash fighter and Overwatch hero art; every one has an answer line and a source, changeable facts an `asOf`; the test script judges every answer/accept/reject and checks each picture exists). **Platform ▸ Trivia** (`PlatformTriviaTab`, `/api/admin/trivia`) adds it as drafts (`importPack`, dedupe on prompt + picture, rerunnable), staff approve/edit/retire; hosts only see approved ones. Builder **Fill every round from the pack** / per-round **Fill from the pack** (`fillFromPack` → `pickForRound`: regular rounds take turns across categories with an easy/medium/hard mix, picture rounds stick to the theme with the most fresh pictures, the final prefers a hard number question, lightning rounds go easy; never repeats inside a night, skips the host's earlier nights while it can; round titles map to categories via `roundCategories`). Our own pictures reach phones and the TV through `/api/trivia/pic/[code]?r=&q=` (only questions the room has reached), so the file name can't give the answer away; host-linked images pass through as is.
- Found from: nav (Community ▸ Trivia nights), `/apps` card, the game-night tools hub (registry entry `trivia` → `/trivia`), sitemap, help `/help/apps/trivia-nights`.
- **Crews at every trivia night + leagues (built 2026-10-13; mockups + picks https://claude.ai/artifact/9S5kJoWxcTHjLG4373J7iJ and https://claude.ai/artifact/3HUeqLVqr4WV95AR16Ywsp, every screen Want, every question as recommended; `supabase/trivia-leagues-m1.sql` on dev + prod).** **Joining** (`src/lib/trivia/join.ts`, `TriviaJoinChoices` on the phone, `GET /api/trivia/room/[code]?join=1`, `POST {action:"join", as}`): signed out = a team name as before (+ "Sign in to play for your crew"); signed in = your crews (`myCrews` in `src/lib/trivia/crews.ts`: communities where you're in a crew for any game, plus groups you own or mod; `canEnter` = a crew captain or the community's owner/mod), yourself (`trivia_teams.user_id`), or a new name. A crew or league team plays as one team a night (unique indexes on `trivia_teams` (game, community_id) / (game, league_team_id)); a member arriving later gets the same team and key with one tap (`insertTeam` returns the twin on a 23505), anyone else still needs the teammate link. On a league night: your league teams (captain or crew member), crews of yours to enter, a new league team, or "just tonight" (yourself or a name, no league points). **Fair play:** the phone posts `away` on `visibilitychange` during an open question (`noteAway`, `trivia_teams.away["r:q"]`, not on stream); `HostView.away` shows "left the page N×" on the console, a nudge never a penalty; paste was already off. **Leagues** (`src/lib/trivia/leagues.ts`, pure rules `leagueRules.ts`, tests `npx tsx scripts/test-trivia-leagues.ts`): `createLeague` makes a weekly game night series (`createSeries`, now carries `locationType` so online leagues' nights are online) then the league (slug from the title, settings weeks 4-12, team size, scoring place|total, best N, template); gated by `canStartLeague` = `organizerHasPaidFeatures` (free while Circuit billing is off; part of Circuit after). Points by place 10/8/6/5 then one fewer, min 1, ties share; best N nights; standings tie-break wins, best finish, nights played (level on all four share a rank). `startLeagueNight` (one open night at a time; `createGame` from the template, `league_id`/`league_week`/`event_id` = the week's game night, `fillFromPack`); the host's live route records points when a league night ends (`recordLeagueNight`, upsert; the last week sets `status finished` + `champion_team_id`, stops the series, posts to Discord's `trivia` route); `undoLeagueNight` deletes that night's results and unlinks the game (the week counts as not played; reopens a finished season). Teams: `enterTeam` (a crew: captains/owner/mods; a new name becomes a crew via `createGroupCommunity` + a Trivia crew row with the maker as captain, league-only if they already own five groups), `renameTeam`, `mergeTeams` (better result per night stays), `setCaptain` (a crew member), `removeTeam`, `leaveLeague` (captain, before playing), `archiveLeague`. Pages: `/trivia/leagues/new` (`LeagueCreateForm`, or the Circuit lock card), `/trivia/leagues/[slug]` (server-rendered in the owner's theme: next night or tonight's code, Join the league (`LeagueEnter` modal), the viewer's team cards with weeks, roster and tonight's team link (`tonightLink`), standings with ▲▼, past nights, Event + BreadcrumbList JSON-LD, Copy link / Copy for Discord; in the sitemap), `/trivia/leagues/[slug]/manage` (`LeagueManage`: Nights with Start tonight's night / Open / Results / Undo points + Print answer sheets / Print the poster / Copy standings, Teams with a row menu, Settings autosaved), `/trivia/leagues/[slug]/poster` (print-only poster with a QR to the league page). The console's final screen shows the league card (`consoleLeague`: tonight's league points, rank moves, tonight-only teams, Undo, Copy results). APIs `/api/trivia/leagues` (GET mine, POST create), `/[id]` (GET manage view, PATCH actions), `/[id]/teams` (POST enter or `tonight`, DELETE leave). `/trivia/answer-sheet?title=&code=` prints a night's name and a QR to `/trivia/play/CODE`. Also: the crew's trivia record on `/c/[slug]` (Play tab, `CommunityTrivia`, `crewTriviaRecord`; embedding leagues from teams needs the FK hint `trivia_leagues!trivia_league_teams_league_id_fkey` because the champion FK is a second relationship), "Trivia nights" on `/u` (nights played as yourself), a "Trivia league" card on each week's game night page (`leagueForNight`), a Leagues section on the signed-in /trivia. Dev season test `scripts/.tmp/league-e2e.ts` (57 checks), staging `scripts/.tmp/league-stage.ts` (Tuesday Trivia at Pine Street on dev, two weeks played, week 3 open).
- **Pages for search (built 2026-10-13, plan in the round's mockup https://claude.ai/artifact/9S5kJoWxcTHjLG4373J7iJ):** copy and lists in `src/data/trivia-seo.ts` (`OCCASIONS`, `QUESTION_TOPICS`, `LANDING_FAQ`, `TRIVIA_CHECKED`), metadata via `triviaMetadata()` (`src/lib/trivia/seoMeta.ts`: restates the folder's share image `src/app/trivia/opengraph-image.jpg`), styles `src/styles/trivia-seo.css`. `/trivia` adds server-rendered visitor sections below the join box for signed-out visitors (`TriviaLanding`: steps, features, a card per occasion, tools, FAQ; SoftwareApplication + FAQPage + HowTo JSON-LD). Occasion pages `/trivia/[occasion]` (office-trivia-night, party-trivia, fundraiser-trivia-night, bar-trivia, trivia-on-stream; `TriviaArticle` with Article/FAQPage/Breadcrumb JSON-LD, the template's rounds, sample questions from the approved pack), `/trivia/how-to-host`, free tools `/trivia/team-names` (`TeamNameGenerator`, names in `src/data/trivia-team-names.ts`, test `scripts/test-trivia-team-names.ts`) and `/trivia/answer-sheet` (`AnswerSheetMaker`, print CSS shows only the sheet), both on the /tools hub. **Game question pages** `/trivia/questions/[topic]` + index (`src/lib/trivia/topics.ts`: approved, family-safe pack questions by game slug, picture category, or a Video games question naming the game; a page 404s and stays out of the sitemap below `MIN_TOPIC_QUESTIONS` (12); answers in the HTML behind tap-to-reveal, `TopicQuestionList`; Quiz JSON-LD). "Play these as a trivia night" (`TriviaStartButton topic=`, `createTopicGame`) builds the On stream shape from them: words in classic rounds, pictures in picture rounds, a classic round with fewer than 3 word questions becomes another picture round. **Prod needs the pack imported and approved** (Platform ▸ Trivia) and game packs drafted + approved before the question pages appear there.
- **On stream (phase 2, built 2026-10-11, GS Pro, no migration):** the builder's **Play it on my stream** switch (default on for the new **On stream** template: 3 rounds of 6, 45s) goes live with `trivia_games.settings.stream` = { communityId, broadcasterId, since } (`go_live` + `stream: true`; Pro + Twitch required; one stream night per host, `stream_busy`). Viewers each play as their own team: the team's secret is `v1.` + an HMAC of game and viewer (`viewerSecret` in `src/lib/trivia/stream.ts`; phone teams' random base64url secrets can't start with "v1."), so viewers don't count toward the room's 40 teams (`MAX_VIEWERS` 3000). Chat answers in the webhook fast lane before Chat Brain (`answerFromChat`: `!a <answer>`, or a bare letter on a choice question; silent; a closed question lets `!a` fall through); `/live` has `LiveTriviaCard` (`/api/trivia/live/[communityId]` GET view + POST answer, signed in; `breakViewer` identity so a Twitch-linked account is the same player as in chat). `submitViewerAnswer` re-reads the phase before saving (a cached copy once let an answer after the reveal reset a judged one). Answers count `STREAM_GRACE_MS` (5s) after the clock (`tick`). Viewers play confidence and final rounds as plain rounds (`scoreQuestion`). Judging is batched (`setVerdicts`, 500 ids a call). Chat lines on open / reveal / end (`announce`, scheduled with `after()` from the host's live route). Overlay piece `trivia` (`TriviaOverlay`, `trivia` in `/latest`, layout default mid-left). The console says it's on stream, drops the per-team answered chips and caps standings at 25; the room TV caps at 10. **Discord** (`src/lib/trivia/discord.ts` + `src/lib/discord/commands/trivia.ts`): servers routing the opt-in `trivia` category get each question as it opens (embed with a `<t:…:R>` countdown, our picture through `/api/trivia/pic`, a button per choice in rows of five, else an Answer button → a one-line modal), edited at the reveal to show the answer (posts kept on `settings.discordPosts`), plus the final standings. Question posts carry a green **Play along** button and the standings **Play more trivia** (`activity:trivia` → LAUNCH_ACTIVITY on the Trivia tab, only while `activityLive()`). Custom ids `trivp:/triva:/trivm:{gameId}:{round}:{question}[:{letter}]`, so an old post can't answer the next question. A Discord user whose account has Twitch linked plays as that Twitch account; an account without Twitch as the site account; anyone else as their Discord id (`viewerForDiscord`). The Activity's Trivia tab plays it too.

### Break mode (GS Pro) — games that run themselves while the streamer is away
- Plan + decisions: https://claude.ai/artifact/PCQJnUSNrAfBKHPuZs9cKD (catalog with Britton's picks https://claude.ai/artifact/3wagwt9xBqcrGGfas1DrTA, seeding answers https://claude.ai/artifact/C172eKhygPGPv9MCUGVBqw). Waves 1a, 1b and most of 2 built 2026-10-10; the rest of wave 2 and wave 3 are planned there.
- **Rules every break game follows:** stream delay (2-15s) means no first-to-answer races: a round has a window, everyone right inside it scores, answers keep counting `GRACE_SECONDS` (5) after the countdown ends; one glance, one word; decided by the game, never a person; only usernames + our content on screen; nothing repeats on a channel within 30 days (`break_seen`); when a game runs out, it may reuse an older item but never one already shown in this break or a pulled one (`skipSet` returns `skip` + `fallback`), else it moves to the next game.
- Schema `supabase/break-mode-m1.sql` (service role only): `break_runs` (one running per community; lineup, game_index/game_round/round_no, phase intro → open → reveal → standings, `round` = public view + reveal + content key, `secret` = the answer, `meta` = chat streak + chat's handicap, `back_by`, `ending_requested_at`, champion), `break_answers` (one per viewer per round, latest counts), `break_scores` (+ `break_add_points` rpc), `break_seen`, `break_pulled` (out of every channel until staff review; `keep`), `break_settings` (lineup, `show` panel|full). Missing tables read as "no break" (`breakReady()`).
- Engine `src/lib/break/store.ts`: `startBreak` (Pro), `tickBreak` (claims each move on phase + round_no; called by the overlay's 2s read, `/api/break`, `/api/hub/running`, the live page; `sweepBreaks` in the polls sweep is the backstop), `requestEnd` (the round in play finishes, then ends; `now` = straight away), `endRun` (champion + chat line), `pullRound`, `answerFromChat` (the webhook fast lane: a short message shaped like an answer while a round is open; 2s per-instance cache of the open round; stored silently, the dispatcher never sees it), `answerFromWeb`, `breakView`. Breaks end on stream.offline and after `MAX_BREAK_MINUTES` (90).
- Games `src/lib/break/games/` (pure; `npx tsx -r ./scripts/server-only-shim.cjs scripts/test-break-games.ts`, ~45k checks; the shim flag is required): `facts.ts` builds Higher or Lower, Odd One Out, Fact or Fiction (false = another character's real value from the same column), Closest Guess and Scramble from the Daily's checked facts (`PUZZLES`), leaning on the game on stream (`puzzleOrder`); `dice.ts` High Roller, Lucky Number, Over or Under (server `randomInt`, rolled at close); `handicap.ts` (Consequences handicaps one streamer can do, reworded; MK adds racing ones; result shown + in chat). Add a game = a `BreakGame` in `games/index.ts` + its `BREAK_GAMES` entry (`types.ts`).
- **Wave 1b (built 2026-10-10):** `tierLive.ts` Tier War Live (six items one a round, room tier = most votes then nearest the average, finished list at the end), `nextBuild.ts` Chat Picks Your Next Build (the Chat Draft pools for the game on stream: MK8DX/MKW combo, Smash fighter), `rollCall.ts` Roll Call (three rolls from the game on stream's own `CHAT_GAMES` randomizer, a secret pick), `shuffleDice.ts` Shuffle Dice: Chat vs the House (every scoring die kept, chat only calls roll or bank, the house banks at 8 or two Bombs, first to 25 or the leader after 12 calls; a sensible chat wins about 57%), `bingo.ts` Stream Bingo on autopilot (a **hosted** game: `BreakGame.hosted` open/finished/close/progress runs on the bingo engine; off the default lineup). Multi-round games keep state in `run.meta` (`MakeContext.meta`, `fresh`, `roundOfGame`; `RoundDraft.meta` merges on open; `BreakGame.rounds(meta)` for a variable count). What chat picked or called for the first game back is `meta.nextPick`, shown at the end and in the "back" line. Champion's prize: `break_settings.champion_tokens` (0 by default; `awardMint` from the streamer's allowance, split on a tie). Staff review: Platform ▸ Originals ▸ Break mode (`PlatformBreakTab`, `/api/admin/break`). Break start/end land in the live session's activity feed (`break_started` / `break_ended`). The /live last-stream recap counts breaks and names the latest break champion (`RecapHighlight.breaks`). Help: `/help/streaming/break-mode`.
- Surfaces: chat `!gs brb [minutes]` / `!gs back [now]` / `!gs pull` (`src/lib/twitch/commands/breakMode.ts`, mods + host); overlay piece `break_screen` (`BreakScreenOverlay`, Layout editor ▸ Apps, `show: full` covers the canvas and scales by `zoom`), in `/latest` as `breakScreen`; Streaming ▸ Live games ▸ **Break mode** and the stream page's Live games tab (`BreakModeTab`: start with a length, live status with Finish the round and end / End now / Pull, lineup checkboxes + break screen look autosave); control room `RunningNow` "On a break" card first with "I'm back"; `/live` `LiveBreakCard` (tap answers; a viewer with Twitch linked is the same player as in chat, else a site player; `/api/break/community/[id]`, `POST /api/break/[id]/answer`). **Wave 2 (built 2026-10-10):** `room.ts` Read the Room (our prompts `src/data/originals/read-the-room.ts`, everyone matching the majority scores, a tie scores all), `zoom.ts` Zoomed In (Draft Night rosters with art, `ZoomFrame` scales the portrait 5/3/1.8/1 over four 8s steps from the round's `openedAt`; `oneGuess`: a viewer's first answer stands, points 4-1 by the step on screen with a 3s delay allowance), `emoji.ts` Emoji Puzzle (`src/data/originals/emoji-puzzles.ts`), `trivia.ts` Trivia (loads the staff-approved pack from `trivia_questions`, judged by the trivia-night `judgeAnswer`, no pictures; skips itself until the pack is approved), `brain.ts` Chat Brain (loads the neediest open public question; `persist` files every answer as a survey answer, source `twitch:break` / `site:break`; answers never shown), `rush.ts` Roster Rush (`multiAnswer`: each name a viewer types is its own row keyed `viewer#name`; `liveAnswers`: `BreakView.found` fills the board live; 1 point a name up to 10, +5 each for clearing it), `royale.ts` Item Box Royale (join window, then `quiet` 5-second knockout waves drawn when joining closes, `meta.royale`), `connect.ts` Four in a Row (chat votes, `botMove` wins/blocks/avoids handing a win, else a scored column with some chance; off the default lineup). Typed names go through `names.ts` (`nameMatcher`: tidy, nicknames, paired names, unique first/last word, one or two typos when unambiguous; `typedAnswer` drops chatter). Runner additions: `RoundDraft.seconds`/`quiet`, `BreakGame.load`/`persist`/`oneGuess`/`multiAnswer`/`liveAnswers`, answers carry `at`. Pulled questions store the answer for staff. **Last of wave 2 (built 2026-10-11, `supabase/break-mode-m2.sql`):** `bounty.ts` **Chat Sets the Bounty** (chat votes one of three challenges for the next game from `src/data/originals/chat-bounties.ts`, the game on stream's first; the voters for the winner are kept in `run.meta.bounty`; `break_settings.bounty_tokens`, 0 = skips itself, also skips while a bounty is open; the streamer or a Full mod calls it with Did it / Didn't on the control room's "Chat's bounty" card or `PATCH /api/break { action: bounty_done | bounty_missed }`; Done splits the tokens between the voters from the allowance via `payViewers`; `openBreakBounty` / `settleBreakBounty` claim on the run's `updated_at`) and `channel.ts` **Know the Channel** (approved questions from `break_channel_questions`, judged by the trivia `judgeAnswer`, 2 points; managed on the Break mode screen by `KnowTheChannelPanel`: add by hand (straight in; a Full mod's are tagged), or **Draft with AI** from a five-field questionnaire (AI feature `channel`, GS Pro; `draftChannelQuestions` uses only what was written; drafts to Keep or Toss); `/api/break/questions` + `/draft`, rules in `src/lib/break/channelQuestions.ts`). Both are in the default lineup and skip themselves until set up; until the migration runs the bounty setting is hidden and the panel says so. Bingo on a break calls its own numbers every 10 seconds (hosted `tick`; bingo's timer only goes down to 30s) and ending a break closes it. **Wave 3 (built 2026-10-11, no migration):** `boss.ts` **Boss Fight** (co-op: `hit` once a round, 8 to 14 damage, 1 in 8 a critical for triple; health = `HP_PER_FIGHTER` (40) per hitter in the busiest round so far, never below `BASE_HP` (60), so drop-ins don't make it unbeatable; five rounds; a win pays every hitter 3 and the final blow 2 more, a loss pays nobody; our own bosses with Tabler glyphs, `BossBar`), `letters.ts` **Five Letters** (a five-letter gaming word with a category clue; each round the most-typed word on our list is played (`markGuess`: right spot / in the word / not, repeats counted once), a word already played can't be played again, six guesses; 1 point for steering, 5/4/3 for solving by guess 2/4/6; our own handwritten lists in `src/data/originals/five-letters.ts`, answers plus everyday and starter words, all through the word filter in the test; `LetterGrid`), `derby.ts` **Kart Derby** (join round: a lane 1 to 8 or `join` for the emptiest; the race is drawn when joining closes (`drawRace`: speeds plus boosts, shortcuts, banana peels, a shell for the leader; the winner crosses at exactly 100) and plays as three quiet lap rounds where karts move from → to over the lap (`DerbyTrack`, our own SVG `Kart`, lane colours in live.css); busy chats race as lane teams, empty lanes get GameShuffle karts; 1 for joining, 5/3/2 for everyone in the podium lanes). All three are in the default lineup, and `withNewGames` (types.ts, `BREAK_GAME_ADDED`) adds games to a saved lineup whose settings were last saved before the game arrived. The live page plays all three (Hit button, the grid with a word box, lane buttons and the race). Tests in `scripts/test-break-games.ts`; dev run `scripts/.tmp/wave3-e2e.ts`. **Game Trivia** (`gameTrivia.ts`, built 2026-10-11, `supabase/trivia-game-packs-m1.sql` adds `trivia_questions.game_slug`): questions about the game on stream from per-game packs, else the pack's Video games questions; judged like Trivia and shares its `trivia:` no-repeat keys. Staff draft packs in Platform ▸ Trivia ▸ **Draft a game pack** (`src/lib/trivia/gamePacks.ts`, `draftGamePack`: games = `CHAT_GAMES` with checked facts first; Claude gets the Daily's facts for that game (`factsFor`, via `PUZZLE_GAMES`) and names references for the rest; saved as AI drafts in Video games with the game's slug, nothing reaches a stream until approved; recorded as AI feature `trivia`); the list filters by game and labels AI drafts. Until the migration runs the button says the database needs updating and Game Trivia uses the Video games questions. Not built: nickname lists beyond the built-in set.

### Chat Draft (GS Pro) — a generic draft engine
- Chat fills a streamer's slots one pick at a time; each pick is a poll (`gs_polls.kind = 'draft'`) of N random options that fit the rules, so chat `!vote`, `/live` taps and the overlay feed one tally. Engine `src/lib/drafts/store.ts` (game-agnostic; `startDraft`/`resolvePick`/`tickDraft`/`cancelDraft`/`viewOf`), table `stream_drafts` (`supabase/stream-drafts-m1.sql`, one open per community).
- **Pools make it reusable:** a `DraftPool` (`src/lib/drafts/types.ts`) says the slots, the candidates for a slot given rules + picks so far, a label lookup and the poll question. Server pools in `src/lib/drafts/pools/` (Pokémon Scarlet/Violet + Champions with rules fully evolved / no legendary-mythical / unique types; Mario Kart combo per part and track lists of 4 or 8; Smash stages, one or a best of 3 from the competitive list or every stage, and the streamer's fighter or a squad of 3), client-safe info + chat aliases in `src/lib/drafts/catalog.ts` (`aliasRules` lets a chat word start a variant: `stages` = best of 3, `squad` = three fighters). **A new kind of draft = a pool + a catalog entry, no migration.**
- Timer: no cron; `tickDraft` runs on overlay and `/live` reads, the polls sweep calls `advanceDraftForPoll` as a backstop, and resolving claims the pick first (`current_poll_id` → null) so it can't double-resolve. Chat announces each pick's options and winner (`announceToCommunity`, now in `src/lib/twitch/announce.ts`).
- Surfaces: Streaming ▸ Live games ▸ **Chat Draft** tab (`ChatDraftTab`, `/api/drafts`, `PATCH /api/drafts/[id]` next/end), `!draft` / `!draft start <pokemon|champions|kart|mkw|tracks|mkwtracks>` / `next` / `end` (`src/lib/twitch/commands/draft.ts`), `/live` `LiveDraftCard` (`/api/drafts/community/[id]`; `LivePollCard` hides draft polls), overlay piece `chat_draft` (`DraftOverlay`; the poll piece steps aside for a draft's pick). Pokémon rosters in `src/data/pokemon/*.json` (researched 2026-09-29; Champions is Regulation M-C and rotates). Names and types only, no art (unofficial fan tool). Help: `/help/streaming/chat-draft`.
- **Captain mode** (team drafts, same table, `mode = 'captains'`): sign-ups (`status = 'signup'`, seeded from the session lobby's `session_participants`, plus `!draft in`/`out` and names added by hand) → captains pick players into 2-4 teams, snake (default) or alternating. Pure rules in `src/lib/drafts/captains.ts` (`teamForTurn`, `picksInARow`, `matchEntrant` loose `!pick` matching, `isCaptainOnClock`); store `openSignups`/`addEntrants`/`removeEntrant`/`startCaptains`/`pickPlayer` (claims the turn via `.eq("turn")`; `turn` doubles as the sign-up list's revision) and a random pick when the timer runs out (lazy tick + `sweepCaptainTimers` in the polls sweep). Captains pick on `/live` (recognized by account or linked `users.twitch_id`; `POST /api/drafts/[id]/pick`, `you.onClock` on the public read) or `!pick name` (`tryCaptainPick` runs first in the `gs.pick` handler, else picks/bans as before); the streamer picks from the dashboard (`CaptainDraftPanel`, PATCH `add`/`remove`/`lobby`/`start`/`pick`). Shared `CaptainBoard` (dashboard + /live, tap then confirm); the overlay piece shows the teams side by side. Hand-off to tournaments/lounge is later.

### Chat Brain (GameShuffle Original, in progress)
- A survey game built from real answers: people answer short prompts, answers are grouped and ranked into boards, boards are played solo (Daily), at the table (live night + pass the phone) and on stream (Pro). Spec + decisions: `specs/gs-originals-chat-brain.md`. Name is final ("Chat Brain", two words); never use the show it's inspired by in names, copy or SEO.
- Schema `supabase/chat-brain-m1.sql` (`brain_*` tables, service role). Pure rules `src/lib/chatbrain/rules.ts` (normalize, autoGroup, buildBoard: groups need ≥2 people and 2%, top 8, points total 100; matchGuess). Store `src/lib/chatbrain/store.ts`. Answers: `/api/chat-brain/answer` (one per prompt per account / hashed browser / chat identity; `source` records site, discord, twitch or `share:<platform>`; blocklist via `src/lib/text/filter.ts`, the shared `obscenity` filter). Public page `/chat-brain` (noindex for now; `?prompt=`, `?src=`). Staff: Platform ▸ Chat Brain. Individual answers are never public: only reviewed groups.
- **Seeding surfaces (before launch).** One shared card, `ChatBrainAsk` (`src/components/chatbrain/ChatBrainAsk.tsx`; `GET /api/chat-brain?view=card&skip=`), sits on the Daily end screen (`source` daily), the Weekly once locked in or signed out (weekly), and after a live night's recap (night) (the homepage's Play today tile went with the 2026-10-16 homepage reorg). After an answer it shows `sameLine(same)` ("You and 6 others said that"; `submitAnswer` returns `same` = others with the same normalized answer so far), also used in the Discord reply. `brainProgress()` (answers + public boards toward `LAUNCH_BOARDS` 30, cached 60s) feeds `BrainProgressBar` on the card and the `/chat-brain` launch panel. **Founding Brain** badge on `/u` at `FOUNDING_BRAIN_ANSWERS` (10) answers, counted by `brainAnswerCount` (account + linked chat identities; set `FOUNDING_CUTOFF` in `rules.ts` at launch). Randomizer pages stay out of it.
- **Hub page** (`ChatBrainHome`): `BrowseHero` band (accent `violet`, art category `brain` added to `EventHeaderArt`: brain/messages/chart-bar/users-group/bulb), How it works and Three ways to play as CDS Card icon tiles, an illustrated example board (made-up numbers, labelled), the launch panel (`LaunchPanel`: progress, "email me when it opens" via `/api/chat-brain/updates` → MailerLite group "Chat Brain Launch" (`ML_CHAT_BRAIN_GROUP` in `src/lib/chatbrain/updates.ts`); non-prod logs), the create-account line, then every open question with category icons (`brainIcons.tsx`, one Tabler glyph per category). The answer card shows a brain mark, the category tag and a crowd row (you + one figure per matching answer).
- **Audiences and editions** (`supabase/chat-brain-audience-m1.sql`, `src/lib/chatbrain/audience.ts`): optional age band (13-17 … 55+, no under-13), gender (woman/man/nonbinary, null = prefer not to say) and country (ISO-2, defaults to the connection's `x-vercel-ip-country` unless chosen). Asked once after the first answer (`AudienceStep` + `useAudienceStep`); accounts save to private `brain_audience` (`/api/chat-brain/audience`), signed-out choices live in localStorage and ride with each answer. Every answer stores the snapshot plus its `edition`. Publishing (`review.ts`) writes the 'all' board and an audience board per segment with ≥ `AUDIENCE_MIN` (30) answers, recounted from the same reviewed groups; boards are keyed (prompt_id, segment, edition) with `answered_from/to`. Platform ▸ Chat Brain ▸ Published ▸ "Run it again" (`adminNewEdition`) opens a new edition; earlier boards stay playable. Privacy policy §2.5 covers it.
- **Question bank** `src/data/originals/chat-brain-questions.ts` (160 reviewed, family-safe, 20 per category). Platform ▸ Chat Brain ▸ "Add the question bank" (`importBank`, admin action `bank`) inserts any not already present (exact normalized match) as drafts; safe to rerun on dev or prod.
- **Platform ▸ Originals** (`PlatformOriginalsTab`, `/api/admin/originals`, `src/lib/originals/overview.ts`): Chat Brain (boards toward launch, answers 24h/7d/total by source, questions by status, collecting furthest from a board, ready for review, Founding Brains, bank questions not yet added, low-draft and email-list warnings), Daily (today's answer, next 7 days with clue status, signed-in plays for 7 days, characters missing facts or clues) and Weekly (this/next week, last week's top 3). Each section degrades on its own.
- **Staff tools** (Platform ▸ Chat Brain): queue lanes (draft → collecting → review → published). "Draft with Claude" (`src/lib/chatbrain/drafts.ts`, `draftStructured` in `src/lib/ai/claude.ts`, word filter + near-duplicate check, `origin 'ai'`); review screen (`ChatBrainReview`, `src/lib/chatbrain/review.ts`: spelling merges, Claude meaning merges where examples fold into a popular broad answer, manual merge/split/hide, live board preview, `publish` freezes `brain_boards`). Share panel on collecting questions (`ChatBrainShare`): `/chat-brain/q/[id]` (link preview from `/api/chat-brain/share/[id]?format=og|story`, an `ImageResponse`), tracked `?src=` links per platform, X/Bluesky intents.
- **Discord** (`src/lib/discord/commands/chatbrain.ts`, free): `/gs-brain [category]` (Manage Server posts the neediest open question publicly via `promptNeedingAnswers`; anyone else gets their next unanswered one, ephemeral). Buttons `brain:{id}` → modal (interaction type 9; submits arrive as type 5 `brainm:{id}`) → `submitAnswer` with source `discord`, keyed to the Discord `gs_identity`; `brainnext:{category}` serves the next one. Daily post: `/api/cron/chat-brain-discord` (9am Pacific: scheduled 16:00 and 17:00 UTC, only the 9am-Pacific run posts; `?force` overrides) to servers routing the opt-in `chatbrain` category (`postComponentsToCategory`, `requireRoute`), claimed per day in `brain_discord_posts` (`supabase/chat-brain-discord-m1.sql`), no repeat within 7 days. **Run `npx tsx scripts/register-discord-commands.ts` to add `/gs-brain`.**
- **Stream** (`src/lib/chatbrain/stream.ts`, schema `supabase/chat-brain-stream-m1.sql`, build order in the stream spec). Built so far: **survey windows**. `!cb ask [seconds] [category | own question]` (mods; 30-180s, default 60; a category or nothing asks the public question that most needs answers, free; an own question becomes a community prompt, GS Pro). Viewers `!a <answer>` / `!answer` / `!cb <answer>`: the Twitch webhook **fast lane** (`answerFromChat` + `recordGuess`, before `parseCommand`) upserts into `brain_stream_guesses` with no reply, skipping the dispatcher; commands in `src/lib/twitch/commands/chatbrain.ts` cover the fallback and YouTube. Close (`closeWindow`, claimed open → closing) turns guesses into `brain_answers` (source `twitch`, `window_id`, Twitch `gs_identity`) and posts one line: public questions keep the board secret, own questions show chat's top 3. No cron: closes on the next `!a`/`!cb` after time is up, `sweepStreamWindows` in the polls sweep as backstop.
- **Chat plays (built 2026-10-07, GS Pro):** chat is one team against 1 to 5 published boards (the public library plus the community's own, family-safe by default, skipping boards played in the community's last 8 games). Pure rules in `src/lib/chatbrain/streamRules.ts` (chat's answer = the biggest group, every guess bucketed to the board answer it matches or its own spelling group, ties to the group whose latest guess came first; hit banks points, anything else including an empty window is a strike; 3 strikes or a cleared board ends it; play along = each viewer earns an answer's points once per board if it was hidden when the window opened). Engine `src/lib/chatbrain/streamGame.ts` on the `brain_stream_*` tables (already in `chat-brain-stream-m1.sql`): `startGame`, `closeGameWindow` (called by `stream.closeWindow` for game windows), `nextBoard`, `lockWindow`, `setGuessTime`, `overrule` (accept/reject the last result within 30s, only while the board is in play), `endGame`, `tickGame`, `gameView`. Windows open after a 6s pause (`REVEAL_PAUSE_MS`, the question's first look and the beat after each reveal); guesses before that are ignored. A board's end reveals the rest and waits for `!cb next`. Chat: `!cb start [boards] [seconds]`, `!cb time`, `lock`, `next`, `accept [answer]`, `reject`, `end`. Dashboard: Streaming ▸ Live games ▸ **Chat Brain** (`ChatBrainStreamTab`, `/api/chat-brain/stream` GET/POST/PATCH). Overlay piece `chat_brain` (`ChatBrainOverlay`, in the `/latest` payload as `brain`; a finished game stays up 2 minutes). Tests: `npx tsx scripts/test-chatbrain-stream.ts`. Not built yet: Redis buffering (not needed so far: a dev load test took 1,000 guesses into one window at ~446/s), and from step 2, phone entry/buzz and accounts for players. Dev has three test boards owned by the novastreams community.
- **1v1 and Teams (built 2026-10-11, GS Pro, no migration):** two player sides (1v1 = one player a side named after them; Teams = 2-5 a side with a team name) play a round per board: face-off (the host types the first answer heard and picks who said it; the top hidden answer wins outright, else the other side answers and the higher answer wins; both miss = again, up to 3, then the host picks), control until cleared or 3 strikes, then one steal answer. Every answer found goes into the round's pot; the winner banks it; the last round of 2+ is doubled. Players say answers out loud and the host or a Full mod types them in (hidden answers stay hidden on the console: the streamer is often a player). Chat plays along on every turn: each turn is a window (kinds `faceoff`/`guess`/`steal`) collecting chat's shadow guesses until the host answers or time runs out. Optional **Trust chat?** (`chat_advisor`: on a final guess or a steal the side's answer is locked in (`side_answer`), chat's shows beside it, `trust` plays one; skipped when they agree or chat said nothing) and **chat's last steal** (`chat_last_steal`: a `last_steal` window for chat team 3 after a missed steal that didn't use chat's answer). Engine `src/lib/chatbrain/streamVersus.ts` (`startVersus`, `submitAnswer`, `trust`, `pickControl`, `undoLast` (a minute, nothing answered since: strikes/control/pot/banked round restored from the turn's snapshot and the turn reopens), `nextRound` (skipping banks nothing), `finishVersus`, `versusView` (`StreamGameView.versus`), `closeTurnChat`); `streamGame.ts` dispatches by `isVersusMode(mode)`. State in existing columns: `games.round` = rounds banked (claimed as `current_board + 1`), `control_team`; each turn window's `answer` jsonb holds `seq` (turn order: read turns by it, not `opens_at`), face-off `pair`/`attempt`, `player`, chat's answer, the outcome and the undo snapshot; taken-back turns are `overruled`. Pure rules in `streamRules.ts` (`playerOutcome`, `faceoffWinner`, `sameAnswer`, `advisorMoment`, `lastStealOpen`, `roundMultiplier`, `onClock`, `cleanSides`, lines). Chat (mods): `!cb play [side] <answer>`, `!cb trust chat|team`, `!cb control <side>`, `!cb undo`; `!cb accept/reject` answer "use !cb undo". Console `ChatBrainVersus.tsx` (mode picker on the start form); overlay `VersusBoard` in `ChatBrainOverlay` (side scores, control, strikes, Trust chat? side by side). Help: `/help/streaming/chat-brain`. A bulk insert with mixed row keys sends missing keys as null (not the default): give every row the same keys.
- **Speed round, /live and prizes (built 2026-10-11, step 5):** **speed round** = mode `speed` (`streamSpeed.ts`; `!cb start speed [boards]` or Who plays ▸ Speed round): one window per board stays open and each `!a` is judged as it arrives; the first insert into `brain_stream_reveals` (primary key) wins the answer, its points go to that viewer as play-along points, every find restarts a 20s timer (`SPEED_SECONDS`), a timeout is a strike, three strikes or a cleared board ends it. The window's `closes_at` is the timer and the version: finds and strikes move it with a conditional update on the old value (`speedTimeout`, so exactly one reader strikes and a stale cache can't strike early). `closeWindow` hands speed windows (`StreamWindow.speed`, from `speed:answer->>speed` in `WINDOW_COLS`) to the timer instead of closing them; lock, overrule and guess time refuse with `speed_round`. Finders live in the window's record (`answer.finds`), shown on the board on the overlay, dashboard and /live. **/live card** `LiveChatBrainCard` (`/api/chat-brain/live/[communityId]`, GET cached 1s per community, POST rate limited): the board as on the overlay, and signed-in viewers answer whatever is open (guess, find, play-along, survey) through `recordGuess`; a Twitch-linked account is the same player as in chat (`breakViewer`), anyone else plays as `site` (survey answers filed by `user_id`, source `site:live`). **Prizes** (`chat-brain-stream-m2.sql`: `brain_stream_games.prize_tokens`; `streamPrize.ts`): the host picks 25 to 500 tokens on the start form, the top three by points split it 50/30/20 when the game ends (`prizeSplit`, ties share, 60/40 or all of it with fewer scorers), paid from the monthly allowance by `payViewer` (`src/lib/economy/viewerPayouts.ts`, shared with break mode) and named in the final chat line; hidden (`prizeReady()`) until the column exists, and never in `GAME_COLS` so games run without it. Public views use `topPlayAlong` (names only); `topPlayAlongKeyed` (platform:id) is only for matching payouts.
- **Co-stream (built 2026-10-11, step 4, no migration):** mode `costream` (`streamCostream.ts`), two channels' chats one team each; only the host needs GS Pro. **Invite without the invitations table** (its kind check only allows session/tournament): the host picks a streamer with Twitch connected (`GET /api/chat-brain/stream?search=`), the game waits in `lobby` with team 2's row holding the invite (`members[0] = { name, userId, state: invited|joined|declined|left }`, `broadcaster_id` null), and the guest gets a `chatbrain_costream` notification (in the Invitations group) linking their Chat Brain tab (`CostreamInvites`; shown even without a channel or Pro). Accepting checks Twitch and our bot in their chat (`twitch_eventsub_subscriptions` channel.chat.message enabled) and binds their channel to team 2; the host presses Start. Rounds: a face-off window for both chats (`costreamFaceoff`: higher hit wins control, same answer = the chat that typed it first via `firstMatchAt`, none = again up to 3 then `pick_control`), guess windows for the chat in control, one steal window for the other, pot to the winner, last round doubled. **Every window lives on the host's channel**; `channelWindow(channel)` in `stream.ts` finds it for the guest through team 2's row and returns the team, `recordGuess` stores `brain_stream_guesses.team` and refuses the other chat in control/steal windows (`StreamWindow.costream` from `costream:answer->>costream`). The webhook passes Twitch Shared Chat's `source_broadcaster_user_id` so a message counts for the channel it was typed in. The guest's overlay and live page show the host's game (`guestGameFor`), the guest can't start games or surveys meanwhile (`inCostream`), a guest mod's `!cb lock` won't close the host's window, and lines go to both chats (`sayBoth`). Views reuse the 1v1 shape (`versus`) with mode `costream`; leaning only in control/steal windows. Dashboard `ChatBrainCostream.tsx` (picker, lobby, invite, console; the guest's console only has Leave). PostgREST `.contains` on jsonb needs the JSON text (`JSON.stringify([...])`): an array argument is sent as a Postgres array literal. Gap: Twitch streamer setup is GS Pro, so in practice the guest needs Pro or a trial too.
- **Survey rounds (built 2026-10-11, no migration):** a survey window (`!cb ask [seconds] [hidden] [category | own question]` or the dashboard's **Survey your chat** card, `ChatBrainSurvey.tsx`, POST `{ survey }` on `/api/chat-brain/stream`) now has a style: the channel's own question (GS Pro) shows its answers on stream as they come in (`live`) unless `hidden`; GameShuffle questions are always hidden (their board must stay a surprise) and still feed the site-wide board. Grouping (`src/lib/chatbrain/surveyRules.ts`, pure): spelling (`autoGroup` + close spellings), then the latest AI meaning pass, then the host's merges, then hides; everything keyed by normalized answers so hides and merges hold as new spellings arrive; a group shows on stream with 2+ people and 2% (`shownGroups`, numbered for `!cb hide <n>` / `!cb merge <n> <m>`). Server `src/lib/chatbrain/survey.ts`: `hostSurveyView` (all groups with keys, for the dashboard; folded behind a spoiler Accordion in a hidden round) / `overlaySurveyView` (no keys; no board for a hidden round), `hideSurveyGroup`, `mergeSurveyGroups`, `maybeAiPass` (live rounds: every 10s while new distinct answers arrive, up to 4, claimed on `answer->ai->>at`; the overlay and dashboard schedule it with `after()`), `tickSurvey`, `finishSurvey` (at the close: one more AI pass with a 15s timeout, then `publish()` freezes the own question into a community-scoped board when it has 10+ answers and 3 groups; result on the window), `latestSurveyBoard`. AI passes use `aiGroupAnswers` (split out of `review.ts` `aiGroups`), cap `SURVEY_AI_PER_MONTH` (150) per channel per Pacific month, and never count against the streamer's AI generations. State lives on the window's `answer` jsonb (`SurveyState` + `result`). Play it: `!cb start survey` or the start form's **Start with my survey board** (`first` in `pickBoards`/`startGame`/`startVersus`). Overlay: `ChatBrainSurveyOverlay` (bars while open, a count when hidden, the board once made) in the `chat_brain` placement, `brainSurvey` in `/latest`; an open survey beats a just-finished game. A stored JSON `null` isn't SQL NULL: filter jsonb keys with `->>` when testing for "missing or null".

### Walk-Up Anthems (MLB-style walk-up songs) — **foundation; playback not yet wired**
- A short (10–15s) clip of a stream-safe track that plays on a streamer's OBS overlay when an eligible viewer shows up (default trigger: **first chat of the stream**). See `specs`-less but tracked in the changelog + the `project_walkup_anthems` memory.
- **Personal to the user, gated by the streamer** — the anthem belongs to the viewer (set on account → **Theme**, part of the personalization layer, travels across channels); the **streamer** owns channel policy (Streaming ▸ Viewers ▸ **Walk-up songs**: enable, eligible roles, volume, cooldown, allow-custom). `resolveAnthemForTrigger` AND-gates both — nothing plays unless viewer AND streamer opted in.
- **Source-agnostic** via `src/lib/anthems/providers/` — `MusicProvider` (mirrors `PlatformAdapter`). Its `redistribution` status (`cleared` | `pending` | `bring_your_own`) gates whether a provider's catalog may actually be **served**: creator-use ≠ platform redistribution. **StreamBeats ships `pending`** (built against, not served) until a platform license is confirmed; **Monstercat** (BYO-Gold / partnership) + **Lickd** (B2B chart music) slot in later. Flip a provider to `cleared` to serve it.
- **Lib:** `src/lib/anthems/` — `types`, `store` (CRUD + `resolveAnthemForTrigger` seam + `recordAnthemPlay`), `providers/{provider,streambeats,registry}`. **APIs:** `/api/account/anthem` (personal + catalog), `/api/account/anthem/policy` (channel), `/api/anthems/catalog`. **UI:** `AnthemSettings.tsx` (Theme tab) + `ChannelAnthemSettings.tsx` (Walk-Up tab). **Schema:** `supabase/anthems-m1.sql`.
- **Not built yet:** first-chat Twitch event handler → `resolveAnthemForTrigger` → overlay audio playback; StreamBeats ingestion to R2 (gated on the `cleared` flip).

### Tier List Maker
- `/tier-list-maker` + `/tier-list-maker/[template]` (`TierListTool`, templates in `src/data/tier-templates.ts`, each with a `group` for the picker). Templates: Mario Kart 8 Deluxe/World/64, Smash fighters + competitive stages, Mario Party Jamboree boards + characters, Superstars boards, Kirby riders + machines, Splatoon 3 stages, Overwatch + Marvel Rivals heroes, party games; picture templates only when the game's art is up (`artReady`) and the game is public. Words-only templates leave `TemplateItem.image` out (Halloween Treats: kinds of treat, no brand names). Every template is also a Tier Wars / Weekly topic (`template:<slug>`; Weekly items are frozen per week in the DB, so new templates only change future fallback picks).
- **Names must be unique in a template:** Tier Wars, the Weekly and break mode key items by name, so `tracksOf` puts the console in front of repeated MK8DX names ("N64 Rainbow Road", "GBA Mario Circuit"); `scripts/test-break-games.ts` checks every topic.
- **Board:** one ordered item list; `moveItem` drops an item before the one it's let go on (or at the row's end), `arrayMove` reorders within a row (dnd-kit sortable, one `SortableContext` per row). MouseSensor (5px) + TouchSensor (180ms hold) so a finger still scrolls (`touch-action: manipulation`, never `none`). Tap or Enter picks an item up: every row becomes a target and the sticky Move to bar (CDS Buttons per tier, Unrank, remove, put down; Escape cancels) is the non-drag path (WCAG 2.5.7). `Tool Used` fires once per visit.

### Game catalog + box art
- `src/data/game-catalog.ts` (`GAME_CATALOG`): every game GameShuffle knows by name. `status` live (we have a tool/randomizer/roster) | candidate (`pitch`: the randomizer or mode it'd make) | listed (popular favorite); `twitch` = the Twitch category name when it differs; `aliases` keep old stored names matching. **Never rename an entry people may have saved in `users.favorite_games`; add an alias.** `catalogGame(name)`, `boxArt(game)`.
- **Box art** = the publisher cover Twitch serves for the game's category (Helix Get Games, app token): `DOTENV_CONFIG_PATH=.env.local npx tsx scripts/pull-box-art.ts [--force] [--only slug]` saves `public/images/box-art/<slug>.webp` (300x400) and writes `src/data/box-art.generated.ts`. 83 games as of 2026-10-07. Search a category name with Helix `search/categories` when a name misses.
- `GameCover` (`src/components/games/GameCover.tsx`): box art, or a lettered colour tile for a game not in the catalog. Favorite games (`src/data/favorite-games.ts`, derived from the catalog; `MAX_FAVORITE_GAMES` 12) are edited in `FavoriteGamesEditor` (search with live games first, "Add another game:" for anything missing, a draggable cover shelf via `SortableList` grid) and shown as a cover shelf on `/u`. **Platform ▸ Game catalog** (`PlatformGamesTab`, `/api/admin/game-catalog`): favorites per game, "Asked for" (custom names, most asked first) and candidates, the curation queue.
- **Look games up by app slug first:** `findGame(slug, name)` = `catalogForApp(slug)` (an entry's `appSlug`, e.g. `mario-party` → `mario-party-n64`) then `catalogGame(name)`, because names vary ("Mario Party" is the N64 game on a live night, the series on a profile). `GameCover({ slug, name, blank: false })` renders nothing for a game without art (GameShuffle Originals); `GameChipIcon` is the small cover for a CDS Chip `icon`. Covers show on: tournament create (cover buttons), the tournament page, TV display and championship badges, game night browse cards, live night phone + TV headers and lineups, `/live` upcoming, communities (crews, battles), the players directory, seasons, the night editors/planner chips, and Discord embeds (randomize header, room code, go-live and game-change posts: box art takes the thumbnail and the streamer's avatar moves to the author line; Discord gets absolute `SITE_URL` paths).

### Jackbox picker (game-night tool)
- `/game-nights/tools/jackbox-picker` (`JackboxPicker`, registered in `src/lib/game-nights/companion/tools.ts`): tick owned packs, set tonight's player count, optional kid-friendly filter; pick one, or **Roll 3 and vote** (tap-to-vote on one device, or GS Pro streamers send the three to a chat poll via `POST /api/polls`). Unofficial fan tool: names only, no Jackbox art or logos.
- Data `src/data/jackbox.ts` (researched 2026-09-29; sources in the file header). Games list every pack they're sold in (so Party Starter / Party Essentials work). `kids`: `filter` (has a family setting) | `clean` (family friendly by design, per Jackbox's chart) | `no`; the kids filter keeps the first two. Naughty Pack games only appear if that pack is ticked. Party Pack 12 added 2026-10-17 (counts from jackboxgames.com). **Fakin' It XL (2026-11-12) still needs adding once released.**
- The sitemap now lists `/game-nights/tools` and every registered tool (they were missing entirely); noindex prototypes go in `NOINDEX_TOOLS` in `src/app/sitemap.ts`.

### GameShuffle Shop (curated packs; builds A to D + order handling done 2026-10-09)
- **Two paths:** TCGplayer stays the place for singles (`TCG_SHOP_URL`, below); the GameShuffle shop at `/shop` sells packs and themes we curate, through Stripe Checkout, shipped from WA to the 50 states and DC. May grow into merch (`category`, default `tcg`). Spec, copy and Phase 0 report: `docs/specs/shop/` (local, gitignored) and https://claude.ai/artifact/LEn3bSVjSdYeauhwL2tLXZ. Target live Nov 10, 2026; test mode only until Britton says otherwise. Britton owns postage and envelopes.
- **Catalog:** shop settings (shipping rule, postage, cutoffs, tax monitor, UTM, contact) live in `src/data/store/shop-seed.json`; products start there but **Platform ▸ Shop orders ▸ Products is the source of truth once a SKU exists** (the seed only adds missing SKUs; `--overwrite` puts the file back over admin edits). Typed in `src/lib/store/config.ts`; pure rules in `src/lib/store/rules.ts` (`shipMethodFor`: any bundle or 5+ singles = parcel via Rollo, 1 single = letter, 2 to 4 = flat; `shipWeightOz`; `postageCents`); tests `npx tsx scripts/test-store-rules.ts`. New shop code lives in `src/lib/store/` (`src/lib/shop/` is the TCGplayer featured cards, and the Arcade owns `gs_shop_purchase`).
- **Schema** `supabase/shop-m1.sql`: `shop_products` (Stripe owns the price, this table owns stock), `shop_stock_holds` (a hold stops counting at `expires_at`), `shop_orders` (order numbers from 1001, shown `GS-1001`; unique `checkout_session_id`), `shop_order_items`. Functions (service role only): `shop_availability()`, `shop_reserve()` (row locks, so two buyers can't take the last unit), `shop_fulfil()` (one order per session however often the webhook arrives; stock never below zero, shortfall recorded). RLS: products public while active, buyers read their own orders.
- **Seed** `scripts/seed-shop.ts [--skip-db] [--wa-tax] [--dry-run] [--overwrite]` (Stripe calls shared with the admin via `src/lib/store/catalog.ts`): Stripe Products with fixed ids `gs_shop_<sku>`, tax code `txcd_99999999`, metadata `gs_kind: "shop"`; one tax-exclusive Price per SKU by lookup key `shop_<sku>` (a price change makes a new Price and archives the old). Refuses a live key. Re-running changes nothing; stock and the active switch are only set when a row is first created (admin owns them after). Pricing ▸ Sync skips `gs_kind: "shop"` prices. Test mode has a WA sales tax registration (account-wide).
- **Hidden for now (Britton, 2026-10-10, while products are worked out):** with `shop_open` and the new `shop_coming_soon` switch both off, `/shop` is page not found for visitors (staff still get the full shop); My Stuff ▸ Shop orders shows only when the shop is open, for staff, or for someone with an order. Turn `shop_coming_soon` on in Platform ▸ Switches to bring back the Coming soon page.
- **Pages:** until the `shop_open` platform flag is on, visitors get a **Coming soon** `/shop` (when `shop_coming_soon` is on) (Britton, 2026-10-09): products with their placeholder pictures, blurbs and View details, but no prices, stock, cart, shipping or checkout, a "Coming soon" badge, and **Notify me** (cards and the details window jump to `ShopUpdates`, the "Get an email when the packs go live" signup: `POST /api/shop/updates` → MailerLite group "Shop Launch" (`ML_SHOP_GROUP` in `src/lib/store/updates.ts`); non-prod logs instead of adding; event `Shop Updates Joined`). Noindex until open; `/shop/policies` and checkout stay closed. Staff get the full shop to test (seed file with sample stock if the tables are missing) and `?view=soon` shows them the visitor version. Copy in `src/lib/store/copy.ts`, verbatim from Britton's copy doc (lines marked NEW aren't in it yet). Store-standard cards (Britton, 2026-10-09): photo (CDS card media, top corners only), title, price, short description, **Add to cart** and **View details** (`ProductDetailsModal`: full description, what's inside, how it ships, quantity). **A cart** (the spec had it out of scope; Britton asked for it): `ShopCart.tsx` keeps lines in localStorage (`gs-shop-cart`, nothing held until checkout), a CDS Drawer with quantity steppers capped at the per-order limit and stock, and a Cart button above the shelf (in the page, not floating; Add to cart opens the drawer); Check out posts the whole cart to `/api/shop/checkout`. CDS doesn't export `CardMedia`/`CardFooter`, so the card uses their CSS classes (raise upstream); `CardTitle`/`CardDescription` are deprecated in CDS, so the card uses a styled `h3`/`p`. The cart Drawer mounts only after the cart loads (the CDS Drawer renders differently on the server and broke hydration).
- **Shipping and returns (Britton, 2026-10-09):** every order ships tracked by USPS Ground Advantage (`shipping.stamped_mail: false`; the letter/flat rules stay behind that switch): $4.99 under $25, free from $25 (`shipping.rate`, `shippingCents`, subtotal before tax; the shipping charge is taxable in WA, tax code `txcd_92010001`). Ship-by = 2 business days in Pacific, skipping weekends and `shipping.no_ship_days` (USPS holidays). Pokémon card products are final sale (`returns.final_sale_categories`, `isFinalSale`): shown in the details modal, cart, Stripe text and policies. Damaged in transit or missing guaranteed contents: the buyer chooses a replacement or a refund, no photo or deadline (matches our TCGplayer sales). Condition: we aim for Near Mint; some cards may be Lightly Played per TCGplayer's guidelines (policies + details modal). Stripe's US address form lists Armed Forces codes and territories as states, so `checkout.blocked_states` (AA AE AP AS GU MP PR VI UM) orders are recorded as `canceled` (`cant_ship_address`), refunded in full and emailed, with stock untouched.
