- **What's in a pack, and "hit" (Britton, 2026-10-09):** every curated pack = regular non-holo cards + 3 holo or reverse holo + 1 30th Celebration Pikachu + 1 Energy card (Spark & Spook keeps Dark, Ghost and Team Rocket for its regular cards); the Anniversary Thirty Set is its own thing (all 30 Pikachu + Pikachu ex 053/054). A **hit** means an ex card or rarer, and copy only says hit where one is guaranteed (Thirty Set, Ready-to-Play Deck); holos are called holos. The multi-pack bundles have no bonus card for now. Policies has a "What counts as a hit" section.
- **Checkout (build C):** `POST /api/shop/checkout` takes the whole cart, holds exactly its quantities with `shop_reserve` (a short line returns `sold_out`/`not_enough` with the SKU and what's left, and the cart fixes itself), then opens hosted Checkout with fixed quantities (no adjustable quantities or optional items: hosted Checkout can't reprice shipping after it opens), one shipping rate from the subtotal, `automatic_tax`, card only, `customer_email` + `client_reference_id`, `metadata { gs_kind: "shop", hold_group, source_page, utm_* }` and a 31-minute expiry; holds last 10 minutes longer (`hold_grace_minutes`). UTMs from the landing URL ride in sessionStorage (`gs-shop-utm`). Rate limited per IP; staff-only until `shop_open`.
- **Orders (build D):** `src/lib/store/orders.ts` `fulfilShopSession` runs from the webhook (`checkout.session.completed` and `async_payment_succeeded`, first branch, `gs_kind === "shop"`, skipped for Connect events) and from `/shop/thanks` (whichever lands first; `shop_fulfil` makes one order). Emails (`src/lib/email/shop.ts`: order received, new-order alert to `contact.alert_email`, can't-ship) are claimed via `confirmation_sent_at`; the PaymentIntent gets `receipt_email` for Stripe's receipt. `checkout.session.expired`/`async_payment_failed` release holds, `charge.refunded` marks a fully refunded shop order `refunded` (restock is manual), `/api/cron/shop-holds` tidies expired holds every 10 minutes. Shipping address via `shippingOf()` (`collected_information.shipping_details` on dahlia). **Next's request memoization:** identical GETs inside one page render return the first answer, so a read, a write and the same read again sees stale data; `summaryFor` passes a fresh `abortSignal` per read (Next's opt-out). Watch for this anywhere a server page reads after writing.
- **Order handling (2026-10-09, `supabase/shop-m2.sql`, on dev + prod):** adds `shop_order_events` (every order's timeline; `visible` rows show the buyer), `shop_lookup_codes`, `shop_product_changes`, `shop_orders.phone/carrier/restocked_at`, `shop_products.archived_at`, `shop_restock()` (once per order; never for can't-ship orders, which took no stock). Checkout now asks for a phone (`phone_number_collection`, Stripe makes it required). **Staff** (Platform ▸ Shop orders, `PlatformShopOrdersTab`, `src/lib/store/manage.ts` + `catalog.ts`, `/api/admin/shop/orders[/id]` + `/api/admin/shop/products`): orders to ship oldest first, search, an order modal with the full address, Mark shipped (tracking `[A-Z0-9]{10,34}`, buyer emailed once via `shipped_email_sent_at`), fix tracking, refund through Stripe with optional restock, restock, staff notes, resend confirmation; products add/edit/archive/restore synced to Stripe (a price change moves `default_price` to the new Price before archiving the old: Stripe won't archive a default price). Live-mode catalog writes need the `shop_live_catalog` flag. **Buyers** see an order three ways (`canViewOrder` in `src/lib/store/lookup.ts`): the account that placed it or whose confirmed email it used (My Stuff ▸ Shop Orders, `/api/account/shop-orders`); a browser that unlocked it, via `/shop/orders` (order number + email or phone → 6-digit code, 10 minutes, 5 tries, same answer whether or not it matched; email codes HMAC-hashed, texts through Twilio Verify in production and logged elsewhere) or the "View your order" link in every order email (`/shop/orders/view?t=`, signed for 120 days); both set the signed `gs_shop_orders` cookie (`src/lib/store/access.ts`, 20 orders, 30 days); and staff. Anyone else is sent to the lookup form with the number filled in. The lookup and order pages work while the shop is closed. `/shop/orders/GS-1005` shows status, ship-by or tracking, items, totals, address and history (CDS `Timeline`). **Refunds, full and partial (`supabase/shop-m3.sql`, 2026-10-09):** the admin Refund form picks units of each item, the shipping charge or a set amount, a reason (damaged / missing / goodwill / other), an optional restock (off by default: damaged packs stay off the shelf) and a staff note. Amounts come from Stripe's own line totals (`refundQuote`, tax included), prorated by `refundCents` in `rules.ts` so unit-by-unit refunds add up exactly. `refundOrderPart` claims the refund in the database first (`shop_claim_refund`, row lock: no double refunds, never past the total; the full amount flips the order to refunded), then calls Stripe with an idempotency key, and undoes the claim (`shop_release_refund`) if Stripe refuses. `markShopRefund` (charge.refunded) brings the order up to Stripe's refunded total, so Dashboard refunds (partial or full) are recorded too; admin refunds already match and do nothing there. Each refund gets one timeline entry and one email (`notifyRefunded` → `sendShopRefunded`: full, or partial listing items/shipping and the reason). Order tracks `amount_refunded`, `shipping_refunded`; items `refunded_quantity`, `restocked_quantity` (`shop_restock_items`; `shop_restock` restocks what isn't back yet). Buyer's order page shows "Refunded −$X" and "Refunded $X" in History. **Digital lists** (Britton, 2026-10-09: checklists and deck lists are digital, never printed): `digital_lists.lists` in `shop-seed.json` maps each list to its SKUs; `digitalListsFor(skus)` returns only lists with a `url`, shown in the order email ("Your digital lists") and on the order page; collect-all-30 and thirty-set-artists point at the 30th Celebration checklist and set-builder-checklist at the Ascended Heroes one; deck-list stays null until the Ready-to-Play decks are defined (relative paths; the email makes them absolute). **Account path:** the order email ends with a My Stuff link for orders placed signed in, else "make a free GameShuffle account with this email" (`/signup?redirect=/account/stuff?tab=orders`); `ShopAccountPrompt` does the same on the order page (guest or owner, never staff) and the thanks page.
- **Stripe rules for the build:** shop sessions use `customer_email` + `client_reference_id`, never the Pro `stripe_customer_id` (the US-only backstop refunds by customer). The webhook gets a first `metadata.gs_kind === "shop"` branch; read payload fields through `src/lib/stripe/payload.ts`.

### GameShuffle TCG store (TCGplayer)
- We **link out**, don't rebuild commerce — TCGplayer owns checkout/inventory/shipping. The TCGplayer API is closed to new keys (post-eBay) and sellers can't affiliate-link their own store, so it's plain storefront deep-links.
- Shared `TCG_SHOP_URL` in `src/data/shop.ts`. Surfaced on the **homepage** ("Shop our Pokémon cards" `AppCard`, `external` + `ctaLabel`) and in the **TCG Companion** (entry landing + an in-app header ghost button). Opens in a new tab.

### Marketing pages (SEO/GEO)
- Public marketing surface lives at top-level slugs (`/apps`, `/tools`, `/features`, `/gs-pro`, the per-app keyword pages). Per-app pages are driven by `src/data/marketing-apps.ts` through `src/components/marketing/AppMarketingPage.tsx`.
- Shared components in `src/components/marketing/`: `FeatureCard`, `DarkBand`, `GamesShowcase` (`src/data/marketing-games.ts`), `AutoplayCarousel` (autoplay-until-interaction), `ProPitchBand`, `MarketingJsonLd` (SoftwareApplication / Breadcrumb / FAQPage JSON-LD).
- **Homepage order (reorg 2026-10-16, `src/app/page.tsx`):** hero → Today on GameShuffle (`HomeToday`: the dailies, the Weekly, today's top 3) → randomizers → Run the night (`HomeHost`) → GS Pro and GS Circuit (`PlansBand`, Circuit's price follows its launch) → free tools → Pokémon → shop → Support (`SupportBand`, hidden for supporters and GS Pro) → Idea Board. Viewer bits in `src/lib/home/viewer.ts`.
- **Homepage hero** = `BrowseHero` (blue, `aside` slot) + `HeroShowcase` (`src/components/home/HeroShowcase.tsx`, styles at the end of `marketing.css`): seven live demo cards (MK8DX `KartSlot` reel, Smash portraits, Jamboree board, a Daily row, `WheelGraphic`, a bracket, a cross-platform poll), shuffled per visit, each held its own time plus up to 0.9s, dealt in/out like cards. Pauses on hover/focus over the card, hidden tab or offscreen; dots hand control to the visitor; reduced motion shows one card at rest. Board art is drawn on a canvas so it can't become the page's LCP. Add a showcase = a demo component + a `SHOWCASES` entry (keep it inside the 46rem card). Clicks send `Hero Showcase Clicked` {showcase}. The old homepage video files in `public/video/` are no longer used by `/`.
- **Page refinement pass (2026-10-19, picks https://claude.ai/artifact/QaSjiFxbYHkNBAQq53xvpq, every page "build"):** consolidate with these shared pieces instead of more cards. `FaqList` (`src/components/marketing/FaqList.tsx`): questions only, tap to open, answers stay in the server HTML for search and FAQPage JSON-LD (native disclosure; CDS Accordion only mounts opened items, so FAQs used to be forced open; swap back once CDS has keep-mounted). `ShowAll` (`src/components/ui/ShowAll.tsx`): a long text/picture list clipped to a height with "Show all N" (Mario Party minigames, roster, Bonus Stars; Circuit's comparison table); not for lists of links. `RowPanel` (`src/components/marketing/RowPanel.tsx`): one tinted surface, a row per thing (Tabler glyph on a gradient tile, name, line, text link `RowLink`), three across from 900px (`row-panel--surface` when it stands alone): the homepage's Run the night and For Pokémon players. `PlansPanel` (rows on RowPanel): free account / GS Pro / GS Circuit worded per page, Circuit says "free during preview" until launch; used by `RandomizerNudge` ("Take {game} further", now rendered **between the randomizer and its `RandomizerLanding`** on all 17 randomizer pages, `tournament={false}` on Fire Red/Leaf Green) and before the FAQ on /mario-kart-tournaments; its Circuit button sends `Circuit Clicked` {from} (`EVENTS.circuitClicked`, needs a Plausible goal). Swipe rows on phones = `ResponsiveCarousel` (spotlights on GS Pro/Circuit/MK tournaments, landing features, Mario Party boards, the game nights tournament rail); a carousel named like its section turns into a plain group (`useCarouselA11y`), so no duplicate landmark. **Curated carousels end with a "see all" card:** More randomizers = up to 3 from the same series, then the first of each other series, capped at 5, then "Check out all N randomizers" (`MoreRandomizers allCount`). **Cards in a row line up:** `AppCard` fills its cell and pins its buttons to the bottom (`.app-card`, `.app-card__actions`). Homepage also gained "Popular on GameShuffle" (`POPULAR_PAGES` in `src/app/page.tsx`, plain links to the search pages, before the support band); the Pokémon hub folds reviews into one quote under Recently sold, keeps the companion grid 2 across on phones, and prints card credits once at the foot (`FeaturedShopCards credits={false}`). /game-nights uses three `EventsBrowser` options: `groupCap: 3` (each time group shows 3, then "Show N more this week"; a group one over the cap shows whole), `createInHead: false` (the hero already says Host a night) and `compactOnPhones` (a square of art beside the text, `.bgn-grid--compact`). Icons are Tabler, never emoji.
- **Refinement round 2 (built 2026-10-20, picks on the round 2 picks page, all as recommended; review with before and after https://claude.ai/artifact/MXXNji2BNNMo3oP2zE1hZv):** `AppMarketingPage` takes `{ content, look?, plans, afterPlans? }` and runs hero, `look` (`.app-look`: the thing working, e.g. `LoungeStandingsArt`, the bracket `WelcomeDemo`, the companion's `CounterTool`), features, how it works, `PlansPanel id="plans"`, `afterPlans` (one line, e.g. the companion's shop and deck guides), About (`aboutHeading`, the overview kept for search), FAQ (left-aligned, `.app-faq`); the DarkBand, cross-sell and end CTA band are gone (`AppCrossSell`, `crossSell`, `ctaBackground` removed from `marketing-apps.ts`). /tournament browse: `groupCap: 5, compactOnPhones: true`, and an event without a cover shows the game's box art (`EventCard`, `.bgn-card__hero-box`). The page's own "Show more" stays hidden while the last group is still cut to its cap (new rows land at the end of that group, so they'd load unseen; fixed 2026-10-20), pressing it opens that group, and either button moves focus to the first new card (`hiddenIn`, `showMore` in EventsBrowser). /trivia signed out: Fire Round and hosting as one `RowPanel` (`trivia-ways`, sr-only h2), occasions and tools as RowPanel rows, a PlansPanel (Circuit row = weekly leagues). /chat-brain: answering first, five questions then "More questions (N)". /discord: one comparison `Table` (Free vs GS Pro, sr-only "Not included") with the two buttons under it, in `TableScroll`. /for-streamers: two paths in one RowPanel, four overlay pictures from the chat games guide, the toolkit as rows, one closing band.
- Standard `.marketing-eyebrow` for eyebrows; Live (green) / New (blue) status badges. `/pricing` was removed (301 → `/gs-pro` in `next.config.ts`). Nav: Apps · Tools · Features · GS Pro · Contact.

### Compliance / Privacy
- Cookie consent banner: `src/components/layout/CookieConsent.tsx` + `src/lib/consent.ts`
- **GPC honored** — Global Privacy Control bit detected via `navigator.globalPrivacyControl`; treated as opt-out for the analytics + marketing categories
- Granular per-category consent (analytics, marketing — both default off)
- Revoke flow: footer "Cookie Preferences" → `#cookie-preferences` hash → CookieConsent watches hash + `open-cookie-preferences` event, opens preferences modal
- DSAR: `/data-request` (custom form, Turnstile-gated, email-verified two-step) + `/api/dsar/submit`
- Policy update workflow: `src/components/layout/PolicyUpdateBanner.tsx` (site banner) + `src/lib/email/policy-update.ts` (MailerSend blast template)
- Account deletion at `/api/account/delete` cascades through Stripe (cancel subs) + `disconnectTwitchIntegration` (revoke tokens, delete EventSub subs, remove channel point rewards)
- Sentry: instrumentation in `src/instrumentation.ts` + `src/instrumentation-client.ts` + `src/app/global-error.tsx`

### Email
- **Supabase auth emails** (confirmation, magic link, password reset) — via MailerSend SMTP configured in Supabase Dashboard > Project Settings > Auth > SMTP Settings
- **Billing emails** — `src/lib/email/billing.ts` (trial reminders, payment receipts, cancellation confirmations)
- **Account emails** — `src/lib/email/account.ts` (deletion confirmations, etc.)
- **Marketing subscriptions** — `src/lib/email/subscriptions.ts`, `email_subscriptions` table, `/api/email/subscriptions/opt-in` route, `/unsubscribe` page
- **Policy update blasts** — `src/lib/email/policy-update.ts` + `scripts/send-policy-update-blast.ts` for the 30-day notice workflow
- Sender: `noreply@gameshuffle.co`; transactional sender separate from marketing
- Aliases (Google Workspace, all pointing to `britton@gameshuffle.co`): `support@`, `privacy@`, `legal@`, `billing@`, `security@` — referenced consistently across legal pages + contact form

### SEO
- Root layout sets `metadataBase`, title template (`%s | GameShuffle`), and default OG
- Static pages use `export const metadata` in page or layout files
- Client components use layout-level metadata (can't export metadata from `"use client"` files)
- Dynamic pages use `generateMetadata()`: `/tournament/[id]` (layout), `/tournament/championship/[id]`, `/game-nights/[id]`, `/championship/join/[token]`, `/live/[streamer-slug]`, `/u/[username]`, `/s/[token]`. A page's `openGraph` replaces the root one whole, so event pages build theirs with `shareMeta()` (`src/lib/og/meta.ts`: siteName, locale, type, url, canonical, twitter card)
- Dynamic sitemap at `src/app/sitemap.ts`. `lastmod` comes from `src/data/sitemap-lastmod.json` (git dates per route; refresh with `npm run sitemap:lastmod` before a release). Left out on purpose: `/beta`, `/features`, `/u/*`, `/quotes/*` and tournament instances. `/u/*` and `/quotes/*` are `noindex, follow`; finished tournaments are `noindex`
- `robots.txt` disallows private routes (`/account`, `/stream`, `/api/`, auth pages)
- OG images: `/images/opengraph/gameshuffle-main-og.jpg` (site-wide), per randomizer `/images/opengraph/<slug>-og.jpg` (`scripts/render-randomizer-og.tsx`, Mario Kart 8 Deluxe and World included since 2026-10-18), the dailies (`scripts/render-originals-og.tsx`), and every static page's `opengraph-image.jpg` plus the tier list / bingo / truth or dare per-slug images (`scripts/render-page-og.cjs`, rebuilt 2026-10-18 from the never-committed Sep 28 script; help and guide titles come from the dev server's h1; a dynamic route like `/ideas/[id]` must pass an out path under `public/images/opengraph/pages/`, which its `opengraph-image.tsx` reads: the script now refuses otherwise, since a static image file in a dynamic segment fails the production build). Every share image uses the official logo file, never the name typed out, and fails the build script if a word repeats across logo, title and subtitle.
- **Event share cards (2026-10-07):** every tournament, game night, championship season, season invite and `/live` page has its own 1200x630 preview from an `opengraph-image.tsx` + `twitter-image.tsx` in its folder (not `/api/og`: robots.txt blocks `/api/` and Twitterbot obeys it). Renderer `src/lib/og/eventCard.tsx` (navy card, accent glow, glyph field, title in Gabarito, date in the organizer's timezone via `cardDate`, up to three chips; art = the organizer's banner, else the game's box art as a tilted card, else three glyph tiles; Cancelled stamp). The renderer can't draw webp, so pictures go through `sharp` (a direct dependency) to PNG; banners are only fetched from our own hosts. What each card says lives in `src/lib/og/eventCards.ts` (service client, so the privacy rules are there: a draft gets the generic card, an unlisted night never shows its place). Fonts: `src/app/fonts/{gabarito-bold,outfit-medium,outfit-semibold}.ttf` (the renderer can't read woff2). Glyph paths are inlined in `src/lib/og/glyphs.ts`.
- **Tournament and season pages render their first paint on the server** (`src/app/tournament/[id]/page.tsx` → `TournamentPageClient`, `tournament/championship/[id]/page.tsx` → `ChampionshipPublicClient`, each with an `initial` prop), so the H1, standings and Event JSON-LD are in the HTML. Same reads as the browser with the viewer's session (drafts stay private); the tournament's lobby details (room code, friend codes, community link) are blanked in the server copy and filled in by the browser's read after mount. Anything these components render must match on server and browser: no `window` in render (the page URL comes from a `baseUrl` prop), dates formatted with a fixed locale and zone, and the site's `useToast` rather than a local CDS ToastContainer (it only mounts in the browser).
- **Event privacy in previews:** unknown tournament/season ids are real 404s (the layout/page checks with the service client); drafts preview as a plain "Tournament" with noindex; unlisted, cancelled, archived and past game nights are noindex, and unlisted nights get no Event JSON-LD (it would publish the address and coordinates) and a link-only share menu (`EventShell shareScope="link"`: Copy link and Copy for Discord, no X/Facebook).
- **Share menu (`EventShell`):** every shared link carries `?src=share-link|share-discord|share-x|share-facebook|share-native`, which the site-wide lead capture already records; `Event Shared` {type, via} in Plausible. "Copy for Discord" copies the title, a `<t:unix:F>` timestamp (Discord shows it in each reader's timezone) and the link. The feed's game-night Discord announcement uses the same timestamp instead of server time.

### Deployment (Vercel)
- Framework preset: Next.js
- Install command: `bash scripts/vercel-install.sh` (injects `GITHUB_TOKEN` for private CDS dependency)
- Env vars: `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY`, `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY`, `GITHUB_TOKEN`
- Turnstile uses explicit render mode (`?render=explicit`) to prevent double widget initialization

## Key Conventions
- **Time zones (decided 2026-10-07): GameShuffle runs on Pacific time, never UTC.** Every site-wide day, week and month (the Daily, the Weekly, AI free tries, seasons, token allowance months, SMS allowance months, bust recovery, release dates, Chat Brain daily post, admin charts) turns over at midnight Pacific (`America/Los_Angeles`, PDT/PST). Use `src/lib/time/gsClock.ts` (`gsDay`, `gsDayStart`, `gsWeekStart`, `gsMonthStart`, `gsHour`, `gsAddDays`); never `toISOString().slice(0, 10)` or `getUTC*` for a calendar boundary. Database functions use `date_trunc('day', now() AT TIME ZONE 'America/Los_Angeles') AT TIME ZONE 'America/Los_Angeles'` for a day start (`supabase/gs-pacific-time-m1.sql`). Vercel crons run in UTC, so a "midnight Pacific" job is scheduled at both offsets and guarded or idempotent. **Exceptions, in the user's own timezone:** tournaments, game nights, events (and their ticket analytics: `organizerAnalytics(…, tz)` from the browser), stream schedules and QOTD (streamer's timezone, Pacific when unset). `zonedDay` / `zonedDayStart` / `zonedParts` / `zonedInstant` / `safeTimeZone` handle any zone. **Recurring things step on the local calendar:** game night series (`nextOccurrence(…, tz)`, the series' timezone, else the host's) and recurring stream sessions (`computeNextScheduledAt(…, tz)`, the streamer's profile timezone) move the local date and keep the local start time, so a 7pm night stays 7pm through daylight saving, and "2nd Friday" is the host's Friday. Never step a schedule by fixed 24h/7d in UTC.
- Dev mode controls (`process.env.NODE_ENV === "development"`) for testing multi-player flows
- SEO redirects from old URLs in `next.config.ts`
- Game name lookup via `src/data/game-registry.ts`
- Config types defined in `src/data/config-types.ts`
- Gamertag platforms defined in `src/data/gamertag-types.ts`
- Auth utilities in `src/lib/auth-utils.ts` (`isEmailVerified()`)
- **Halloween week glyphs:** from Oct 24 to Oct 31 (Pacific) every `IconField` (site headers, marketing heroes, video stages, Activity band) swaps to the `spooky` glyph set (ghost, pumpkin, bat, candy, spider, moon); minigame and mystery fields keep theirs. Read after hydration (`useSyncExternalStore`, server snapshot null), so pages built before the week still swap. `?season=spooky` previews it, `?season=none` turns it off.
- **Shared patterns (design round 3, 2026-10-20, picks https://claude.ai/artifact/FkXQ839JCDxGuhUBqnM1XZ).** *Arrow links:* every "go somewhere" text link is `ArrowLink` (`src/components/ui/ArrowLink.tsx`), never a typed "→": inline, a Tabler arrow that slides 4px on hover and focus while the label underlines (reduced motion keeps the arrow still), the brand ink kept on hover (global `a:hover` is `a:where(:not(.arrow-link)):hover`); `external` opens a new tab, `plain` renders an `<a>` (API routes, hashes: never Next `Link` to an API route, it would prefetch). A whole-card link adds `ARROW_HOST_CLASS` and an `ArrowCue` inside. No arrows typed into Button labels; client components may use `iconAfter={IconArrowRight}`. *Icon tiles:* one tile, `IconTile` (`src/components/ui/IconTile.tsx`; `.icon-tile` and the `--tile-*` tokens in globals.css): seven tones (blue, violet, magenta, teal, green, orange, red) plus shuffle, dex, fire and weekly; 40px in cards, rows and lists, 56px only beside a page heading or band title; cards put it above the title, rows and lists beside the label (RowPanel too, at every width). CSS-only tiles use the tokens, never new gradients (the support levels keep their own three colors on the token size). *Lists:* classless `ul`/`ol` inside `.deck-prose`, `.guide__body`, `.help-article__body` and `.faq-list__a` get brand squares or numbered tiles; `ul.list-check` adds green checks; classed lists and legal pages keep their own look. *Plug band:* a plug for one of our tools inside an article is `PlugBand` (`src/components/tcg/rules/PlugBand.tsx`: navy band, glyph field, 56px tile, "From GameShuffle"), used by the rules pages, the rules hub and the trivia articles and question pages. *Eyebrows (Britton, 2026-10-20):* one short label of 1 to 3 words, never two things joined with " · ", and only where it adds something the heading doesn't; homepage section eyebrows take the section's color and never repeat the heading's words. Just-launched randomizers say "New randomizer". Status lines on live and TV screens ("Round 2 of 5 · Rank it") are live data, not eyebrows.
- **Containers and gaps (Britton's rule, 2026-10-19, picks https://claude.ai/artifact/SGrYdQmo7MKyFHoL7mYefK; applies site-wide, desktop and phone, to every page as it's touched).** Be intentional with containers: a box must hold things that belong together and suit what's in it. (1) **One surface per moment:** a section gets at most one card or tinted panel; inside it, separate things with space and thin lines, never another card (no box in a box). (2) **Data keeps its frame:** tables and grids keep their border. (3) **Prompts sit flat:** sign-up forms, extra questions and How it works sit on the page under a thin line (CDS Accordion `variant="flush"`, not a bordered box). (4) **The main action may be boxed** when a section is mostly one thing to press (the Daily's Start card). **Gaps:** 4px only inside one unit (icon + label, number + caption); 8px inside a tight pair (a field and its button, chips); 12px between items in a group (a button row, a label block and its controls); 16-20px between groups in a section; 24px+ between sections, with a thin line when they sit flat (the dailies: `.daily > section` gets a top line, 24px padding, 8px extra margin). Don't leave 4-8px where things need separating. First applied on the Daily Shuffle and Daily Dex (the result is the one panel; streak strip, Card of the day, join form, Chat Brain (`ChatBrainAsk flat`) and How it works sit flat).
- **A page that opens on `BrowseHero` must be in `HERO_ROUTES` (or `HERO_DETAIL`) in `SiteNavbar.tsx`**, or the nav renders solid with a 64px spacer above the header instead of floating over it (/daily-dex, /discord and /support were missing until 2026-10-19).
- **Footer on phones (2026-10-19):** below 640px the footer's link columns and description are hidden and a CDS Accordion copy of the same sections (`FOLDED_SECTIONS` in `SiteFooter.tsx`, passed through CDS `MarketingFooter`'s only spare slot, `trustBadges`) folds them into tap-to-open rows (1,343px → about 680px). CDS can't fold its footer; it's on the CascadeDS fix list. New footer links go in `SECTIONS` once and show in both.
- **Sticky UI follows the nav:** the site nav hides on scroll down and returns on scroll up; `SiteNavbar` sets `--gs-nav-offset` (64px shown, 0 hidden). Every sticky bar, sidebar and section picker uses `top: calc(var(--gs-nav-offset) + gap)` with `transition: var(--gs-nav-slide)`, so it slides with the nav instead of riding under it. Full-screen custom overlays (drawers, dialogs) sit above the nav (z-index 1050-1101; the nav is 1000; CDS overlays are 9990+).
- **Writing (Britton, 2026-10-20: repeated lines and machine-sounding copy).** Say each fact once per page: the header says what the thing is, a game's Start card steps are the only summary of its rules, How it works and the FAQ add only details the steps don't cover, and the line under a leaderboard covers scoring. Plain labels for headings, concrete facts in body text. Avoid: "not just" / "X, not Y" contrasts, "a real", "whether you're", rhetorical openers ("Done for today?", "Want more?", "Ready to…?"), slogan filler ("stack up", "everything you need", "built for"), "journey", "finally", "simply", "the whole couch", dashes. Checks: `npx tsx scripts/copy-check.ts [path] [--summary]` (phrases in string literals and JSX text; a pointer, not a judge) and `node scripts/repeat-check.cjs <path…>` (opens the page with folded answers open and lists word runs that appear in two places; reworded repeats still need a read). The dailies' rewrite (picks https://claude.ai/artifact/J58R3AfjrFppAEtZqK6uhK, all recommended) is built: the homepage Today cards, the Shuffle, Dex, Fire Round (its separate How it works section is gone; the FAQ is "About the daily trivia quiz") and Weekly pages. GS Pro, GS Circuit and Support followed (picks https://claude.ai/artifact/RctfnEhoVEAZUpm8AjYP9Q, all recommended): GS Pro's price card tag is "For streamers" (most accounts are Free, so not "Most popular"), the first spotlight names only Twitch and Discord (no unbuilt platforms), the Circuit cards leave out Ticket sales analytics until paid entry is on (`CircuitPricing paidEntry`), Circuit Events is offered in its banner and no longer under Coming later, and Circuit's three FAQ questions feed its search data too (`faqFor`). **Randomizer landings** (picks https://claude.ai/artifact/KvbY3uz6EfAvw7GjgaJPPV): the intro card line says what to do ("Add a player for each racer, then randomize…"; Mario Kart 64's says nobody gets the same character), the overview says what people use it for (search names like "MK8DX randomizer" stay inside a sentence), feature cards are only things about that game (fewer than six is fine; no "Save and share" or "Works on any screen": the plans panel covers saving), the FAQ drops what the page already answers and "Is it free?" covers phones, and the cards under the FAQ have plain titles. Done: the three Mario Kart and five Mario Party pages; next the other randomizers, then the tools.
- **Text size (Britton, 2026-10-20: consolidating must never shrink text).** Never scale anything that holds text (no CSS `zoom` or `transform: scale` on it). Marketing body text is 16px; reading text never goes under 14px; 12px only for labels (uppercase eyebrows, tags, counts, one- or two-word statuses); inputs 16px on touch screens; tap targets 24px at least (44px where there's room). A compact version drops elements, it doesn't shrink them. Check with `node scripts/text-size-check.cjs <path> [path...]` (phone + desktop, same flags as a11y-check; exits 1 on text under the floor or buttons under 24px). CascadeDS shrinks Table text to 12px (headers 10px) and Badges to 10px below 480px; globals.css keeps the desktop sizes on phones, and a table too wide for a phone goes in `TableScroll` (`src/components/ui/TableScroll.tsx`: a focusable region that scrolls sideways, since CDS's own table container can't take focus). Both are on the CDS fix list.
- **Headless Chrome scripts delete their profile when they end** (`fs.rmSync` after `chrome.kill`): on 2026-10-20 485 leftover profiles (15GB) filled the disk, and 32 headless Chromes from killed scripts were still running. `a11y-check.cjs` and `text-size-check.cjs` clean up after themselves.
- **Accessibility (WCAG 2.1 AA):** check new and changed screens with `node scripts/a11y-check.cjs <path> [path...]` (axe at phone + desktop widths against the dev server; exits 1 on failures; `A11Y_COOKIES` signs in, `A11Y_STORAGE` seeds localStorage and `A11Y_CLICK="Resume||Attack"` presses buttons first, for screens behind a game in progress) and fix what it finds; it's a floor, so also check keyboard focus and contrast on gradients by eye. Known CDS gaps (raise upstream with CascadeDS; the running fix list with workarounds and suggested fixes is https://claude.ai/artifact/56PTSfgVQD1tiogrEPXJX9, add new ones there): `Combobox` sets aria-expanded on a plain input with no role or label (wrap it in `LabeledCombobox`, `src/components/ui/LabeledCombobox.tsx`); `Progress` names its bar only via `label`, which it also prints (add `className="admin-progress-quiet"` to hide the print where the number is written beside it); date/time `Input`s with a `floatingLabel` overlapped their own mm/dd/yyyy text (floated from the start in globals.css); `MarketingFooter` section titles are h4s that skip heading levels; `Tabs` colours the active tab with `--primary-600`, which stays dark in dark mode (overridden in globals.css with `--brand-ink`); `Menu` items fail aria-required-children/parent (the account sidebar, every account page) and its submenus always start closed (no defaultOpen); `Table`'s scroll container isn't keyboard-focusable and its sortable headers use unsupported ARIA; `Carousel` leaves hidden slides focusable; `DonutChart` slices are svg paths with role img and only a `name` attribute (axe svg-img-alt, seen on My Stuff ▸ Payouts); `EmptyState` always renders its title as an h3, with no heading-level prop (axe heading-order on a screen that has only an h1, e.g. an empty Deck lists). Also raise with CascadeDS (not a11y): the icon set has no cards glyph (the account sidebar uses Tabler's `IconCards` for My Stuff ▸ Decks), and there's no toggle grid for a 5×5 board (Stream Bingo's pattern editor and the bingo cards use plain `aria-pressed` buttons). A selected success/warning/error `Chip` is white on the 500 shade (2.3:1 for green); globals.css moves it to the 700 shades.
- **Missing spaces in JSX text (compiler bug, found 2026-10-09):** Next 16.2's SWC drops the leading space of a JSX text run that contains an HTML entity (`&apos;`, `&rsquo;`, `&amp;`…) and a line break, so `Go to <a>Setup</a> and you&apos;re set.` (wrapped) renders "Setupand you're set." Put `{" "}` after the element. `npx tsx scripts/fix-jsx-entity-spaces.ts` reports them (exits 1), `--write` fixes them; run it before a release (80 were fixed on 2026-10-09).
- **Lost styles:** `npx tsx scripts/find-unstyled-classes.ts [--since <ref>]` lists class names that had CSS at the ref (default `origin/main`), have none now and are still used, and exits 1 if any. Run it after big CSS edits and before a release: on 2026-10-11 an edit had silently dropped Switches, Shop orders, Notifications and the Discord server picker styles. `--all` lists every BEM class with no CSS (mostly harmless hooks). Node's `/bin/sh` here finds a stale Intel-only `git` in `/usr/local/bin`, so scripts that shell out should call `/usr/bin/git`.
- **Dev cache:** `.next/dev` (Turbopack) grows without limit (31GB on 2026-10-09, which filled the disk and crashed the dev server). If the disk gets tight: stop the dev server, `rm -rf .next/dev`, start it again.
- **Supabase returns at most 1,000 rows per request**, whatever `.limit()` says. A read that needs every row pages with `readAllPages` (`src/lib/supabase/pages.ts`; build the query with a stable order ending in an id, then `.range(from, to)`). Found 2026-10-19 when the game packs took the trivia pack past 1,000: Fire Round's pool, the question pages, the night builder's pack, Platform ▸ Trivia and the import's duplicate check (which would have inserted duplicates) all stopped at 1,000; those now page. Other `.limit(5000)` reads elsewhere (break, activity, channel cards) are bounded by day or run and were left alone.
- Toasts: `useToast()`. CDS runs its 5s auto-dismiss only on non-touch devices, so `ToastProvider` adds its own timer on touch screens (5s, 7s warning, 8s error, 10s with a button). Never mount a CDS `ToastContainer` of your own, and mount a CDS `Drawer` only after hydration (`useSyncExternalStore(noopSubscribe, () => true, () => false)`): both render differently on the server and broke hydration on /live, /tournament and /game-nights (fixed 2026-10-11).
- Confirmations use `useConfirm()` (`src/components/confirm/ConfirmProvider.tsx`, one CDS Modal mounted beside the toasts): `await confirm({ title, body?, confirmLabel })`, the button saying what happens ("Clear scores"). Never `window.confirm`/`alert`; errors go to `useToast()`
- Free tool pages (wheel, dice, coin, name picker, tier list, bingo, 8-ball, timer, truth or dare, yes/no, plus template pages) use `FreeToolShell` (`src/components/tools/FreeToolShell.tsx`): breadcrumb + the game night tools' compact header, reading left. Tools with settings put the toy and its main button on the left and the settings on the right in one panel (`ToolLayout` + `ToolSetting`, `src/components/tools/ToolLayout.tsx`, from 760px; phones stack); tier list and bingo keep their full-width boards.
- **Tool looks (built 2026-10-20, design round 3 items 9-14, picks https://claude.ai/artifact/FkXQ839JCDxGuhUBqnM1XZ).** One saved look per account for dice, coin, timer and wheel (`src/lib/tools/looks.ts` rules, `looksServer.ts`, `/api/account/tool-looks`, `?for=stream` for a Full mod; stored in `streamer_module_defaults` game_slug "*", module ids dice/coin/timer/wheel, no migration). `useToolLook(tool)` (`src/components/tools/looks/`): the account's look on any device, a visitor's in localStorage `gs-look-<tool>`, and a browser look moves into an account that has none. GS Pro choices (dice materials wooden/rust, the picture coin, Match my brand) are kept when saved but drawn only while GS Pro (`mergeForSave`/`freeLook`); `module-defaults` saves for dice, coin and timer go through `saveLook` too. Dice: `DiceTable` (green felt default, navy felt, wood; the dice-box canvas sits inside the rim so the physics walls stop at the felt), dice-box `smooth-pip` (`Ndpip`; dice-box throws without a `themeColor`, always pass one) plus GS Pro `wooden`/`rust` (MIT, Frank Ali; themes and their package.json in `public/assets/dice-box/themes/`; gemstone left out, its d6 reads poorly); pips follow the die color (`pipFor`, dice-box's light/dark rule). Coin: finish (Classic gold, Silver, Site blue, Your color) and a letter or two per side for everyone; GS Pro adds the profile picture on heads and brand colors. Shared drawings: `DiceCube`/`DiceRow`, `Coin` (`coinPaint`), `EightBall`, used on the free pages, the overlay, the Layout preview and the Tool looks drawer; overlay events carry the drawn look; "Show the table on stream" is off by default. The wheel offers "Your theme" when signed in (brand theme with GS Pro, else the personal color). The control room's dice count and timer length start from the saved look. CDS ColorPicker's inline custom-color row fails axe, so tools use `LookColorPicker` (on the CDS fix list). Known: a signed-in visitor briefly sees the default look before theirs loads.
- Just-launched features are marked **New** (renamed from "Beta" 2026-10-06): `NewBanner` component, `isNew` prop on `AppCard` / randomizer catalog entries / game configs / Party `hero`, and a single-label eyebrow such as "New randomizer" (never "Free randomizer · New", see Shared patterns). The Streamer Beta program (/beta) keeps its name; the ToS §3.2 still says "Beta Features"
- Legal pages: full Terms of Service and Privacy Policy with anchor-linked sections, content lives directly in `src/app/{privacy,terms,cookie-policy}/page.tsx` (NOT Termly embeds)
- `tsconfig.json` excludes `specs/`, `docs/` from type checking
- `scripts/` — operational scripts. Test scripts (`test-*.ts`) run via `npx tsx`; one-shot ops scripts (`authorize-twitch-bot`, `backfill-twitch-avatars`, `send-policy-update-blast`, etc.) live here too
- `legacy-static/` no longer exists — the original static HTML site has been fully removed (was a GDPR exposure due to hardcoded GA tracking)

## Spec Documents

The `specs/` directory holds the source-of-truth specs for major workstreams. Keep these in sync with reality:

- **`specs/gs-cc-backlog.md`** — Running P0/P1/P2 backlog with shipped items section
- **`specs/gs-pro-updates/gs-product-roadmap.md`** — Roadmap + operating principles + current state
- **`specs/gs-token-economy/`** — 7-spec set for the token economy + command suite + module registry + compliance (build order in `README.md`)
- **`specs/gs-pro-updates/`** — Major workstreams: live view, discord cross-platform, mod accounts, picks/bans evergreen drafts, track + item randomization, personalization + trust-&-safety, **polling** (`gs-polling-spec.md` — cross-platform live polls, shipped)
- **`specs/gs-refinements/`** — Refinement specs that touch existing surfaces (command taxonomy, sync/lifecycle/scheduling)
- **`specs/gs-marketing/`** — Marketing-side specs
- **`specs/gs-parking-lot.md`** — Deferred work (overlay info architecture, positioning system) — DO NOT act on without a focused spec session
